Fix : a new independent ticker `uptimeTick` posted on its own `uptimeHandler` at `UPTIME_TICK_MS` = 1 s, which only updates `tv_stat_uptime` from the locally-tracked `upStartElapsed` reference. It fires no ADB call, no `dumpsys`, no `TrafficStats` read — pure `SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()` subtraction + `formatUptime()`, ~µs per tick. Lifecycle : started in `onResume`, removed in `onPause`. The 5 s `statsTick` is unchanged and still drives Rx/Tx + clients + the DOWN→UP session lifecycle. **Bug 4 — connected clients not shown.** The previous `parseClients` relied exclusively on `dumpsys wifip2p` to enumerate peers, walking lines flagged as `"Client:"` / `"mClients:"` / `"WifiP2pDevice"`. On the BYD ROM this section is empty (or formatted differently) even when a device is actually connected to the TetherFi GO, so the user saw « 0 clients » with a phone clearly visible in TetherFi's own UI. Fix : the enumeration now combines **three independent data sources** in a single ADB roundtrip (`dumpsys wifip2p ; echo ===ARP=== ; cat /proc/net/arp ; echo ===NEIGH=== ; ip neigh show`) and takes the **union** of MACs discovered. **Source 1 — dumpsys wifip2p** (unchanged primary path, kept for the device-friendly-name harvest). **Source 2 — /proc/net/arp** : every entry whose `Device` column starts with `p2p-`, `p2p`, `ap`, contains `ap0`/`softap`, or ends in `-ap`, with `Flags != 0x0` (i.e. a complete entry, not an incomplete probe), is treated as authoritative — if a peer has an IP on our tether interface, it IS connected. **Source 3 — `ip neigh show`** : same logic for kernels that surface neighbours only via the `ip` tool and not via `/proc/net/arp` (some recent Android variants). Lines containing `FAILED` / `INCOMPLETE` are dropped. The three sources merge into a `LinkedHashMap<mac, HClient>` (insertion-ordered dedup), so the dumpsys-derived `deviceName` wins when available and the ARP/neigh fallbacks fill in the rest. Now every device with an IP on our hotspot is visible — even when the WifiP2pGroup dump section is empty. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (301→302, 1.2.43-beta→1.2.44-beta), new `NavRailHotspot.java` (~50 LoC), `HotspotActivity.java` (+`wireHotspotNavRail`, +`uptimeTick` + `uptimeHandler` + `UPTIME_TICK_MS`, rewritten `parseClients` + `refreshClients`), `DiagActivity.java` / `LogActivity.java` / `SysInfoActivity.java` / `SettingsActivity.java` (+1 line each calling the helper), `activity_diag.xml` / `activity_log.xml` / `activity_sysinfo.xml` / `activity_settings.xml` (+1 hotspot wrapper each), `activity_hotspot.xml` (root rewrapped + navrail prepended). No new dependency, no new permission, no new string, no manifest change. **Zero behavioural regression** on non-DL3 platforms : the new navrail entries stay `gone` (helper bails out when `Platform.isDiLink3()` is false), so DL4/DL5 users see the exact same navrail as before. **Behavioural contract.** On a DL3 with the « I use my own SIM » toggle on (default since v1.2.38), every navrail in the app shows the same 6 entries (Apps / Settings / Diag / Sysinfo / Log / Hotspot), with the active screen highlighted. Entering Hotspot now keeps the navrail visible for one-tap escape to any other screen. The session uptime visibly ticks every second. The clients card shows every device connected to the TetherFi hotspot (MAC + IP, friendly Wi-Fi Direct name when available). |
| **1.2.43-beta** | 301 | **Diagnostic — new « Voice (alpha) » tab : step 1/3 of the cross-DiLink voice-control roadmap (audio-chain validator, fully isolated, zero ML, zero touch to production code).** Roadmap : v1.2.43 = chaîne audio + VU-meter, v1.2.44 = wake word « Ok DashCast » via openWakeWord (ONNX Runtime Mobile, FOSS Apache-2.0), v1.2.45 = ASR multilangue via Vosk avec packs téléchargés à la demande. La séparation 3 paliers est volontaire : on prouve d'abord que le micro arrive jusqu'à l'app sur DL3/DL4/DL5 *avant* de gonfler l'APK avec les dépendances ML. **Stack choisi** (et pourquoi pas Porcupine) : Porcupine impose un AccessKey nominatif par utilisateur final + validation en ligne périodique + free tier limité à ~3 monthly-active-users + binaire propriétaire, donc inadapté à un APK distribué hors-ligne ; openWakeWord + Vosk + ONNX Runtime Mobile sont 100 % FOSS, sans clé, sans réseau, fonctionnent sur tous les DiLink ≥ 3 sans Google Play Services. **Architecture v1.2.43 (isolée).** Tout le code voice vit dans `com.byd.dashcast.voice.*`, aucun import vers le reste de l'app à part `AppLogger` (logging) et `R` (strings de la notification). Production code zero-touch : aucun fichier de prod n'est modifié sémantiquement — `DiagActivity` reçoit juste un nouvel onglet en append, `AndroidManifest` reçoit une nouvelle permission + un nouveau `<service>`, `activity_diag.xml` reçoit un TabItem + un `<include>`. Le service peut être tué à n'importe quel moment sans impact sur le reste. **New tab.** `activity_diag.xml` index 13 (appended after Export APK BYD), visible sur toutes les plateformes — la décision de support cible est « DL3/DL4/DL5 only » mais la discrimination se fait à l'exécution (feature detection mic) plutôt qu'au manifest, donc minSdk reste à 28. **Layout** `include_diag_voice.xml` (~190 LoC, NestedScrollView root) : header MaterialCardView (`ic_btn_speak_now` tinté primary + titre + pill d'état + sous-titre + ligne sample rate + bouton toggle tonal **Démarrer l'écoute** / **Arrêter** + chrono mm:ss), VU-meter MaterialCardView (deux `ProgressBar` horizontaux 0..32767 : RMS moyen tinté primary, pic instantané tinté tertiary, ligne stats monospace « RMS X • pic Y • clip Z • frames N »), hint final spelling out the no-data-recorded / 16 kHz mono 16-bit / wake word and ASR coming in v1.2.44 + v1.2.45 contract. **VoiceService** (`com.byd.dashcast.voice.VoiceService`, ~280 LoC) : `Service` foreground avec `foregroundServiceType="microphone"` (requis sur API 30+ pour lire AudioRecord en background, DL5 = API 32), notification persistante channel `dashcast_voice_poc` IMPORTANCE_LOW, ouvre `AudioRecord(MediaRecorder.AudioSource.VOICE_RECOGNITION, 16_000, CHANNEL_IN_MONO, ENCODING_PCM_16BIT, max(minBuf, FRAME_SAMPLES*2*4))`. Thread `voice-capture` priorité normale lit par frames de 800 samples (≈50 ms), calcule sumSq + peak + clip count en pure boucle Java (aucune lib native, aucune allocation par frame), publie via `LocalBroadcastManager` sur `ACTION_LEVEL` (extras `EXTRA_RMS`, `EXTRA_PEAK`, `EXTRA_CLIP`, `EXTRA_FRAMES`, `EXTRA_RUN_MS`) au plus toutes les 50 ms pour throttler l'UI. Transitions d'état publiées sur `ACTION_STATE` (`STATE_STARTED` / `STATE_STOPPED` / `STATE_ERROR` + `EXTRA_REASON`). Toute erreur AudioRecord (`getMinBufferSize` ≤0, ctor exception, state ≠ INITIALIZED, `startRecording` exception, `read` ≤0) → `broadcastError` + `stopSelf` propre. `stopCapture` symétrique : `mRunning=false` → join thread (timeout 500 ms) → `mRecord.stop()` → release → broadcast STOPPED. Singleton statique `sIsRunning` pour que l'UI reflète le bon état au moment du rebind (orientation change ou re-entrée dans l'onglet). **Permissions.** Nouvelle `RECORD_AUDIO` dans le manifest, demandée à *runtime* via `ActivityCompat.requestPermissions` au premier clic sur Démarrer (request code `RC_VOICE_RECORD_AUDIO = 0x7AC0`). Refus → toast localisé `diag_voice_perm_denied` + bouton repasse à « Démarrer ». Service déclaré `android:exported="false"` + `foregroundServiceType="microphone"`. Aucune autre permission ajoutée. **DiagActivity wiring.** Nouveau `TAB_VOICE = 13`, `TAB_COUNT` 13→14. Nouveaux fields : `panelVoice`, `tvVoiceStatePill`, `tvVoiceSampleRate`, `tvVoiceRunTime`, `tvVoiceStats`, `pbVoiceRms`, `pbVoicePeak`, `btnVoiceToggle`, `voiceReceiverRegistered`, `voicePanelBound`, `voiceReceiver` (BroadcastReceiver privé final qui dispatche `ACTION_LEVEL` et `ACTION_STATE`). Nouvelles méthodes : `bindVoicePanel`, `onVoicePanelEntered`, `registerVoiceReceiverIfNeeded`, `unregisterVoiceReceiver`, `onVoiceToggleClicked`, `startVoiceService`, `applyVoiceState`, statique `formatVoiceRunTime`, override `onRequestPermissionsResult`. `showPanelForTab` étendue avec `isVoice` + visibility gate + `onVoicePanelEntered()` à l'entrée. `onDestroy` étendu avec `unregisterVoiceReceiver()` pour ne pas leaker le receiver. `onCreate` étendu avec `bindVoicePanel()`. Aucune modification sémantique des autres branches. **i18n 100 % — 19 nouvelles clés × 12 locales = 228 entrées.** Nouvelles clés : `diag_tab_voice`, `diag_voice_header_title`, `_header_subtitle`, `_btn_start`, `_btn_stop`, `_state_idle`, `_state_running`, `_state_error_fmt`, `_perm_denied`, `_meter_label`, `_meter_rms`, `_meter_peak`, `_stats_fmt` (`RMS %1$d • pic %2$d • clip %3$d • frames %4$d`), `_stats_idle`, `_hint` (contrat no-data-recorded + roadmap v1.2.44 + v1.2.45), `_sample_rate_fmt`, `_notif_channel`, `_notif_title`, `_notif_text`. Français (default) + anglais shippés à la main avec la phraséologie UX correcte ; les 10 autres locales (`ar`, `be`, `de`, `es`, `it`, `kk`, `ru`, `tr`, `uk`, `uz`) injectées via le script idempotent `/tmp/i18n_v1_2_43.py` avec traductions hand-curated par locale (identificateurs techniques `DashCast` / `openWakeWord` / `Vosk` / `RMS` / `VOICE_RECOGNITION` / `kHz` / `mono` / `16-bit` / `Ok DashCast` / `PoC` / `ML` / `clip` / `frame` laissés tels quels, placeholders `%1$d` / `%2$d` / `%3$d` / `%4$d` / `%1$s` strictement préservés). Zéro régression lint `MissingTranslation`. **Nouvelle dépendance.** `androidx.localbroadcastmanager:localbroadcastmanager:1.0.0` ajoutée — broadcast in-process uniquement, aucune IPC système, aucune perm supplémentaire. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (300→301, 1.2.42-beta→1.2.43-beta, +1 dep), `AndroidManifest.xml` (+`RECORD_AUDIO`, +`<service>` voice), nouveau `voice/VoiceService.java`, nouveau `res/layout/include_diag_voice.xml`, `res/layout/activity_diag.xml` (+1 TabItem + 1 `<include>`), `DiagActivity.java` (+~150 LoC : tab constant, fields, receiver, bind/start/stop pipeline, permission handler, onDestroy cleanup), `res/values/strings.xml` + `res/values-en/strings.xml` (+19 clés chacun), 10 autres locales (+19 clés chacune via le script i18n). Zéro autre fichier modifié. **Zero behavioural regression.** Aucun chemin de prod (cluster, freedom, overdrive, mirror, sniffer, export apk…) n'est touché. Le service ne démarre jamais sans clic utilisateur explicite. Le receiver UI n'est enregistré qu'à l'entrée dans l'onglet Voice (lazy). Si le user n'ouvre jamais l'onglet, aucun bytecode voice n'est exécuté (à part le static init de la classe). APK +~40 KB seulement (un service + un layout + 19 strings × 12 locales). **Behavioural contract.** Toute plateforme — install v1.2.43 par-dessus v1.2.42, ouvrir Diagnostic → Voice (alpha), cliquer **Démarrer l'écoute** : popup système RECORD_AUDIO → Autoriser → notification persistante « DashCast — écoute micro (PoC) » apparaît → pill passe à « En écoute… » → bouton devient « Arrêter » → les deux ProgressBar bougent en temps réel selon le bruit ambiant → ligne stats égrène RMS/pic/clip/frames → chrono mm:ss avance. Cliquer **Arrêter** ou quitter l'activité → service stoppé, notification retirée, ProgressBar à zéro, pill à « Service arrêté ». Si AudioRecord échoue (mic indisponible sur la ROM), pill affiche « Erreur : <message> » et la cause exacte est dans `AppLogger` (tag `VoiceService`) — c'est exactement ce qu'on cherche à mesurer sur les 3 plateformes cibles avant d'investir dans openWakeWord. |
| **1.2.42-beta** | 300 | **Diagnostic — new « Export APK BYD » tab : list every BYD/XDJA/DiLink/cluster package and ship its APK to Telegram in one tap, with auto-cleanup.** Field need: when investigating an unknown BYD component (smarttravel, commander, freedom, overdrive, window_mgmt, containerservice, clusterdebug…) the only reliable way to RE its behaviour is to grab the actual `.apk` from the production ROM — which until now required a USB cable, `adb pull /data/app/.../base.apk`, a host machine, and someone to do the dance in person. The new tab does it from inside the car in two taps. **New tab.** `activity_diag.xml` index 12 (appended after Cluster DL5), default visible on every platform (the listing is informational and the export action is harmless). New layout `include_diag_export_apk.xml` (~125 LoC, NestedScrollView root): header MaterialCardView (`ic_share` icon tinted primary + title + subtitle + counter line « X APK détectés • Y MB sur disque » + tonal **Rafraîchir** button); a hint TextView spelling out the cache+share+auto-delete contract; programmatic list container `ll_export_apk_list`. **Row layout** `row_export_apk.xml` (~95 LoC) — visually mirrors `row_log.xml` (4 dp colored bar on the left, `bg_log_row_info` rounded background, monospace typography) so the user immediately recognises the « one line per item » convention from the Journal screen: package name (primary tint, bold), `v<versionName> (<versionCode>)`, file size (right-aligned), APK path on a second line with `ellipsize=middle` to keep `/data/app/~~hash~~/base.apk` readable, and a tonal **Exporter** button at the end. **Scanner.** `scanBydPackages()` runs `PackageManager.getInstalledPackages(0)` off the UI thread, filters by case-insensitive substring match on `com.byd.` / `com.xdja.` / `com.dilink.` / `cluster` / `automotive` / `fission` / `projection` / `dilink` (covers every known BYD-side package family on DL2/DL3/DL4/DL5), reads `applicationInfo.sourceDir` to compute the on-disk size, sorts alphabetically. Lazy: triggered only when the tab is first opened (`refreshExportApkListIfNeeded` from `showPanelForTab`) and rerun on every **Rafraîchir** click. **Export pipeline.** Click on a row's **Exporter** button → background thread → reads the APK from `sourceDir` (every APK in `/data/app/` is mode `0644` so the app uid can read it directly via `FileInputStream`, no ADB shell needed — equivalent of `adb pull` minus the USB cable) → copies it to `getExternalFilesDir(null)/exported_apks/<pkg>-v<vername>-<vercode>.apk` (versionName sanitised through `sanitiseVer` to strip any non-alphanumeric char that could break the filename) → posts back to UI → fires `Intent.ACTION_SEND` with MIME `application/vnd.android.package-archive` + `FileProvider`-wrapped `content://` URI (`com.byd.dashcast.fileprovider` is already declared for the existing log/report sharing) + `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION` → walks 5 known Telegram packages (`org.telegram.messenger`, `org.telegram.messenger.web`, `org.telegram.plus`, `nekox.messenger`, `org.thunderdog.challegram`), targets the first one installed via `setPackage(hit)` so the chooser is bypassed → falls back to the generic chooser if no Telegram variant is found or if the direct intent throws. **Auto-cleanup contract** (user requirement: « il faut qu'une fois l'APK envoyé, on garde pas le fichier stocké dans la mémoire de la voiture »). Three independent layers : (1) `wipeExportApkCacheDir()` runs eagerly when `bindExportApkPanel` is called during `onCreate` — wipes every orphaned APK from a previous session before the user even sees the tab. (2) Same wipe re-runs on every **Rafraîchir** click, so the cache never grows beyond what the user is actively exporting. (3) `scheduleExportApkDelete(file)` registers a `Handler.postDelayed(..., 5 minutes)` on the main looper after the share intent is fired, deleting the cached APK after 5 minutes — long enough for Telegram to finish uploading even on a slow connection, short enough that the file never lingers. The handler is `removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)` in `onDestroy` so a destroyed activity does not leak its scheduled deletes (also obeys the standard sanity-check rule for Handler.postDelayed lifecycle). Net result: even in the worst case (user exports an APK, Telegram is missing, chooser is dismissed without sharing), the file is wiped on the next open of the tab. **DiagActivity wiring.** New constants `TAB_EXPORT_APK = 12`, `TAB_COUNT = 13`. New fields: `panelExportApk`, `tvExportApkCounters`, `btnExportApkRefresh`, `llExportApkList`, `exportApkInitialised`, `exportApkCleanupHandler`, the static nested `ExportApkEntry` type, plus the constants `EXPORT_APK_SUBDIR` / `EXPORT_APK_AUTO_DELETE_MS` (= 5 min) / `EXPORT_APK_NEEDLES`. New methods : `bindExportApkPanel`, `refreshExportApkListIfNeeded`, `refreshExportApkList`, `scanBydPackages`, `onExportApkClicked`, `shareApkToTelegram`, `scheduleExportApkDelete`, `exportApkCacheDir`, `wipeExportApkCacheDir`, plus three private statics (`copyFile`, `sanitiseVer`, `formatBytes`). `showPanelForTab` extended to handle the new tab and call `refreshExportApkListIfNeeded` on first visit. `onDestroy` extended with `exportApkCleanupHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)`. **i18n 100% — 15 new keys × 12 locales = 180 entries.** New keys: `diag_tab_export_apk`, `diag_export_apk_header_title`, `_header_subtitle`, `_refresh`, `_counters_idle`, `_counters_scanning`, `_counters_fmt` (`%1$d APK • %2$s on disk`), `_hint` (explains the cache+share+auto-delete contract), `_btn_export`, `_btn_exporting`, `_version_fmt` (`v%1$s (%2$d)`), `_sent_fmt`, `_chooser_title`, `_err_fmt`, `_err_no_source`. French (default) + English shipped manually with the proper UX phrasing; the other 10 locales (`ar`, `be`, `de`, `es`, `it`, `kk`, `ru`, `tr`, `uk`, `uz`) injected via the idempotent script `/tmp/i18n_v1_2_42.py` with hand-curated translations per locale, respecting the established invariants (no machine pseudo-translation, technical identifiers `APK` / `BYD` / `XDJA` / `DiLink` / `cluster` / `Telegram` left untouched, format placeholders `%1$s` / `%2$d` strictly preserved). Zero `MissingTranslation` lint regression. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (299→300, 1.2.41-beta→1.2.42-beta), `DiagActivity.java` (+~225 LoC net : tab constant, fields, bindings, scan/render/export/cleanup pipeline, onDestroy handler cleanup), new `res/layout/include_diag_export_apk.xml`, new `res/layout/row_export_apk.xml`, `res/layout/activity_diag.xml` (+1 TabItem + 1 `<include>` between Cluster DL5 and Coming Soon), `res/values/strings.xml` + `res/values-en/strings.xml` (+15 keys each, scoped to the new panel — no rename of any existing key), 10 other locales (+15 keys each via the i18n script). No new permission, no new manifest entry (the existing `FileProvider` `com.byd.dashcast.fileprovider` covers `getExternalFilesDir` via the already-declared `<external-files-path name="logs" path="."/>` wildcard), no new dependency, no new drawable, no new style. **Zero behavioural regression** on any platform — the tab is purely additive, scanning is lazy, no shell command is ever dispatched from the new code path, no system mutation is performed, and the cleanup contract guarantees no APK is ever persisted on the car's storage. **Behavioural contract.** Any platform — install v1.2.43 over v1.2.42, open Diagnostic → Export APK BYD: the tab lists every BYD-side package installed on the ROM with its version + size + source path. Pressing **Exporter** on any row copies the APK to a temporary cache, opens Telegram (or the chooser if Telegram is not installed) pre-populated with the APK file ready to send, surfaces a « APK envoyé : <pkg> » toast, then transparently auto-deletes the cached copy 5 minutes later. Pressing **Rafraîchir** wipes the cache immediately and re-runs the scan. Closing and reopening the app, or just switching tab and coming back, always shows the same authoritative list with no stale exports. |
| **1.2.41-beta** | 299 | **DL5 Cluster diag — recon cleanup (R14–R17) + new Test Fission live battery (F01–F10) targeting Yandex Maps.** The v1.2.40-beta DL5 field report (`log/byd_report_20260525_113903.log`, 27 PASS / 2 WARN / 0 FAIL / 1 SKIPPED) confirmed three things needed for the next step: (a) `ChangeId(174042936; name=FORCE_RESIZE_APP; disabled; overridable)` is registered and overridable on the DL5 ROM, (b) Yandex Maps declares `resizeMode=RESIZE_MODE_UNRESIZEABLE` at the activity level so it is the canonical blocker we want to bypass, and (c) the global `force_resizable_activities` flag is still `0` (reverted in v1.2.35), so we must use the per-package compat override rather than re-flipping the system-wide knob. It also surfaced two recon bugs we fix here. **Bug — R14/R15/R16 wrong AOSP syntax.** v1.2.38-beta wrote them as `am compat list <package>` for read-back, but on the DL5 production ROM the first argument of `am compat list` is interpreted as a **change ID**, not a package name (every call returned `Unknown or invalid change: 'com.byd.dashcast'` / `'ru.yandex.yandexmaps'` / `'com.waze'`). The proper per-package override read-back is `dumpsys platform_compat`, not `am compat list`. **Fix.** R14 is rewritten as `am compat 2>&1 \| head -20` (clean usage dump on stderr-merged stdout, no stack trace, surfaces every supported verb on this ROM). R15 becomes `dumpsys platform_compat 2>&1 \| sed -n '/PackageOverrides:/,/^$/p' \| head -60` — lists every currently-set per-package compat override on the device, which is the data we actually want before flipping FORCE_RESIZE_APP on Yandex. R16 becomes `dumpsys platform_compat 2>&1 \| grep -E 'ru\\.yandex\\.yandexmaps\|com\\.waze\|com\\.byd\\.dashcast' \| head -20` — pre-flight check that our three test packages have no leftover override that could skew the next F-battery run. **Bug — R17 BusyBox grep BRE alternation.** v1.2.40-beta wrote `grep -B1 -A1 '174042936\\\|FORCE_RESIZE_APP'` (BRE alternation), but the BusyBox grep on the DL5 ROM does not honor the BRE `\\|` — R17 returned 0 lines on the testeur's device, even though the same pattern with `-E` (in R18) matched correctly. **Fix.** Switched to `grep -E -B1 -A1 '174042936\|FORCE_RESIZE_APP'` (ERE alternation, BusyBox-compatible). Catalog descriptions for R14–R17 were rewritten end-to-end with an inline note explaining the v1.2.40→v1.2.41 transition. **New feature — `Test Fission` live test battery (F01–F10, DL5 only).** Recon-only suites are not enough: the user explicitly asked for « une batterie de nouveaux tests pour essayer de faire en sorte d'avoir le redimensionnement et le move qui fonctionne sur le cluster en ciblant Yandex Maps comme client de test ». New dedicated tertiary-style **Test Fission** button in `include_diag_cluster_dl5.xml`, in a separate action row below the existing Run-recon / Copy / Telegram row, gated to `View.VISIBLE` only when `Platform.get().isAutoDetectedDiLink5()` (hidden on every other platform — DL2/DL3/DL4 never see the button). Confirm dialog before launch (the test is destructive: it kills Yandex Maps, mutates a compat override, then cleans up). The same row container `ll_dl5_cluster_test_list` and renderer (`bindDl5Row`) are reused — the row prep is refactored into a shared `prepareClusterDl5TestRowsFor(List<TestDef>)` that either takes the R-catalog or the F-catalog, with a `dl5ClusterShowingFission` flag steering the Copy / Send Telegram exporters toward the right renderer (`renderReport` for R-tier, `renderFissionReport` for F-tier). **F-battery (12 tests, ~30 s typical):** **F01** detect fission display via `DisplayManager.getDisplays()`, prefer the lowest non-zero id with a `fission\|xdja\|cluster` name match, fall back to any non-zero id. **HARD RULE: id=0 is explicitly excluded as a fission target — if no non-zero display is available, the whole F-tier SKIPs without executing any shell command.** **F02** verify Yandex Maps is installed (`PackageManager.getPackageInfo`). SKIPs F03..F12 cleanly if absent. **F03** `am compat enable 174042936 ru.yandex.yandexmaps 2>&1` — enables FORCE_RESIZE_APP on the target. **F04** `am force-stop ru.yandex.yandexmaps` — the compat override only takes effect on the next `Application.onCreate`, so the live process has to be killed first. **F05** `am start --display <fissionId> --windowingMode 5 -a android.intent.action.MAIN -c android.intent.category.LAUNCHER -p ru.yandex.yandexmaps 2>&1` — launches Yandex on the fission display in `freeform` windowing mode. Hard guard at this step: `fissionId <= 0` short-circuits to FAIL + sets `abortFromHere`. **F06** waits ~3 s, then `dumpsys activity activities` filtered to `ru\.yandex\.yandexmaps\|displayId=\|mWindowingMode=\|Task=Task\|mBounds=Rect`. Parses the first `#\\d+` token on any line containing the package name to extract the taskId, and asserts (a) `displayId=<fissionId>` and (b) `mWindowingMode=freeform` (or `windowingMode=5`) appear in the filtered dump. **F07** `cmd activity task resize <taskId> 100 80 1820 640 2>&1; echo __exit=$?` — applies a 100×80 inset rectangle inside the 1920×720 fission framebuffer. PASS = `__exit=0`. **F08** waits ~2 s, then dumpsys filtered to `#<taskId> \|mBounds=Rect\|mWindowingMode=`, asserts the new bounds are present. **F09 (NEW)** `am start --display 0 -a android.intent.action.MAIN -c android.intent.category.LAUNCHER -p ru.yandex.yandexmaps 2>&1` — moves the Yandex task back to the main display (id 0). User clarification: the « don't touch display 0 » rule means « no resize / no size-mutation command on display 0 », not « never address display 0 at all » — moving an app TO display 0 via the standard launcher intent is the exact same code path the system fires when the user taps the app icon on the head unit, so it's explicitly safe and validates the move pipeline end-to-end (fission → main). **F10 (NEW)** waits ~2 s, dumpsys filtered to `displayId=\|Task=Task\|ru\.yandex\.yandexmaps`, asserts `displayId=0` is present on a line that also mentions the package. **F11** cleanup `am force-stop ru.yandex.yandexmaps` — leaves the device empty of Yandex state, ready for a fresh test on a different fission display. **F12** cleanup `am compat reset 174042936 ru.yandex.yandexmaps 2>&1` — restores Yandex Maps to its declared resize behaviour, leaves zero state behind for the next test or for the user's normal Yandex usage. F11 + F12 are cleanup steps that run **unconditionally** (even if F03..F10 aborted) so the device is always left clean between runs. **Hard safety rules baked into the runner.** (1) DL5-only enforcement: `runFission` checks `Platform.get().isAutoDetectedDiLink5()` before any work — on non-DL5 platforms every F-result is marked SKIPPED with the message `DL5 only — current platform: <describeMode(ctx)>` and no shell command is dispatched. (2) Display 0 policy clarified by user: « pas de commande qui change la taille » sur display 0 (no resize / no `wm size` / no `cmd activity task resize` / no `am display set-overscan`) — but moving an app TO display 0 via the standard launcher intent (F09) is explicitly allowed since it's the exact same code path the system uses when the user taps the app icon. F01 still excludes display 0 from the **fission target** candidate list (we only fission-launch on id > 0), F05/F07/F08 still gate every per-display command behind `st.fissionDisplayId > 0`, and F09 uses the safe MAIN/LAUNCHER intent — no resize command is ever issued against display 0. (3) The compat override mutation kills only Yandex Maps (the test target), never DashCast itself — the v1.2.40 R17 self-kill class of bug cannot recur with this design because the change ID is paired with `ru.yandex.yandexmaps`, not with `com.byd.dashcast`. (4) F-tier results are exported via a dedicated `renderFissionReport` (separate header, separate target/compat-id summary) so the report is self-contained and disambiguous from the R-tier recon report. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (298→299, 1.2.40-beta→1.2.41-beta), `dilink5/Dl5ClusterReconRunner.java` (R14–R17 dispatcher + catalog rewrites; new `fissionCatalog()` + `emptyFissionResults()` + `runFission(Context, Listener)` + `runFissionOne(...)` + 10 F-step implementations + `FissionState` + `renderFissionReport(...)`, ~340 LoC net), `res/layout/include_diag_cluster_dl5.xml` (existing action row preserved, new vertical `ll_dl5_cluster_fission_row` block added below it containing the tertiary-style `btn_dl5_cluster_test_fission` + a hint TextView), `DiagActivity.java` (+`btnDl5ClusterTestFission` / `llDl5ClusterFissionRow` / `dl5ClusterShowingFission` fields, +`prepareClusterDl5TestRowsFor(List<TestDef>)` refactor, +`confirmAndRunClusterDl5FissionTests` + `runClusterDl5FissionTests`, copy/Telegram exporters now switch renderer based on `dl5ClusterShowingFission`), `values/strings.xml` + `values-en/strings.xml` (+6 new keys: `diag_dl5_cluster_test_fission`, `_hint`, `_confirm_title`, `_confirm_msg`, `_confirm_ok`, `_only_dl5`; full 12-locale i18n pass — the same 6 keys translated and shipped in `values-ar`, `values-be`, `values-de`, `values-es`, `values-it`, `values-kk`, `values-ru`, `values-tr`, `values-uk`, `values-uz` via the idempotent script `/tmp/i18n_v1_2_41.py`, total +60 entries — zero `MissingTranslation` lint regression). No new permission, no manifest change, no new dependency, no protocol bump. **Behavioural contract.** DL5 testeur — install v1.2.41-beta over v1.2.40, open Diagnostic → Cluster DL5: the existing **Run recon** / **Copy report** / **Send via Telegram** trio is unchanged, with a new violet **Test Fission** button + warning hint appearing on a second action row. Pressing **Run recon** still runs the 30-test R-catalog (now with R14–R17 properly returning data instead of error noise). Pressing **Test Fission** brings up a confirm dialog explaining the destructive nature; on confirmation the F-catalog replaces the row list, F01 detects the fission display, F02–F10 execute sequentially with live row updates, and at the end the testeur can press **Copy report** or **Send via Telegram** to ship the F-tier report back. DL2 / DL3 / DL4: the **Test Fission** row is `View.GONE`, every existing path is unchanged. **Zero behavioural regression** on any platform — the change is purely additive on the UI side and the runner's existing `runAll`/`renderReport` paths are untouched. |
| **1.2.40-beta** | 298 | **Critical recon bug fix: R17 was killing the DashCast process mid-suite.** DL5 tester running v1.2.39-beta recon-v2 reported: « à un moment l'écran flash et ça sort de DashCast pour revenir. Du coup le résultat est perdu et la personne ne peut pas exporter le log. » Root cause: R17 was implemented in v1.2.38-beta as `am compat reset 174042936 com.byd.dashcast 2>&1` — a deliberate idempotent probe against our own package, expected to be a no-op when no override was set. The flaw: **Android 11+ platform_compat service forces a full process restart of the target package on EVERY compat-override mutation (including a reset that resolves to a no-op)**. This is by design — compat changes are evaluated at `Application.onCreate`, so the platform has to recycle the process to ensure the new value takes effect on the next launch. Net effect on DL5: the recon suite called `am compat reset` against its own package, the platform killed DashCast's process, DiagActivity was recreated when the user re-opened the app, the in-memory `dl5ClusterLastResults` list was wiped, and the tester was left with no report to copy or send via Telegram. **Fix.** R17 is now a fully read-only probe: `dumpsys platform_compat 2>&1 \| grep -B1 -A1 '174042936\|FORCE_RESIZE_APP' \| head -30` — surfaces the same registered metadata of change ID 174042936 (registered? overridable? default state? min sdk?) without touching the override state of any package, hence zero process kill and zero side effect on any other app. The catalog description for R17 was rewritten to reflect the change and to document the v1.2.38→v1.2.40 regression history inline, so the report itself is self-documenting for future testers. **No other test in the catalog mutates state** — R13 (`am compat enable` no args), R14–R16 (`am compat list <pkg>` read-back), R18 (`dumpsys platform_compat \| grep`) are all read-only, and R01–R12 / R19–R30 are pure dumpsys / settings get / pm dump / getprop / service list / wm size queries. So R17 was the ONLY suite-killing test. After this fix, the suite runs to completion every time and the report can be copied / shared via Telegram normally. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (297→298, 1.2.39-beta→1.2.40-beta), `dilink5/Dl5ClusterReconRunner.java` (R17 dispatcher in `runOne` + R17 description in `catalog()`). No new permission, no string change, no layout change, no new dependency, no protocol bump. **Zero behavioural regression** on every other platform — the recon tab is DL5-only-targeted and the change is a pure command swap inside a single test case. |
| **1.2.39-beta** | 297 | **Two field-reported UX fixes: (a) Hotspot icon now visible by default on DiLink 3 navrail, (b) projection no longer auto-starts when launching the DashCast app.** **Fix A — Hotspot navrail icon missing on DL3.** A DL3 user reported the Hotspot icon in the left navigation rail never appeared on their device. Root cause: the `refreshNavHotspot()` gate added previously (in a forward-tagged comment dated "v1.2.42") read `getBoolean(PREF_USE_OWN_SIM, false)` — defaulting to `false` meant new DL3 users had to discover and tick the "I use my own SIM card" toggle in Settings before the icon appeared, which most users never did. Fix: default now flipped to `true`, so on DL3 the Hotspot navrail entry appears immediately on first launch (out-of-the-box discoverability of the TetherFi feature shipped in v1.2.36-beta). Users without a personal SIM can still hide the icon via the same Settings toggle (the checkbox and its support string are unchanged). The gate stays in place — only the default is flipped — so the runtime path is identical (DL3 + opted-in → VISIBLE + click handler; everything else → GONE). **Fix B — Auto-projection on app launch removed.** Since v1.2.77 (well before the current beta channel — the comment is a forward-tag and the behaviour landed long ago in fact), opening the DashCast app would unconditionally call `activateCluster()` in `onStart()` if no cluster service was running. User feedback: "I don't want projection to start automatically when I open the app. It's enough that projection auto-starts when I launch an app on the cluster." Rationale acknowledged: every cluster-bound use of DashCast goes through tapping an app in the launcher grid, and `onSendToDashboard()` already auto-activates the cluster (slow path) when `mClusterService == null` or `getDisplayId() <= 0`, then replays the tapped app via `mPendingAppAfterActivation` once the service is up (fast path). So the launch-time `activateCluster()` call was redundant for any actual workflow, and intrusive for users opening DashCast just to browse the app grid, tweak Settings, view SysInfo, or trigger an OTA update — none of which require the cluster surface to be powered on. Fix: the `else { activateCluster(); }` branch in `MainActivity.onStart()` is removed; the preceding `if (ClusterService.sIsRunning)` branch is preserved untouched so an already-running service still gets re-bound when the Activity returns to foreground. **Slow path** = `onSendToDashboard()` → `mClusterService == null` → `mPendingAppAfterActivation = app ; activateCluster()` then the service-connected callback replays the app once `displayId > 0`. **Fast path** = `onSendToDashboard()` → service already bound, `displayId > 0` → direct `moveTaskToDisplay()` (or `launchOnDashboard()` for cold app). Both paths are unchanged and explicitly documented in the inline comment that replaces the removed branch. **Behavioural contract.** Opening DashCast cold (no projection active) now lands on the app grid with the cluster status pill in "OFF" state and no shell calls fired — exactly like opening any standard launcher. Tapping any app in the grid still kicks the slow path automatically (`mPendingAppAfterActivation` is set, `activateCluster()` runs, and the tapped app is replayed once the cluster comes up — same flow as v1.2.83+). Opening DashCast while projection is already running (`ClusterService.sIsRunning == true`) still binds and re-attaches the listener — no regression on the "leave then come back" scenario. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (296→297, 1.2.38-beta→1.2.39-beta), `MainActivity.java` (`refreshNavHotspot` default flip from `false` to `true` + explanatory comment; `onStart()` else-branch removed + replaced with comment documenting the slow/fast paths preserved in `onSendToDashboard`). No new permission, no string change, no layout change, no new dependency, no protocol bump. **Zero behavioural regression on DL2/DL4/DL5** — `refreshNavHotspot` is still platform-gated on `isDiLink3` (the icon stays GONE on every non-DL3 platform regardless of the pref value), and the `onStart` change applies uniformly to every platform (the v1.2.77 auto-activation behaviour was global, so its removal is global too — but it was never desirable on any platform per the user's clarification). |
| **1.2.38-beta** | 296 | **Two field-reported bug fixes: (a) UpdateChecker infinite-prompt loop on v1.2.37-beta installs, (b) recon-v2 — 8 bug fixes in the DL5 Cluster Recon tab, including hidden display id=2 detection.** **Bug A — UpdateChecker loop.** Multiple v1.2.37-beta users reported the OTA banner re-proposing v1.2.37-beta install on every launch, even after the install completed. Root cause traced in `UpdateChecker.isNewer(String latest, String currentName, int currentCode)`: the helper strips the `-beta` / `-buildN` suffix from `latest` via `stripSuffix(...)` before calling `parseVer(...)`, but does NOT apply the same treatment to `currentName`. So `parseVer("1.2.37-beta")` calls `String.split("\\.")` → `["1","2","37-beta"]`, then `Integer.parseInt("37-beta")` throws `NumberFormatException` which is caught and silently leaves that segment at 0. Result: `current` parses as `[1,2,0]` while latest parses as `[1,2,37]`, the version-segment comparison loop returns `lv > cv = 37 > 0 = true` at index 2 → `isNewer` returns `true` → OTA prompt shown → user installs → install completes → next launch → same loop. Fix is a one-liner: apply `stripSuffix` to `currentName` before parseVer, mirroring exactly what's done for `latest`. Also tightened the post-loop equality path: when both base versions are identical and no `-buildN` suffix is present (`latestBuild < 0`), the helper now explicitly returns `false` (`latestBuild > 0 && latestBuild > currentCode`) instead of falling through. So v1.2.37-beta installed users will, on first launch with v1.2.38-beta, see one final correct update prompt, install v1.2.38-beta, and then never be re-prompted for the same version again. v1.2.36-beta and earlier users are unaffected. **Bug B — Recon-v2.** Field log `log/byd_report_20260525_105105.log` from the DL5 tester (Yandex Maps on cluster) running the v1.2.37-beta recon-v1 tab revealed 8 distinct bugs in `Dl5ClusterReconRunner.java`, all fixed in this release. (1) **R01 NPE.** `probePlatform` called `Platform.get().describeMode(null)` — the `null` argument crashes on `ctx.getApplicationContext()` inside the `describeMode` implementation. Fixed: `probePlatform(Context ctx, TestResult r)` now takes a Context and passes it through. (2) **R02 last-match bug.** `probeDisplays` walked `DisplayManager.getDisplays()` and overwrote `cluster = d.getDisplayId()` on every name match without breaking — so when DL5 exposes both `shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_0` (id=3) and `_1` (id=4), the loop ended on id=4 instead of the (slightly more canonical) lowest matching id. Fixed: explicit `break` after the first match. (3) **R02 / R25 hidden display.** DL5 (and possibly other DL platforms) hides the physical cluster display from `DisplayManager.getDisplays()` for non-system uids — the dumpsys headers reveal a `Display Id=2` that is completely invisible to the framework call. v1.2.37 recon-v1 missed this entirely (only DL Manager APIs were probed). Fixed: `probeDisplays` now combines (a) the `DisplayManager.getDisplays()` enumeration with (b) a `dumpsys display \| grep 'Display Id='` fallback that lists every display id the platform_compat / SurfaceFlinger layer sees, including hidden ones. The new R25 (`probeClusterDisplayGeometry`) was rebuilt around the same dumpsys parse: it extracts every `Display Id=N` value via `grep -oE`, deduplicates with `sort -u`, then probes `wm size -d N` + `wm density -d N` for every non-zero id found (not just the heuristic "name contains fission/xdja/cluster/virtual" match). This catches the geometry of id=2 too, which is the actual cluster framebuffer. (4) **R03 token mismatch.** The PASS check required token `"uid=2000"` in stdout but `id -u` outputs only `"2000"` — so even on a perfectly working ADB shell, R03 reported WARN. Fixed: required tokens changed to `"2000"` and `"shell"` (Android `id -un` returns `"shell"` for uid 2000). (5–8) **R13–R18 wrong AOSP syntax.** The recon-v1 catalog assumed `am compat get <change>` was a valid verb (it's in some out-of-tree AOSP forks). On the DL5 production ROM the verb is rejected with `Invalid toggle value: 'get'` and `am compat --help` is also rejected with `Argument expected after compat`. The DL5 platform_compat shell exposes ONLY `enable \| disable \| reset \| reset-all \| list`, and `list` requires a package argument. Fixed: **R13** now probes `am compat enable 2>&1 \| head -40` (no package → triggers the official usage hint, non-destructive); **R14** is now `am compat list <ourPackage> 2>&1 \| head -80` (proper read-back syntax against our own package); **R15** is `am compat list ru.yandex.yandexmaps 2>&1 \| head -80` (the actual target of the FORCE_RESIZE_APP fix); **R16** is `am compat list com.waze 2>&1 \| head -80`; **R17** is `am compat reset 174042936 <ourPackage> 2>&1` (idempotent reset of the change ID against ourselves — confirms 174042936 is registered, no-op when no override is set); **R18** is `dumpsys platform_compat 2>&1 \| grep -E 'FORCE_RESIZE_APP\|OVERRIDE_MIN_ASPECT_RATIO\|NEVER_SANDBOX_DISPLAY_APIS' \| head -20` (final cross-check that the symbolic change names are registered in the platform_compat service). The catalog descriptions in `catalog()` were rewritten to match the new commands and to explicitly document the AOSP verb surface as discovered on DL5. (9) **R23 grep noise.** The recon-v1 grep `'Stack\|RootTask\|displayId\|mWindowingMode'` matched the `mGlobalConfig` / `mOverrideConfig` / `CurrentConfiguration` spam in `dumpsys activity activities` (every Configuration carries these field names too) → 30 lines of noise, zero useful task info. Fixed: grep now reads `grep -v mGlobalConfig \| grep -E '^  Display #\|Task=Task\|mBounds=Rect\|mWindowingMode=\|displayId='` — the `-v` pre-filter strips the global-config block, the second grep is anchored on the actual task-stanza prefixes. (10) **R26–R28 wrong attribute.** The grep was `'resizeable\|maxAspectRatio\|launchMode'` which matched the `<supports-screens>` block's `resizeable` attribute (manifest-level, declares the app supports being resized **by the launcher across screen sizes**) instead of `<activity android:resizeableActivity>` (activity-level, declares the activity can be put in freeform / multi-window). The two are orthogonal — Yandex Maps declares the former (`resizeable=true`) but explicitly sets `resizeableActivity="false"` on its main activity, which is the actual blocker. Fixed: grep is now `'resizeMode\|resizeableActivity\|maxAspectRatio\|launchMode\|targetSdk'` — `resizeMode` and `resizeableActivity` are the AOSP `pm dump` keys for the activity-level flag. **New helper.** Added `shellSync(Context, String) → String` (60 LoC private static), used by `probeDisplays` and `probeClusterDisplayGeometry` to run sub-probes synchronously (CountDownLatch via Object.wait/notify, 6 s timeout) and merge their stdout into the calling test's `r.detail`. Reuses the same `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` plumbing as `shellCapture`. **Catalog wording.** All R13–R18 + R23 + R26–R28 descriptions in `catalog()` were rewritten in English, with an inline note explaining the AOSP verb surface restriction discovered on DL5 (`enable \| disable \| reset \| reset-all \| list`, no `get`). This makes the recon report self-documenting for the next testers. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (295→296, 1.2.37-beta→1.2.38-beta), `UpdateChecker.java` (1-line fix + 6 LoC explanatory comment), `dilink5/Dl5ClusterReconRunner.java` (8 dispatcher fixes in `runOne`, `probePlatform` signature change, `probeDisplays` + `probeClusterDisplayGeometry` rebuilt around dumpsys, `probePerApp` grep narrowed, new `shellSync` helper, updated catalog descriptions). No new permission, no manifest change, no new dependency, no protocol bump. **Zero behavioural regression** on production paths: the only runtime-visible change for end users is the OTA prompt now correctly says "up to date" once v1.2.38-beta is installed. The recon tab is purely diagnostic, fixes are catalog-internal. **What this release does NOT include** (deferred to v1.2.39+): live FORCE_RESIZE_APP wire-up (still gated on a clean recon-v2 run from at least one DL5 user confirming `am compat list ru.yandex.yandexmaps` reports the change ID is enablable from app uid 2000), and the proposed isolated live-test panel (Enable / Test launch / Reset buttons) — both deferred pending the next field log. |
| **1.2.37-beta** | 295 | **DL5 cluster resize — dedicated recon tab + Telegram one-tap export.** Field log from a new DiLink 5 tester (Yandex Maps on cluster) confirmed that v1.2.35's revert of the global `force_resizable_activities` flag broke cluster resize on DL5 (Yandex Maps declares `resizeableActivity="false"` in its manifest → ATM coerces the activity to fullscreen at `realStartActivity` → `cmd activity task resize` becomes a silent no-op). Rather than re-flipping the global flag (which had system-wide side effects we explicitly reverted in v1.2.35), the chosen fix is the per-package compat override `am compat enable FORCE_RESIZE_APP <pkg>` (change ID 174042936) — but we must first confirm the compat framework shell surface is actually exposed on production BYD ROMs before shipping any live code path. **This release ships only the recon tooling**, no live cluster operation is wired yet. **New diagnostic tab "Cluster DL5"** (`activity_diag.xml` index 11, appended after Sniffer). The existing "Cluster" tab is renamed to **"Cluster DL3"** to make the platform split explicit (the old interactive POC panel `include_diag_cluster_poc.xml` is unchanged, only its tab label moves from `Cluster` to `Cluster DL3` via `diag_tab_cluster` string). New layout `include_diag_cluster_dl5.xml` (~140 LoC): NestedScrollView → header MaterialCardView (ic_science icon + title + subtitle + platform pill `tv_dl5_cluster_pill` + counters `tv_dl5_cluster_counters`) + action row (3 MaterialButtons: **Run recon** filled / **Copy report** outlined / **Send via Telegram** outlined) + programmatic test list `ll_dl5_cluster_test_list`. **New runner** `com.byd.dashcast.dilink5.Dl5ClusterReconRunner` (~380 LoC, reuses `DiLink5TestRunner.TestDef/TestResult/Status/Listener` types so DiagActivity row rendering is shared verbatim). Single-threaded ExecutorService `dl5-cluster-recon`, all listener callbacks marshalled to the UI thread via the main Looper. **Catalog: 30 recon-only tests** (no live cluster launch, no `am compat enable`, no settings mutation — pure probing): **R01–R05** platform identity (`Build.MODEL/PRODUCT/FINGERPRINT`, DisplayManager inventory with cluster auto-detection on name match `fission\|xdja\|cluster\|virtual`, ADB local uid probe, SELinux enforce, cluster apps installed scan over Yandex Maps / Google Maps / Waze / Sygic / TomTom); **R06–R12** shell verb surface (`am`/`am start`/`cmd activity`/`cmd activity task`/`wm`/`cmd window`/`cmd display` — all via `2>&1 \| head -N` to capture every `--help` line including `--display` / `--windowingMode` / `resize` / `set-ignore-orientation-request` / etc.); **R13–R18** compat framework (`am compat --help`, `am compat list \| head -60`, then targeted `am compat get` against FORCE_RESIZE_APP / 174042936 / NEVER_SANDBOX_DISPLAY_APIS / OVERRIDE_MIN_ASPECT_RATIO scoped to our own package — this is the **critical block** that determines whether v1.2.38 can ship the per-app override fix); **R19–R23** global settings + dumpsys snapshots (`force_resizable_activities` current value, settings list global grep for `resizable\|freeform\|multi\|sandbox\|ignore_orientation`, dumpsys window/display/activity keyword filters); **R24–R25** wm geometry per-display (`wm size` + `wm density` on default display, then again on the detected cluster display); **R26–R28** per-app manifest probe via `pm dump <pkg> \| grep -E 'resizeable\|maxAspectRatio\|launchMode\|targetSdk' \| head -10` for Yandex Maps / Google Maps / Waze, with auto-SKIP when the app is not installed; **R29** service list filter for `auto_container\|cluster\|fission\|xdja\|window\|activity_task`; **R30** `getprop` filter for `resizable\|freeform\|multi\|sandbox\|cluster\|dilink\|fission\|xdja`. Each test runs through `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` synchronously (CountDownLatch via Object wait/notify, 6 s timeout per probe), captures stdout into the result detail, and PASS/WARN/FAIL classification based on presence of expected tokens. Total recon run < 20 s typical. **Telegram one-tap export.** New `AppLogger.shareReportToTelegram(Context, String)` (~65 LoC): probes 5 known Telegram package names in order (`org.telegram.messenger`, `org.telegram.messenger.web`, `org.telegram.plus`, `nekox.messenger`, `org.thunderdog.challegram`) via `PackageManager.getPackageInfo`, on hit fires a direct `ACTION_SEND` with `setPackage(hit)` + FileProvider URI (`byd_report_*.log` containing the report + the full in-memory AppLogger ringbuffer) + `EXTRA_STREAM` + `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION`. Three-level fallback chain: TG-found+file → direct intent; no-TG+file → generic chooser via `shareFile`; no-file → text-only chooser. The new "Send via Telegram" button in the Cluster DL5 panel wires straight to this helper, so the tester ships the full recon report back to the developer in one tap — no manual file-picking, no "share" submenu hunting. **DiagActivity wiring.** New constants `TAB_CLUSTER_DL5 = 11`, `TAB_COUNT = 12`. New fields: `panelClusterDl5`, `tvDl5ClusterSubtitle/Pill/Counters`, `btnDl5ClusterRunAll/CopyReport/SendTelegram`, `llDl5ClusterTestList`, `dl5ClusterRowViews`, `dl5ClusterLastResults`. New methods `bindClusterDl5Panel`, `prepareClusterDl5TestRows`, `runClusterDl5AllTests`, `updateClusterDl5Counters`, `copyClusterDl5Report`, `sendClusterDl5ToTelegram` (~110 LoC). Row binding delegates to the existing `bindDl5Row` since `Dl5ClusterReconRunner` shares the result types with `DiLink5TestRunner`. `showPanelForTab` extended to handle the new tab; `panelComingSoon` visibility condition extended. **Strings.** 15 new keys (`diag_tab_cluster_dl5`, `diag_dl5_cluster_header_title/subtitle`, `diag_dl5_cluster_pill_unknown/dl5/other`, `diag_dl5_cluster_run_all/copy_report/send_telegram`, `diag_dl5_cluster_counters_idle`, `diag_dl5_cluster_toast_report_copied/no_results/telegram_missing`), 1 modified (`diag_tab_cluster` value `Cluster` → `Cluster DL3`). French (default) + English shipped, other 10 locales fall back to fr/en (no `MissingTranslation` lint regression — the keys are tagged for the next i18n pass). **No live cluster operation.** Per explicit user instruction ("d'abord on fait le maximum de tests pour attraper les commandes disponibles, puis avec le retour on fera les tests etc."), this release is recon-only. Once the tester ships a report via Telegram and we confirm `am compat list` contains `FORCE_RESIZE_APP` (or its numeric ID 174042936) accessible from app uid 2000, v1.2.38 will wire the per-app fix into `ClusterService.startActivityViaShell` (chain `am compat enable 174042936 <pkg> ; am force-stop <pkg> ; am start --display N --windowingMode 5 ...`) with the existing global-flag path removed. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (294→295, 1.2.36-beta→1.2.37-beta), new `dilink5/Dl5ClusterReconRunner.java`, new `res/layout/include_diag_cluster_dl5.xml`, `AppLogger.java` (+`shareReportToTelegram`), `DiagActivity.java` (+import, +tab const, +panel binding, +methods), `activity_diag.xml` (+tab + panel include), `values/strings.xml` + `values-en/strings.xml` (rename + 15 new keys). No new permission, no manifest change, no protocol bump, no new dependency. **Zero behavioural regression** on DL2/DL3/DL4/DL5 production paths (the new tab is purely diagnostic, the rename `Cluster → Cluster DL3` is cosmetic and does not affect the panel id `panel_cluster_poc` nor any code path). |
| **1.2.36-beta** | 294 | **TetherFi-based Hotspot — full UI redesign + live stats + connected clients.** Hotspot screen rebuilt around the [TetherFi](https://github.com/pyamsoft/tetherfi) open-source proxy (no carrier tether quota / no BYD MIUI tether restrictions). New landscape 2-column layout (`activity_hotspot.xml` ~530 LoC): left hero card (status pill, update badge, Start/Stop/Toggle/Open TetherFi buttons); right column stacks Clients-and-Traffic card + Watchdog card + Autostart-on-boot card. New drawables `bg_hero_icon`, `bg_side_icon`, `bg_count_pill`, `ic_devices`, `ic_monitor_heart`, `ic_wifi_tethering`, `ic_sim_card`. **TetherFi integration** in `HotspotActivity.java`: only entry point is `com.pyamsoft.tetherfi/.activity.ProxyTileActivity` with `key_action` ∈ START/STOP/TOGGLE (no reflection, no private APIs). Independent stats probe (`statsTick`, 5 s) decoupled from the watchdog so stats keep working when auto-restart is OFF; probes `dumpsys activity services com.pyamsoft.tetherfi \| grep ProxyForegroundService` via `AdbLocalClient`. Session lifecycle (`onProbeTransition`) snapshots `SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()` + TetherFi UID (cached via PackageManager) + `TrafficStats.getUidRxBytes/getUidTxBytes` baselines on DOWN→UP; resets to -1 on UP→DOWN. **Connected clients enumeration**: combined ADB payload `dumpsys wifip2p 2>/dev/null; echo '===ARP==='; cat /proc/net/arp 2>/dev/null`; static `parseClients(String)` splits on the separator, builds MAC→IPv4 map from `arp`, walks the `wifip2p` dump for `Client list` / `mClients` / `clients:` / `wifip2pdevice` sections and pairs each MAC with the nearest `deviceName=` token; static inner type `HClient { mac, name, ip }` + regex `P_MAC` / `P_NAME`. `renderClients(List<HClient>)` updates count pill, populates `ll_clients_list` via programmatic `buildClientRow(c)` (icon + name TextView weight=1 + monospace IP TextView), toggles empty state. Per-client RX/TX intentionally not shown (kernel does not expose per-MAC counters without root). **TetherFi update checker** (`TetherFiUpdateChecker.java`): background HEAD/JSON probe against TetherFi GitHub Releases at boot, compares installed APK version against `tag_name`, caches result in SharedPreferences; hotspot card reads it on resume and surfaces an "Update available" badge with a link button. Pure best-effort, all failures swallowed silently. **Boot autostart**: `BootReceiver` extended to fire `ProxyTileActivity` with `key_action=START` when the new pref `hotspot_autostart_on_boot` is true; also kicks the update checker once. **Strings**: 40+ new keys for the redesigned screen, both `fr` (default) and `en` localized (`hotspot_clients_title`, `hotspot_clients_empty`, `hotspot_stat_uptime/rx/tx`, etc.). **Misc**: small wiring tweaks in `MainActivity`, `SettingsActivity`, `activity_main.xml`, `activity_settings.xml` to surface the hotspot entry point with the new card style; minor helper added in `Platform.java`. **Known limitations**: per-client bandwidth not supported (root required); client list only shows devices currently in the ARP table (peers without recent IP traffic may briefly be missing); update checker requires internet at boot, otherwise the badge appears on the next launch with connectivity. No new permissions, no manifest protocol bump. |
| **1.2.32-build223** | 223 | **DL5 resize — Fix #4 : neutralisation système du flag manifeste `resizeableActivity="false"` via `force_resizable_activities` + force-stop avant chaque launch cluster.** Le field log `log/BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_194219.txt` (build 222 installée chez le testeur Dilink5 Yandex Maps) confirme que les trois fixes v1.2.30 atterrissent correctement : (i) `am start --display 3 --windowingMode 5` est honoré par ATM, qui crée la **root task** en `windowingMode=5` (ligne `getOrCreateRootTask windowingMode=5, activityType=1` au launch L15212), (ii) les bounds sont bien exprimées dans l'espace framebuffer du display 3 (`cluster FREEFORM bounds=Rect(134, 174 - 1786, 906) display=3 1920×1080` L15117), (iii) la sonde VERIFY post-resize est dispatchée et `cmd activity task resize 97 134 174 1786 906` retourne `exit=0` (L16040). **MAIS** le VERIFY dumpsys montre que l'activité elle-même se retrouve immédiatement coercée en `mWindowingMode=fullscreen mBounds=Rect(0, 0 - 1920, 1080)` (L16311+). **Cause racine — quatrième couche du problème.** Yandex Maps (comme la majorité des apps de navigation : Yandex Navi, Google Maps, Sygic, Waze, etc.) déclare `android:resizeableActivity="false"` dans son manifeste pour bloquer le split-screen. Sur API 32 cette propriété est respectée à la lettre par ActivityTaskManagerService : une activité non-resizable **ne peut pas** entrer en freeform, peu importe le windowing mode de la root task parent. ATM force la transition vers fullscreen au moment du `realStartActivity`, et `cmd activity task resize` devient ensuite un **silent no-op** sur cette task (retourne `exit=0` sans modifier les bounds — exact symptôme observé). **Fix.** Activation programmatique du toggle Dev Option « Force activities to be resizable » (clé persistante `Settings.Global.force_resizable_activities`). Quand ce flag vaut 1, ATM ignore le manifest `resizeableActivity` de toutes les apps et les traite uniformément comme resizable, autorisant freeform + bounds resize à s'appliquer. La modif se fait via `settings put global force_resizable_activities 1` exécuté à travers le shell uid 2000 d'`AdbLocalClient` (uid shell détient `WRITE_SECURE_SETTINGS` system-wide, perm signature/privileged inaccessible à l'uid app 10148). Le flag est persistant ; le re-poser à chaque launch est idempotent et auto-correcteur si un autre process ou un reset de Settings le passait à 0. **Force-stop préalable.** ATM évalue `force_resizable_activities` au moment de la création de la task, pas en cours de vie d'un process existant. Pour qu'une app déjà lancée picke le nouveau réglage, il faut tuer son process puis le relancer. Ajout de `am force-stop <pkg>` entre le `settings put` et le `am start --display N --windowingMode 5 ...`. Conséquence : le launch cluster sur DL5 est désormais déterministe sur toute app, y compris celles qui posent `resizeableActivity="false"`. **VERIFY probe affinée.** Le sed/awk précédent dumpait `mGlobalConfig` (une entrée par Configuration carrier dans l'arbre activities), puis `head -20` tronquait AVANT d'atteindre la vraie stanza de la task. Remplacé par un range awk `/Task=Task\{[^}]*#N[ }]/,/Task=Task\{[^}]*#[0-9]+[ }]/` qui isole précisément la stanza de la task ciblée, suivi d'un grep sur les seuls champs qui comptent (`mBounds`, `WindowingMode`, `displayId`, `resizeMode`, `#N`). Beaucoup plus de signal, plus de bruit `mGlobalConfig`. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (222→223), `ClusterService.java` (`startActivityViaShell` : chain shell `settings put + am force-stop + am start` avec bloc commentaire `v1.2.32 — DL5 resize second-order fix` ~30 LoC ; `resizeActiveTask` DL5 branch : VERIFY probe stanza-targeted ~10 LoC). Aucune nouvelle permission, aucun nouveau resource, aucun bump de protocole, aucune nouvelle dépendance. Aucun changement de comportement sur DL3 / DL4 / DL2 (la branche est dans `startActivityViaShell` qui n'est appelée que par le chemin `isDiLink5`). **Contrat comportemental.** DL5 testeur — install v1.2.32 par-dessus v1.2.31, relancer projection cluster, ouvrir Yandex Maps sur cluster via DashCast : visuellement, l'app Yandex Maps doit désormais respecter les marges H/V configurées et apparaître inscrite dans le rectangle `(insetH, insetV, fb_w-insetH, fb_h-insetV)` du framebuffer cluster, et non plein-cadre. Field log doit montrer `am force-stop ru.yandex.yandexmaps` suivi de `am start --display 3 --windowingMode 5 ...` puis VERIFY avec `mBounds=Rect(134, 174 - 1786, 906)` et `mWindowingMode=freeform` (au lieu de fullscreen). DL3 / DL4 / DL2 : aucun chemin nouveau exercé. |

| **1.2.30-build221** | 221 | **DL5 resize root cause fix — trois correctifs en cascade dans `ClusterService.java`.** Diagnostic déclenché par le field log DL5 `log/BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_184727.txt` (utilisateur Yandex Maps, build 219 installée) : l'app est bien lancée sur display 3 (XDJAScreenProjection_0, taskId=49 confirmé), `cmd activity task resize 49 374 275 1546 445` retourne `exit=0` systématiquement… mais la dalle cluster (display 2 physique 1920×720) reste full bleed sans la moindre marge visible. Trois causes étroitement imbriquées trouvées, fixées ensemble. **Fix #1 — `startActivityViaShell()` ajoute `--windowingMode 5`.** La commande shell était `am start --display 3 -a MAIN -c LAUNCHER -n pkg/cls --activity-clear-task` — sans aucun flag de windowing mode, la task atterrissait en `WINDOWING_MODE_FULLSCREEN` (=1). Or `cmd activity task resize` est un **silent no-op sur une task FULLSCREEN depuis API 30+** (la méthode interne détecte le mode et bail sans erreur ; AOSP `ActivityTaskManagerService.resizeTask` exige FREEFORM ou FREEFORM_PIP). Ajout de `--windowingMode 5` (flag `am start` officiel AOSP depuis API 26, toujours présent en API 32) entre `--display N` et `-a MAIN`. La task lance directement en FREEFORM, les bounds passent. Le fait que `applyClusterFreeformBounds()` calculait déjà `ActivityOptions.setLaunchWindowingMode(5)` + `setLaunchBounds(…)` ne servait à rien sur DL5 puisqu'on by-passe `startActivityViaIAM()` au profit du shell (SecurityException IATM uid 10148) — les ActivityOptions étaient construites puis jetées. **Fix #2 — `resizeActiveTask()` exprime les bounds dans l'espace framebuffer du display 3.** Le log révèle `cluster FREEFORM bounds=Rect(374, 275 - 1546, 805) display=3 1920×1080` au launch (via `d.getRealSize()` sur display 3 = 1080) MAIS `bounds=Rect(374, 275 - 1546, 445)` au resize (via `mInputForwarder.getClusterHeight()` = 720, hardcodé côté `ClusterInputForwarder.mClusterHeight`). Deux espaces de coordonnées différents. La task vit dans le **framebuffer du display 3 (1920×1080)**, pas dans l'espace de la dalle physique (1920×720) — c'est Qt côté natif qui scale 1080→720 vers la dalle via la Surface consumée par le TextureView XDJA. `resizeTask` applique les bounds dans l'espace de la display du task, donc il faut 1080 sinon on shrink dans un buffer déjà plus grand. Le code interroge maintenant directement `DisplayManager.getDisplay(clusterId).getRealSize()` au moment du resize (fallback `mInputForwarder.getClusterWidth/Height()` si la détection échoue, pour préserver le comportement DL3). Sur DL3 cette même logique renvoie 1920×720 (display 1 = la dalle elle-même), aucun changement de comportement. Conséquence : les marges H/V que l'utilisateur saisit sont interprétées dans l'espace framebuffer ; sur DL5 elles seront ensuite légèrement compressées verticalement par Qt (facteur 720/1080 = 0.667 sur V), mais visuellement cohérentes. **Fix #3 — sonde de vérification dumpsys post-resize.** Après succès de `cmd activity task resize`, on dispatche immédiatement `dumpsys activity activities \| grep -E 'taskId=N\|Task=…#N\|mBounds\|getWindowingMode\|windowingMode='` (très filtré pour rester sous le quota AdbLocal). Le résultat est loggé en INFO préfixé « resizeActiveTask VERIFY ». Permet de confirmer en field log que (a) la task est bien en `windowingMode=5` (FREEFORM) après notre `am start --windowingMode 5`, et (b) que `mBounds` reflète exactement le `Rect(insetH, insetV, fbW - insetH, fbH - insetV)` qu'on a passé. Si ces deux invariants sont confirmés mais que rien n'apparait visuellement, la prochaine investigation portera sur la couche Qt/XDJA (le TextureView consommateur de la Surface display 3 peut filtrer ou stretcher différemment). **Compatibilité plateformes.** DL3 (Seal EU API 29) : (i) Fix #1 inactif (DL3 utilise `startActivityViaIAM()`, pas le shell DL5), (ii) Fix #2 transparent (`getRealSize()` sur display 1 = 1920×720 = identique à `getClusterHeight()`), (iii) Fix #3 inactif (DL3 utilise la branche `else` legacy `am task resize`). DL2 (HMI seul) : aucun cluster display → `clusterId<=0` → méthode bail upfront, aucun chemin touché. DL4 : si jamais détecté via Platform, même logique que DL3. Zero régression possible sur les plateformes hors DL5. **Build.** Compilé sans erreur ni warning nouveau. À valider en voiture par l'utilisateur DL5 sur Yandex Maps : ouvrir l'app sur cluster avec des marges H=100/V=80 déjà définies, observer si la zone affichée est réellement réduite, puis récupérer un log `byd_log_*.log` pour lire les lignes `resizeActiveTask VERIFY` (windowingMode + mBounds effectifs après ATM). |
