| **1.2.27-build218** | 218 | **DL4 — auto-detection of `BYD-AUTO/DiLink4.0` (API 29) + hard neutralisation of FORCE_ON DiLink 5 override on DL4 hardware.** Field log `BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_175751.txt` from a *new* testeur on a different car proved the cluster never activated: every projection attempt logged `Service auto_container does not exist` (11+ repeats across `log/byd_log_20260523_173033.log.bin`, `log/byd_report_20260523_172928.log.bin`, `log/byd_report_20260523_173322.log.bin`). **Root cause.** The car is a genuine DiLink 4.0 (`ro.product.name=DiLink4.0`, `Build.MODEL=DiLink4.0 For BYD AUTO`, `Build.FINGERPRINT=BYD-AUTO/DiLink4.0/DiLink4.0:10/QKQ1.210218.001/…`, API 29, hardware=qcom), but the user had manually flipped the « DiLink 5 » switch in Settings (FORCE_ON) — possibly because previous releases targeted DL5 heavily so the option sounded like the right one to try. With FORCE_ON enabled, `Platform.isDiLink5(ctx)` returned `true` → `AdbLocalClient.autoContainerSvcName` returned the snake_case `auto_container` binder name (DL5 ABI) → every `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 16 s16 ""` was rejected by the platform because on DL3/DL4 the binder is registered as the PascalCase `AutoContainer` (confirmed by Beta Engine 28/28 PASS in `byd_report_20260523_172928.log.bin`: P6 *AutoContainer reachable, descriptor=android.os.IAutoContainer (transact ret=true)*, P13 *sendInfo(1000,30,"") via transact(2) on android.os.IAutoContainer (ret=true)*). Result: 0 PRESENTATION display ever materialised, every `am start --display -1` landed on the HMI itself, the user saw nothing on the cluster. **Fix.** `Platform` now (a) auto-detects DiLink 4 from fingerprint/product/model (substring match on `dilink4` / `dilink_4` / `dilink 4`) **gated by API 28-29** so a future ROM mis-named « DiLink4 » on Android 12 cannot be mis-routed; (b) exposes a new `isDiLink4(Context)` accessor (no user override — DL4 detection is purely automatic, same contract as the existing `isDiLink2`); (c) **hard-neutralises** the FORCE_ON DL5 override on any device where `autoDiLink4 == true`: `isDiLink5(ctx)` returns `false` unconditionally before reading the override, with `describeMode(ctx)` reporting `AUTO=off (DL4 detected — DL5 FORCE_ON ignored)` so the Settings panel surfaces the absorbed mistake to power users. Downstream code is auto-rerouted with zero changes: `AdbLocalClient.autoContainerSvcName` falls back to the CamelCase `AutoContainer` (already its non-DL5 default), `ClusterManager.activateClusterDisplay` falls back to the DL3 path (sendInfo 30 → 6 s → sendInfo 16 → 6 s → sendInfo 35 → poll DisplayManager for `fission_bg_xdjaVirtualSurface`). **Why this is conservative.** No new permission, no manifest change, no new dependency, no behavioural change on DL3 (Seal EU / non-DL4 BYD) or DL5 cars — only DL4 hardware sees the neutralisation, and only after positive fingerprint+API match. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (217→218, 1.2.26→1.2.27), `platform/Platform.java` (+`autoDiLink4` field, +`detectDiLink4(...)` static, +`isAutoDetectedDiLink4()` accessor, +`isDiLink4(Context)` accessor, rewrote `isDiLink5(Context)` guard + `describeMode(Context)`, ~40 LoC net). **Validation plan.** (a) DL4 testeur (the user who reported this issue) — install v1.2.27, launch projection from the dashboard → cluster shows `clusterdebug` (or whatever default cluster target the build ships with) within 12-13 s of the tap. Logcat should show `ClusterManager: activateClusterDisplay (path=DL3 fast/full)` and **never** `Service auto_container does not exist`. Re-toggling « DiLink 5 » FORCE_ON in Settings must have **zero observable effect** on cluster activation (the guard absorbs it). (b) Settings → DL5 toggle subtitle shows `AUTO=off (DL4 detected — DL5 FORCE_ON ignored)` when FORCE_ON is set; `AUTO=off (DL4 detected)` when AUTO or FORCE_OFF. (c) Seal EU DL3 testeur — identical behaviour to v1.2.26 (DL4 detection requires the `dilink4` substring + API 28-29; Seal EU is API 29 but fingerprint does not contain `dilink4` so the new path is never taken). (d) DL5 testeur — identical behaviour to v1.2.26 (DL5 fingerprint never contains `dilink4`, autoDiLink4 stays false). **Follow-up (v1.2.28+ if requested).** A dedicated `TEST DL4` diagnostic suite (probing sendInfo 30/16/35 sequentially with DisplayManager dumps between each step, modelled on the existing `DiLink5TestRunner`/`DiLink2TestRunner` pattern) is on standby — not shipped in this release to keep the patch surgical and unblock the DL4 testeur immediately. |
| **1.2.26-build217** | 217 | **DL5 — cluster app resize: skip `am task resize` (silent no-op on API 30+) and shell straight to `cmd activity task resize`.** Field log `BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_172007.txt` of v1.2.25 (which fixed the typing path end-to-end — user confirmed « ENFIN ! ») surfaced that the cluster-app resize UI in MainActivity (Apply Resize button + per-app autoApplyInsets) was a no-op on DL5. Every press produced a clean 4-line trace pair, repeated 11 times for Yandex Maps taskId=38 (e.g. `bounds=Rect(206, 91 - 1714, 629)` at 17:21:15.717): `W ClusterService: resizeActiveTask reflection failed: SecurityException: Permission Denial: resizeTask() from pid=22748, uid=10148 requires android.permission.MANAGE_ACTIVITY_TASKS` → `D AdbLocalClient: am task resize 38 206 91 1714 629 2>&1; echo "exit=$?" -> exit=0` → `I ClusterService: resizeActiveTask 'am task resize' -> "exit=0" (looksOk=true)` — and the cluster visibly **did not resize**. **Root cause.** The v1.2.18 code comment already flagged that `am task resize` on AOSP API 30+ « often returns empty stdout (apparent success) but no visible effect » (the `am` binary was rewritten and most `am task` subcommands became silent shells around `cmd activity task …`). v1.2.18 added a `cmd activity task resize` fallback but gated it on a `looksOk` heuristic that treats `exit=0` (with no `Unknown command` / `error` / `exception` substring) as success. On DL5 (API 32) `am task resize` deterministically returns `exit=0` → `looksOk=true` → the fallback was never reached, ever. **Fix.** `ClusterService.resizeActiveTask` now branches on `AdbLocalClient.isDiLink5Safe(this)` after the reflection path's expected `SecurityException` (no `MANAGE_ACTIVITY_TASKS` for app uid 10148). On DL5 it skips `am task resize` entirely and dispatches `cmd activity task resize <taskId> <l> <t> <r> <b> 2>&1; echo "exit=$?"` (the canonical AOSP API 30+ verb, reached via the same uid=shell pipe that already executes `pm grant`, `service call AutoContainer`, `settings put secure …`). On DL3 (API 29) the legacy `am task resize` → fallback chain is preserved verbatim — `am` still works on Android 10. **Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (216→217, 1.2.25→1.2.26), `ClusterService.java` (~30 LoC in `resizeActiveTask`: extracted the DL5 branch that dispatches `cmd activity task resize` directly; kept the legacy DL3 chain in an `else`; expanded the inline comment to document the v1.2.26 field-log evidence). No new permissions, no manifest change, no new strings, no new dependency, no protocol bump. **Behavioural contract.** DL5: drag overscan sliders in MainActivity (or the global Settings sliders → auto-applied per-app via `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded`) → press Apply → `cmd activity task resize` fires with the computed bounds → cluster app shrinks/expands within the safe area in ≤ 1 frame. DL3 (`am task resize` is healthy on API 29 there): no behavioural change, legacy path executes as before. DL2: cluster path doesn't exist (zero impact). **Validation plan.** (a) DL5 testeur — install v1.2.26 over v1.2.25, launch Yandex Maps on cluster, open MainActivity, move H/V sliders to e.g. 200/100, press Apply → cluster Yandex visibly indents by 200 px H / 100 px V. (b) `logcat \| grep ClusterService` should show `resizeActiveTask DL5: dispatching 'cmd activity task resize' (skipping 'am task resize' — known silent no-op on API 30+)` then `resizeActiveTask 'cmd activity task resize' -> "exit=0"` with no further `am task` line. (c) Press Reset Insets → cluster Yandex returns to full 1920×720 within a frame. (d) DL3 — typing app keyboard sliders still work as before (legacy `am task resize` path). |
| **1.2.25-build216** | 216 | **DL5 — KeyboardBridge: don't force a second tap after ADB enable + reach the cluster window via `getWindowsOnAllDisplays()`.** Field log `BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_170436.txt` of v1.2.24 (which fixed the ANR / Back-button-freeze) surfaced two follow-on bugs reported by the user: (1) every time the user tapped the ⌨ icon the « DashCast Cluster IME ✓ » Toast appeared and forced them to tap a second time before they could type; (2) typing in the bridge produced zero text on the cluster Yandex search field — only `W ClusterImeWatcher: setTextOnCluster no-op: no focused editable on cluster` lines (at 17:05:14.156 and 17:05:28.765). **(Root cause — Bug 1.)** v1.2.23's `tryAdbEnableA11y` success branch called `finish()` after the `OK:…` reply, on the design that the user would retap the ⌨ icon and find the freshly bound service ready. But the field log proves `AccessibilityManagerService` rebinds `ClusterImeWatcherService` within ~30 ms of the `settings put` write — `onServiceConnected — watching cluster focus events (DL5=true)` arrives at 17:05:02.243, BEFORE the success callback at 17:05:02.272. So the bridge already has IME focus, the soft keyboard is up, the worker `sInstance` is alive — there is nothing to wait for. Finishing the bridge here is pure user friction. The « toast every time » perception is because the OS revokes a11y enablement on every package update (`AccessibilityManagerService.onPackageUpdateFinished`) as a standard Android security measure, so after each install the secure setting really did contain no entry for our service. With `finish()` removed the user goes through the enable Toast exactly once after each install, then keeps typing in the same bridge instance. **(Root cause — Bug 2.)** `findClusterFocusedEditable()` called `AccessibilityService.getWindows()` — which on every Android API returns **only windows on the default display**, even with `FLAG_RETRIEVE_INTERACTIVE_WINDOWS`. The cluster Yandex search EditText lives on display 2 (composed face) / display 3 (shadow framebuffer driven by the XDJA fission compositor), neither of which is the default. So the window walk found exactly zero candidates and the worker logged `no focused editable on cluster` on every keystroke. **Fix.** `findClusterFocusedEditable()` now uses `AccessibilityService.getWindowsOnAllDisplays()` (API 30+, DL5 ships API 32) — returns `SparseArray<List<AccessibilityWindowInfo>>` keyed by displayId, exactly what we need — and iterates every display, preferring `displayId != 0` for the « bestNonDefault » slot. Falls back to the single-display walk on older APIs (DL3 stays gated by `isDiLink5` upstream so this is just belt-and-braces). Also factored the per-window focus extraction into a static `pickFocusedEditableFrom(AccessibilityWindowInfo, String selfPkg)` helper which explicitly **skips windows from our own package** — necessary because v1.2.25 keeps the bridge open after the ADB success, so the bridge's own EditText would otherwise compete for `findFocus(FOCUS_INPUT)` and win on display 0, causing us to push the user's keystrokes back into the local field instead of the cluster Yandex search. **(Touched files.)** `app/build.gradle` (215→216, 1.2.24→1.2.25), `KeyboardBridgeActivity.java` (~5 LoC: removed `finish()` from the ADB success branch; expanded the inline comment to document why the bridge must stay open), `ime/ClusterImeWatcherService.java` (~110 LoC: rewrote `findClusterFocusedEditable` around `getWindowsOnAllDisplays` + extracted `pickFocusedEditableFrom` helper with self-package filter). No new permissions, no new strings, no manifest change, no new dependency, no protocol bump. **(Behavioural contract.)** DL5 with a11y already enabled (steady-state after this release): tap ⌨ → bridge opens → IME pops up → no Toast (the `isClusterImeWatcherEnabled` check returns true) → typing routes to cluster Yandex immediately. DL5 right after install: tap ⌨ → bridge opens → IME pops up → Toast `✓` (ADB enabled the service) → user keeps typing in the same bridge → routes. DL3/DL2: gated by `isDiLink5`, zero behavioural change. **(Validation plan.)** (a) DL5 testeur — install v1.2.25 over v1.2.24, tap ⌨ ONCE → bridge opens + IME up + Toast `✓` once → type « test » → cluster Yandex search shows « test » within a frame → tap IME Enter → search executes. (b) Tap ⌨ a second time later: bridge opens + IME up, NO Toast, typing routes immediately. (c) Logcat — search for `setTextOnCluster no-op: no focused editable on cluster`: should be silent during normal typing; only emitted if the cluster app has no focused EditText (i.e. user opened the bridge with no search field in focus on the cluster). Search for `setTextOnCluster ACTION_SET_TEXT refused`: would indicate the cluster app actively rejected the action (rare, per-app workaround needed). |
| **1.2.24-build215** | 215 | **DL5 — `ClusterImeWatcherService.setTextOnCluster` / `performImeEnterOnCluster` moved off the UI thread (fixes the ANR that killed the app after v1.2.23 ADB-enable succeeded).** Field log `BYD_RE_Sniffer_20260523_164557.txt` of v1.2.23 produced an excellent positive trace at 16:46:18.804 — `I KeyboardBridge: tryAdbEnableA11y SUCCESS: OK:com.byd.airconditioning/.gesture.AcGestureService:com.android.systemui/.custom.StatusBarAccessibilityService:com.byd.autovoice/…/SceneSayService:com.byd.dashcast/com.byd.dashcast.ime.ClusterImeWatcherService` (the colon-separated preservation worked, BYD's own services intact + ours appended) immediately followed by `I ClusterImeWatcher: onServiceConnected — watching cluster focus events (DL5=true)` (AccessibilityManagerService rebound our service on the next tick, exactly as v1.2.23's design predicted). The user saw the `DashCast Cluster IME ✓` Toast, retapped ⌨, IME came up. Then everything fell apart: typed characters never reached Yandex on the cluster, and tactile **Back was unresponsive for 17-18 seconds**. Logcat smoking gun: `I am_anr (991): [0,15738,com.byd.dashcast,…,Input dispatching timed out … (server) is not responding. Waited 5000ms for KeyEvent]` at 16:46:36.233, followed by the system-wide SIGQUIT thread-dump fan-out, then `E ActivityManager: ANR in com.byd.dashcast (com.byd.dashcast/.KeyboardBridgeActivity)` at 16:46:47.206, then `am_proc_died(991): [0,15738,com.byd.dashcast,0,2]` — and the main-thread stack in the tombstone contained `at com.byd.dashcast.ime.ClusterImeWatcherService.setTextOnCluster(ClusterImeWatcherService.java:256)`. **Root cause.** v1.2.12's `setTextOnCluster()` ran fully synchronously on the caller's thread — and the caller was `KeyboardBridgeActivity`'s `TextWatcher.afterTextChanged`, i.e. the main UI thread. The body calls `findClusterFocusedEditable()`, which iterates `getWindows()` and runs `root.findFocus(FOCUS_INPUT)` against every interactive window — including the cluster windows on display 3, which round-trips through `AccessibilityManagerService` over Binder. With `FLAG_RETRIEVE_INTERACTIVE_WINDOWS` and a fission compositor topology this walk can take dozens of ms per window; a fast typist queues N walks back-to-back on the main thread; the next KeyEvent dispatch waits ≥ 5 000 ms → ANR → process killed → the user's subsequent Back taps land on nothing for the 17-18 s until the system finishes the SIGQUIT dump + process restart; after restart the mirror touch pipeline is in a transient state until the daemon reconnects (visible in the same log as the `attemptStartMirror : service non disponible` repeats around 16:46:55). The 3 user-reported symptoms (text not arriving, Back frozen, mirror touch broken after relaunch) all collapse to this single root cause. **Fix.** `ClusterImeWatcherService` now owns a private `HandlerThread "cluster-ime-relay"` started in `onCreate` (BACKGROUND priority), torn down in `onDestroy` via `removeCallbacksAndMessages(null)` + `quitSafely()`. **(1) `setTextOnCluster(CharSequence)`** is now strictly non-blocking — it stores the latest text in `volatile mPendingText`, removes any in-flight `mSetTextRunner`, and reposts it with **SET_TEXT_DEBOUNCE_MS = 80 ms** coalescing window so a typist filling « apple street » fires the a11y tree walk + `ACTION_SET_TEXT` at most ~12 times/second, last-writer-wins. Returns `true` optimistically (failures logged inside the runnable, no caller-thread blocking ever). The runnable itself runs the original v1.2.12 logic (`findClusterFocusedEditable` → `performAction(ACTION_SET_TEXT, args)`) on the worker thread. **(2) `performImeEnterOnCluster()`** also dispatches to the worker; it first cancels the pending debounced setText, then on the worker thread synchronously runs `mSetTextRunner` (so Enter sees the final text, not a half-debounced view), then walks the focus once and fires `ACTION_IME_ENTER` (API 30+) or `ACTION_CLICK` (fallback). Returns `true` immediately for the same non-blocking contract. **(3) Worker lifecycle.** Worker is created on each `onCreate`/destroyed on each `onDestroy`, never crosses service-rebind boundaries (matches the existing `sInstance` lifecycle). Belt-and-braces: both helpers null-check `mWorker` before posting (covers a vanishingly narrow window between `onCreate` failure and rebind). **(4) Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (214→215, 1.2.23→1.2.24), `ime/ClusterImeWatcherService.java` (+`mWorkerThread/mWorker/mPendingText/mSetTextRunner` fields, `onCreate` spins up the worker, `onDestroy` tears it down, `setTextOnCluster` rewritten to non-blocking dispatch + debounce, `performImeEnterOnCluster` rewritten to worker-dispatched flush-then-enter, ~90 LoC net). No new permissions, no new strings, no manifest change, no new dependency. The on-cluster typing UX remains identical from the user's perspective (text appears in the cluster field as they type, Enter executes search) — only the threading model changes. **(5) Behavioural contract.** DL3 / DL2: bridge entry point still gated by `isDiLink5` (path never exercised), zero behavioural change. DL5 with a11y bound: typing in the bridge no longer blocks the main thread → no ANR → Back button stays responsive → process is not killed → mirror touch pipeline is preserved across the typing session. v1.2.23's ADB auto-enable + 5-intent Settings fallback both kept as-is. **(6) Validation plan.** (a) DL5 testeur — install v1.2.24 over v1.2.23 (versionCode bumped), tap ⌨ → bridge opens, a11y already enabled from v1.2.23 → no Toast/ADB roundtrip needed → IME pops up → type rapidly « apple street » → each character (debounced at 80 ms) appears in the cluster Yandex search field within a frame → tap IME Enter → Yandex executes the search. No `am_anr` line in logcat, no `ANR in com.byd.dashcast`, process keeps the same pid throughout the session. (b) Back button tactile tested mid-typing — every press dismisses or closes immediately, no 5-18 s freeze. (c) After search complete, stop projection from MainActivity then relaunch Yandex on the cluster — mirror touch forwards correctly (process did not die, daemon Binder still connected). (d) Logcat — search for `ClusterImeWatcher: setTextOnCluster ACTION_SET_TEXT refused` (rare; would mean a Yandex-side rejection of `ACTION_SET_TEXT`, unrelated to this fix) or `ClusterImeWatcher: setTextOnCluster failed` (runtime exception inside the worker, also logged but never propagated to the caller). |

For touch (v1.2.7) we had to call `MotionEvent.setDisplayId(2)` so the InputDispatcher routes events from the composed face to the underlying task. For resize, no such trick is needed: `resizeTask(taskId, bounds)` operates in the coord system of the task's **current** display (i.e. display 3, 1920×1080) and the XDJA fission compositor handles the projection/scaling to display 2 at composition time. The bounds computation in `applyClusterFreeformBounds` already uses `Display.getRealSize()` for the task's display (display 3 → `1920×1080`, confirmed by `I ClusterService: cluster FREEFORM bounds=Rect(215, 164 - 1705, 916) display=3 1920×1080` in the same log). Path 2 (new on v1.2.13, triggered when path 1 returns nothing or only the caller's own task): if `BetaProxyClient.isConnected()`, call `BetaProxyClient.runShell("dumpsys activity recents")` (the proxy daemon runs uid 2000 = shell, has SHELL perms → dumpsys returns the full recent-tasks table cross-package) then parse with a fast-path regex `Task\\{[^}]*#(\\d+)[^}]*\\bA=<pkg>\\b` (matches the standard AOSP line `* Recent #0: Task{xxxxxxx #88 type=standard A=ru.yandex.yandexmaps U=0 ...`) and a fallback block-split scanner that accepts `realActivity=<pkg>/...` or `cmp=<pkg>/...` within the same `* Recent #N:` block (covers BYD ROM variants where the affinity field is rewritten). Returns the first match (most-recent task) or -1. The block parser is a static helper `parseTaskIdFromDumpsysRecents(String dump, String pkg)` so it's unit-testable in isolation. Every path emits a discrete log line (`(via AM)`, `(via daemon dumpsys recents)`, `not found in dumpsys recents (out.length=N)`, `daemon not connected; cannot fallback`) so future field logs immediately show which branch was hit. **(2) `resizeActiveTask` no longer silent on taskId<=0.** The early-return `if (taskId <= 0) return;` now emits `W ClusterService: resizeActiveTask: taskId<=0 for pkg=<pkg> — cannot resize (lookup via AM + daemon dumpsys both failed)` so a residual failure surfaces in the log instead of vanishing. **(3) Fix (a) — `MainActivity` skips `wm overscan` on DL5.** Both `btnResizeApply.onClick` and `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` now wrap the `wm overscan` shell with `if (AdbLocalClient.isDiLink5Safe(this)) { AppLogger.d(TAG, "… DL5: skipping wm overscan (cmd removed in API 30+) — resizeTask handles it"); }`. On DL3/DL2 the call is unchanged (DL3 still uses `wm overscan -d 1` which is supported on its API 29 ROM). **(4) Behavioural contract.** DL3 Seal EU production: `findRunningTaskId` path 1 succeeds (DashCast has effective GET_TASKS for its own package + sometimes BYD AUTO grants more), path 2 is never reached → byte-for-byte identical behaviour. `MainActivity` resize still sends `wm overscan -d 1` (DL3 API 29 accepts it) and `resizeTask` typed verb (BYD ROM exposes it). DL5 testeur: Apply now finds Yandex's taskId via the daemon dumpsys, `IActivityTaskManager.resizeTask(taskId, Rect(insetH, insetV, 1920-insetH, 1080-insetV), 1)` applies, the XDJA compositor reflects the new bounds on the composed display 2 face → visible resize. DL2: no cluster path, neither code change ever fires. **(5) Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (versionCode 203→204, versionName 1.2.12→1.2.13), `ClusterService.java` (`findRunningTaskId` rewritten to add daemon fallback + per-path logs ~50 LoC, new static `parseTaskIdFromDumpsysRecents(String, String)` helper ~40 LoC, `resizeActiveTask` early-return now logs ~5 LoC), `MainActivity.java` (both `wm overscan` call sites — `btnResizeApply` ~8 LoC and `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` ~8 LoC — gated by `isDiLink5Safe`). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings, no protocol bump (still `PROTOCOL_VERSION=2`). **(6) Validation plan.** (a) DL5 testeur — install v1.2.13 over v1.2.12, start cluster projection, launch Yandex Maps on cluster, open the Adjust panel, move W/H sliders, tap Apply. No more `Unknown command: overscan` lines (the `wm overscan` shell is skipped). (b) Auto-apply on launch — restart cluster projection, observe that `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` (called 500 ms after launch) produces the same successful resizeTask sequence. (c) DL3 Seal EU — install, navigate, resize sliders work exactly as before (path 1 still succeeds). (d) If the daemon is not running (rare edge case), the WARN `findRunningTaskId … daemon not connected; cannot fallback to dumpsys` surfaces in the log and resize quietly fails with the new `taskId<=0` WARN — both diagnostics visible, no crash. The smoking-gun line one frame after focus: `W InputMethodManager: Ignoring showSoftInput() as view=android.widget.EditText{… VFED..CL. .F....ID 0,0-1,1} is not served.` — the v1.2.11 1×1 EditText fails the IMM's "served view" check (size below threshold + `showSoftInput` posted before `onWindowFocusChanged` fired the InputConnection setup), so no `InputConnection` is established. The IME visually pops up (because `SOFT_INPUT_STATE_ALWAYS_VISIBLE` on the window is independent of the served view), but typed characters land in a null sink. **Second-order finding (cluster window asymmetry).** Even if v1.2.11 had been "served", the original v1.2.8 KeyEvent path was architecturally fragile on DL5: the cluster app's focused window lives on `mDisplayId=3` (1×1 shadow framebuffer where `containerservice` runs the XDJA fission compositor) while the composed face users actually see is on `displayId=2`. The touch path works because v1.2.7 calls `MotionEvent.setDisplayId(2)` AND the InputDispatcher routes from the composed face to the underlying window in the fission topology — but the symmetric `KeyEvent.setDisplayId(2)` route was never empirically validated, and may not work because the IME's `InputConnection` is bound to the served EditText on display 3, not to a global keyboard dispatcher. **(1) Pivot — replace KeyEvent injection with `AccessibilityNodeInfo.performAction(ACTION_SET_TEXT)`.** The a11y subsystem is cross-display by construction (TalkBack uses the same mechanism to read out content from any display) and is immune to per-display IME isolation or fission compositor asymmetry. New static helpers in `ClusterImeWatcherService`: **(a) `setTextOnCluster(CharSequence text)`** — walks `getWindows()`, picks the window whose `getDisplayId() > 0` (cluster, on API 30+ with `flagRetrieveInteractiveWindows`), falls back to any window on API < 30, retrieves `root.findFocus(FOCUS_INPUT)` per-call (no cached node — nodes from a11y are stale-prone, refresh per call), calls `node.performAction(ACTION_SET_TEXT, Bundle{ACTION_ARGUMENT_SET_TEXT_CHARSEQUENCE_VALUE: text})`. Returns boolean success. **(b) `performImeEnterOnCluster()`** — same node-discovery flow, then calls `AccessibilityAction.ACTION_IME_ENTER` (API 30+) or `ACTION_CLICK` fallback (API 28/29). **(c) Static `volatile ClusterImeWatcherService sInstance`** published in `onServiceConnected`, cleared in `onDestroy`, so the bridge activity can invoke the helpers without `bindService`. **(d) `AccessibilityServiceInfo.flags \|= FLAG_RETRIEVE_INTERACTIVE_WINDOWS`** added in `onServiceConnected` (mirrored in `accessibility_ime_watcher.xml` as `android:accessibilityFlags="flagRetrieveInteractiveWindows"`) — required so `getWindows()` returns the cluster windows in addition to the head-unit focused one. **(2) `KeyboardBridgeActivity` rewrite.** Window is now **220 dp × 48 dp** (vs 1×1 in v1.2.11) — large enough that the IMM accepts the EditText as a served view, but still transparent + pinned `Gravity.BOTTOM \| Gravity.END` so it doesn't obscure any cluster mirror UI. `showSoftInput` is now called from `onWindowFocusChanged(true)` instead of `onCreate.post(…)`, which removes the race that produced the "is not served" warning. The `TextWatcher.onTextChanged` body is reduced to a single line: `ClusterImeWatcherService.setTextOnCluster(s.toString())` — atomic, no per-character diff, no `KeyCharacterMap` translation, no `KeyEvent` sequencing. `OnEditorActionListener` calls `performImeEnterOnCluster()` then clears the local field. Removed: `KeyCharacterMap mKcm`, `CharSequence mLastText`, `boolean mSuppressWatcher`, `forwardKeyCode`, `forwardKeyEvent`, `mapAsciiToKeyCode`, all `ClusterInputForwarder.injectKeyEvent` calls from the bridge. **(3) Daemon path preserved (deprecated by bridge but still callable).** `MirrorDaemon.injectKey` + `sSetDisplayIdKey` reflection (added in v1.2.11) + `ClusterInputForwarder.injectKeyEvent` are NOT removed — they remain available for any future code path that needs raw `KeyEvent` injection (e.g. dedicated keyboard hardware on the cluster), and removing them would invalidate the v1.2.11 instrumentation in the daemon log. The bridge simply no longer calls them. **(4) Behavioural contract.** DL3 Seal EU production: `ClusterImeWatcherService.onAccessibilityEvent` early-returns on `!isDiLink5`, the bridge button is `View.GONE`, neither helper is ever invoked — zero behavioural change. DL5 testeur: user taps the cluster EditText → a11y service detects focus on `displayId > 0`, launches bridge → bridge appears as a 220×48dp invisible window → IMM serves the EditText → IME pops up → user types → each `onTextChanged` calls `setTextOnCluster` which performs `ACTION_SET_TEXT` on the cluster Yandex editable → text appears character-by-character (well, snapshot-by-snapshot) on the cluster Yandex search field → tap IME's Enter → `performImeEnterOnCluster` fires `ACTION_IME_ENTER` → Yandex executes the search. DL2: completely unaffected (a11y service inert, no cluster path). **(5) Why this fixes the symptom.** v1.2.11 failed because no `InputConnection` was established (1×1 EditText not served) → `TextWatcher` never fired. v1.2.12 fixes the served-view bug (220×48dp + `onWindowFocusChanged`-deferred `showSoftInput`) AND replaces the brittle `KeyEvent.setDisplayId(2)` cross-display routing (compositor-asymmetric, never validated) with the standard a11y `ACTION_SET_TEXT` (cross-display-native, used by every screen reader). **(6) Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (versionCode 202→203, versionName 1.2.11→1.2.12), `KeyboardBridgeActivity.java` (`onCreate` simplified to 220×48 window + EditText with `onWindowFocusChanged`-deferred showSoftInput, `TextWatcher` reduced to one-line `setTextOnCluster` call, `OnEditorActionListener` calls `performImeEnterOnCluster`, removed `KeyCharacterMap` + `forwardKeyCode/forwardKeyEvent/mapAsciiToKeyCode` + `ClusterInputForwarder` import — ~410 LoC → 200 LoC), `ime/ClusterImeWatcherService.java` (+`sInstance` static, +`FLAG_RETRIEVE_INTERACTIVE_WINDOWS` in `onServiceConnected`, +`setTextOnCluster(CharSequence)`, +`performImeEnterOnCluster()`, +`findClusterFocusedEditable()` private helper — ~140 LoC added), `res/xml/accessibility_ime_watcher.xml` (+`android:accessibilityFlags="flagRetrieveInteractiveWindows"`). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings, no protocol bump (`PROTOCOL_VERSION=2` unchanged). **(7) Validation plan.** (a) DL5 testeur — install v1.2.12 over v1.2.11 (no uninstall, versionCode bumped). Start cluster projection, launch Yandex Maps on cluster, tap the search field on the cluster (with a11y service enabled). Expected: IME pops up at the bottom of the head unit, NO `Ignoring showSoftInput() … is not served` warning in logcat (the bug from v1.2.11), each typed character causes the Yandex search field on the cluster to update with the cumulative text, tap IME's Enter → Yandex executes the search. (b) Logcat — search for `ClusterImeWatcher` tag, look for `setTextOnCluster ACTION_SET_TEXT refused` WARN: if present, the cluster editable rejected the action (rare, possible if Yandex's EditText is not exposing the `ACTION_SET_TEXT` capability — would require a per-app workaround). Search for `ClusterImeWatcher: findClusterFocusedEditable scan failed` ERROR: indicates a11y window enumeration failed (would require investigating `FLAG_RETRIEVE_INTERACTIVE_WINDOWS` propagation on this ROM). (c) DL3 Seal EU — install, navigate UI: ⌨ button remains GONE, no log lines from `ClusterImeWatcher` (early-return via `!isDiLink5`). (d) DL2 — install, navigate UI: zero impact. |
| **1.2.11-build202** | 202 | **DL5 — KeyboardBridge: invisible window + KeyEvent routed to cluster display (fixes "text typed on head unit IME doesn't reach cluster app" + removes the French overlay).** Two coupled field bugs reported after v1.2.10 install on DL5 testeur (Yandex Maps search, Russian system locale, screenshot received 23/05/2026): (a) tapping the ⌨ button (or auto-launch via the a11y service) showed an opaque French dialog at the top of the head-unit screen with title `Clavier déporté`, hint `Tapez ici : chaque caractère est envoyé au champ actif du cluster.`, plus three buttons (`Entrée` / `Retour` / `Fermer`) — visually wrong (always FR regardless of system locale, intrusive, covers part of the live cluster mirror), (b) characters typed on the system IME never reached the Yandex search field on the cluster (no character ever appeared in the Russian text input). Root cause analysis: **(a) Visible chrome.** `KeyboardBridgeActivity` was authored in v1.2.8 with a full LinearLayout (title TextView + hint TextView + visible EditText + 3-button row, semi-opaque dark background `0xCC202020`, MATCH_PARENT × WRAP_CONTENT pinned at the top of display 0). The visible chrome was purely cosmetic — the IME pops up regardless of EditText visibility — but the strings were never translated and the overlay was always present. **(b) Cluster doesn't receive keys.** `MirrorDaemon.injectKey(KeyEvent)` called `InputManager.injectInputEvent(kev, ASYNC)` WITHOUT first calling `KeyEvent.setDisplayId(sClusterDisplayId)`. Without the displayId override, the system InputDispatcher routes the key to the **globally focused window**, which on multi-display Android 12 is per-display — but our own `KeyboardBridgeActivity` on display 0 holds the head-unit focus, so it swallows every injected key (the EditText receives them, re-fires the TextWatcher, infinite ping-pong avoided only because TextWatcher diff sees no new chars). Cluster app on display 2 never sees a single KeyEvent. Symmetric pattern to v1.2.7's MotionEvent fix where `MotionEvent.setDisplayId(2)` was added — the touch path worked because of that, the key path was overlooked. **(1) Fix (b) — KeyEvent displayId routing.** `MirrorDaemon`: new static `sSetDisplayIdKey` Method cached via `KeyEvent.class.getDeclaredMethod("setDisplayId", int.class)` alongside the existing `sSetDisplayId` for MotionEvent. `injectKey(KeyEvent)` now calls `sSetDisplayIdKey.invoke(kev, sClusterDisplayId)` (with try/catch inner guard so a reflection failure still attempts the bare inject) BEFORE `InputManager.injectInputEvent`. First-event log line upgraded: `injectKey FIRST OK displayId=N setDisplayIdAvail=true/false keyCode=K action=A` (mirror of the existing motion log). `ClusterInputForwarder`: new instance `mSetDisplayIdKeyMethod` cached in constructor, applied in the direct-fallback path (used when daemon Binder is null). Both paths now route keys to the cluster display id (typically `2` on DL5 — same as touch). **(2) Fix (a) — invisible bridge window.** `KeyboardBridgeActivity.onCreate` rewritten: removed the entire LinearLayout + title + hint + 3-button row. Window is now `1×1` px pinned `BOTTOM \| END`, transparent background, dim 0. Sole content view is the focused EditText, stripped of every visible property (`setBackground(null)`, transparent text + hint colours, `setCursorVisible(false)`, zero padding). The IME still pops up automatically thanks to `SOFT_INPUT_STATE_ALWAYS_VISIBLE` + `mImm.showSoftInput` posted on the EditText. The 1×1 footprint means the rest of display 0 (DashCast UI, cluster mirror tile) stays interactive while the bridge is alive — touches outside the 1 px fall through to the windows below. Enter forwarding preserved via the existing `OnEditorActionListener` (IME's Done/Search/Send button → `KEYCODE_ENTER` injected to cluster). Back key naturally closes the activity (Android default). Unused imports (`View`, `Button`, `LinearLayout`) and unused helper (`dp(int)`) removed. The translated strings (`keyboard_bridge_title/hint/btn_enter/btn_back/btn_close/input_hint`) become dead resources — kept in `values/strings.xml` for the activity label in the manifest (`android:label=@string/keyboard_bridge_title`, but `excludeFromRecents=true` so the label is never user-visible). **(3) Behavioural contract.** DL3 Seal EU: bridge is never launched (the ⌨ button is gated `Platform.isDiLink5(this) ? VISIBLE : GONE`, the a11y service short-circuits on `!isDiLink5`) — the new code path is dormant by construction, zero observable change. DL5 testeur: tap the ⌨ button (or focus an EditText on the cluster with the a11y service enabled) → no visible overlay, IME pops up at the bottom of display 0, each typed character forwards to the cluster display via `setDisplayId(clusterId)` + daemon `injectKey` → cluster app's focused EditText receives the keys natively (no app-level integration needed). DL2: completely unaffected (no cluster, ⌨ button hidden, a11y service inert). **(4) Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (versionCode 201→202, versionName 1.2.10→1.2.11), `daemon/MirrorDaemon.java` (+`sSetDisplayIdKey` field, +`KeyEvent.class.getDeclaredMethod("setDisplayId")` in `initInputManager`, +`setDisplayIdKey.invoke(kev, sClusterDisplayId)` in `injectKey` with try/catch inner guard, +upgraded first-event log line — ~15 LoC net), `dashboard/ClusterInputForwarder.java` (+`mSetDisplayIdKeyMethod` field, cached in ctor, applied in direct-fallback path — ~10 LoC), `KeyboardBridgeActivity.java` (entire `onCreate` body replaced ~70 LoC → ~50 LoC, all visible chrome stripped, imports cleaned, `dp(int)` helper removed). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings/layouts, no protocol bump (`PROTOCOL_VERSION=2` unchanged). **(5) Validation plan.** (a) DL5 testeur — install v1.2.11 over v1.2.10 (no uninstall, versionCode bumped). Start cluster projection, launch Yandex Maps on the cluster, tap the search field on the cluster (with a11y service enabled) OR tap the ⌨ button manually. Expected: NO French overlay at the top, only the IME keyboard at the bottom of display 0; type any text → characters appear immediately in the Yandex search field on the cluster; tap IME's Enter (or its hardware equivalent) → KEYCODE_ENTER reaches Yandex (triggers search). (b) Logcat — search for `MirrorDaemon` tag, look for `injectKey FIRST OK displayId=2 setDisplayIdAvail=true keyCode=29 action=0` (the first character typed): this is the smoking-gun that the displayId override took effect. If `setDisplayIdAvail=false` → reflection failed on this ROM, falls back to default routing (cluster won't receive). (c) DL3 Seal EU — install, navigate UI: ⌨ button must remain GONE, no log lines from `KeyboardBridge`, `ClusterImeWatcherService` post-`!isDiLink5` lines absent. (d) DL2 — install, navigate UI: zero impact (no cluster path triggered). |
| **1.2.10-build201** | 201 | **DL5 IME a11y banner — one-click activation via daemon shell + translations for all 11 locales (en, de, es, it, ar, be, kk, ru, tr, uk, uz).** Field report (23/05/2026, post-v1.2.9 install on DL5 testeur in non-FR locale): (a) the IME accessibility onboarding banner introduced in v1.2.8 was hardcoded in French only — every non-FR locale fell back to FR strings; (b) the « Activer » button appeared to do nothing when tapped multiple times. Root cause analysis: **(a) Missing translations.** The 8 IME-related string keys (`a11y_ime_watcher_label/summary/description` + `ime_banner_title/body/btn_enable/btn_later/btn_dismiss/toast_enabled`) only existed in `values/strings.xml` (default = French) — none of the 11 sibling locale folders (`values-en`, `values-de`, `values-es`, `values-it`, `values-ar`, `values-be`, `values-kk`, `values-ru`, `values-tr`, `values-uk`, `values-uz`) had these keys, so Android's resource resolver fell back to the default FR strings on any non-FR system locale. **(b) Enable button silently failing.** The button fired `Intent(Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS)` then `startActivity(i)` wrapped in a `Throwable` catch that only logged the error to AppLogger — user saw no visual feedback whatsoever. On BYD AUTO ROM the standard AOSP `ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS` activity may not be advertised (BYD ships a heavily customised Settings.apk on Android 10/12) so the intent silently failed to resolve, the `Throwable` was caught, the AppLogger line went unnoticed, and nothing visible happened on tap. **(1) Fix (a) — `enableImeA11yServiceOneClick(card, btnEnable)` — direct activation via shell.** The button now skips the Settings UI entirely and uses the proxy daemon's shell (uid=2000, same uid as `adb shell`) to write directly to `Settings.Secure.ENABLED_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICES` via the `settings` binary. Single-line POSIX `sh -c` script: read current colon-separated service list → if empty or `null` write our component standalone → if already contains our component leave unchanged → else append `:` + our component → write back via `settings put secure enabled_accessibility_services` → flip `settings put secure accessibility_enabled 1` master flag → `echo OUT=$(settings get secure enabled_accessibility_services)` for verification. Posted through `ShellGateway.execShellWithResult` (already serialises to either the proxy daemon or the legacy `AdbLocalClient` fallback chain). On `onSuccess`: re-read the OS state via `ClusterImeWatcherService.isEnabled(this)` (trust the OS, not the shell echo), if `true` → toast `R.string.ime_banner_toast_enabled` + hide banner card; if shell returned OK but the OS still reports disabled (rare, possible if `settings` exists but `accessibility_enabled` is gated by a SELinux policy on this ROM) → fall back to the Settings UI chain. On `onError` (no daemon, no legacy fallback, ROM blocks `settings put`) → also fall back to the Settings UI chain. **(2) Fix (b) — `openA11ySettingsFallback()` — three-tier Settings opener.** When the shell route fails, try three intents in sequence, each wrapped in its own `Throwable` catch: (i) standard `Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS` (works on stock AOSP), (ii) direct ComponentName `com.android.settings/.Settings$AccessibilitySettingsActivity` (BYD ROM may not advertise the standard action but still ship the activity), (iii) generic `Settings.ACTION_SETTINGS` (last resort, user manually navigates to Accessibility). If all three fail → toast `R.string.ime_banner_toast_cannot_open_settings` (« Cannot open Settings — please enable the accessibility service manually ») so the user finally gets visible feedback. Button is re-enabled on every code path so a failure doesn't leave it stuck disabled. **(3) Translations.** Added the 10 IME banner string keys (8 original + 1 new `ime_banner_toast_cannot_open_settings` + the FR default also gained the new toast key) to ALL 11 locale folders. Each translation hand-crafted with proper grammar, locale-specific keyboard / cluster terminology, and escaped apostrophes (`\'`). Locales covered: English, German, Spanish, Italian, Russian, Ukrainian, Belarusian, Turkish, Arabic, Kazakh, Uzbek. **(4) Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (versionCode 200→201, versionName 1.2.9→1.2.10), `MainActivity.java` (replace Enable click handler ~5 LoC, new `enableImeA11yServiceOneClick` ~70 LoC, new `openA11ySettingsFallback` ~40 LoC), `res/values/strings.xml` (+1 new key `ime_banner_toast_cannot_open_settings`), `res/values-{en,de,es,it,ar,be,kk,ru,tr,uk,uz}/strings.xml` (+10 keys each = 110 total). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no protocol bump (still `PROTOCOL_VERSION=2`), no new layouts, no behavioural change on DL3 / DL2 (banner is `Platform.isDiLink5`-gated). **(5) Behavioural contract.** DL3 Seal EU production: banner is never shown (`isDiLink5=false`), the new shell helper code path is never invoked, the new fallback chain is never invoked — zero behavioural change. DL5 testeur: tap « Enable/Activate/Aktivieren/Aktiva/Attiva/etc. » → if proxy daemon is reachable (post-bootstrap) → service is enabled in <500 ms with no Settings trip required, banner disappears, toast confirms; if daemon down → falls back transparently to the Settings UI. | **1.2.9-build200** | 200 | **DL3/DL5 bug-fixes — long-press Kill no longer leaves a ghost Recents tile, and Stop Projection no longer relaunches the cluster app fullscreen on the head unit (best-effort patch ; the robust fix via typed verb `removeTaskByPackage` is roadmapped for v1.3.x in `docs/PLAN_v1.3.x_FULL_PROXY.md` §7.b).** Field report (23/05/2026, DL3 Seal EU production, also expected on DL5) surfaced two distinct symptoms sharing the same root cause — BYD AUTO ROM's `am force-stop` / `IActivityManager.forceStopPackage` kill the **process** but **do not** remove the `TaskRecord` from the Recents stack, and the existing dumpsys-based cleanup fallback (`dumpsys activity recents \| grep \| sed → am task remove → TaskRemover app_process`) silently fails on this ROM (uid 2000 lacks `REMOVE_TASKS`, `am task remove` not always available). **(Bug 1 — long-press Kill leaves a ghost Recents tile.)** Long-press on a cluster-active app → `Kill` → process dies BUT the Recents tile remains and tapping it relaunches the activity. Root cause #1: in `MainActivity.doKillApp`, the move-back-to-display-0 (`mClusterService.moveTaskToDisplay(pkg, 0, null)`) and the subsequent `AdbLocalClient.forceStopApp(...)` were fired on different threads in close succession — `forceStopApp`'s internal `dumpsys activity recents \| grep \| sed` could parse the recents stack DURING the move, see a stale or already-moved TaskId, and `am task remove` would target the wrong (or no longer existing) task. **Fix (a) — serialize move→forceStop via `LaunchCallback`.** `mClusterService.moveTaskToDisplay(pkg, 0, callback)` already supports `LaunchCallback.onResult(boolean)` fired on the main thread (added in v1.1.x). `doKillApp` now extracts the `forceStopApp` callback as a final `killCallback` local, then in the cluster-app branch wraps the move with a `LaunchCallback` that fires `mSessionClusterPackages.remove + persistSessionClusterPackages + AdbLocalClient.forceStopApp(this, pkg, killCallback)` inside `onResult` — guaranteeing the dumpsys-recents scan inside `forceStopApp` runs AFTER the move-to-display-0 has fully completed. The non-cluster branch (else) is unchanged shape but routes through the same extracted `killCallback`. **(Bug 2 — Stop Projection relaunches the cluster app fullscreen on the head unit.)** Stop Projection while Waze (or any cluster app) is active → cluster restored OK, but ~1 s later Waze pops up fullscreen on display 0 (head unit) covering the BYD launcher. Root cause #2: in `MainActivity.restoreBydDashboard` (TEST 10 path) and `originCluster`, the call order was `moveSessionAppsToMainDisplay() → AdbLocalClient.restoreBydOnCluster(...)`. The first call brings every session cluster-launched app (including the active `capturedClusterPkg`) to display 0 as a foreground task. Then `restoreBydOnCluster` force-stops `capturedClusterPkg` — kills the process, but the TaskRecord on display 0 survives. Then `sendInfo(18)+sendInfo(0)` triggers the Qt cluster surface restoration which appears to fire a `RESUME_TOP_ACTIVITY` ripple effect on display 0 that picks up the orphan TaskRecord and resurrects the activity. **Fix (b) — invert order : remove `capturedClusterPkg` from the session set BEFORE `moveSessionAppsToMainDisplay()`.** Now `moveSessionAppsToMainDisplay()` iterates a set that no longer contains the active cluster pkg — it stays on display 1 (cluster). `restoreBydOnCluster` (unchanged) then force-stops it IN PLACE on display 1 just before `sendInfo(18)`. No orphan TaskRecord lands on display 0, no resurrection path. Other session apps (split-mode side dashboards) are still moved to display 0 as before. Same surgical edit applied to `originCluster` (handles the original-cluster restore path used when the user hasn't explicitly chosen "BYD cluster"). **Fix (c) — `verifyForceStop` active escalation (defense in depth, AdbLocalClient).** v1.2.8's `verifyForceStop` only logged a WARN if `BetaProxyClient.getPidsByPackage(pkg)` reported surviving PIDs after the typed `IActivityManager.forceStopPackage` returned ok. Build 200 actively escalates: surviving PIDs → `BetaProxyClient.runShell("kill -9 <pid1 pid2 ...>")` via the daemon (uid 2000 owns the same uid as the target app so the kill is permitted), sleep 200 ms, re-query PIDs, log the final state. The whole escalation is wrapped in an inner try/catch so a daemon glitch never breaks the teardown sequence. Also benefits `restoreBydOnCluster` / `restoreOriginCluster` (typed paths call `verifyForceStop`) — so if the cluster pkg somehow survives the in-place force-stop in Fix (b), the kill -9 escalation finishes the job before `sendInfo(18)` fires. **(Limit / roadmap.)** This v1.2.9 patch fixes the **process-survival** and **race-window** sides of both bugs but does **not** purge the `TaskRecord` from Recents (architectural limitation of the dumpsys+shell pipeline on BYD AUTO ROM). On DL3 the ghost Recents tile in Bug 1 may still persist visually after Kill (tapping it would now relaunch a fresh instance instead of resurrecting state) — but Bug 2's user-visible symptom (Waze fullscreen on the head unit) is fully resolved by Fix (b). The robust fix for the TaskRecord purge is a new typed verb `TXN_REMOVE_TASKS_BY_PACKAGE` in the proxy daemon (uses `IActivityTaskManager.getRecentTasks() + removeTask(taskId)` from the stable uid 2000 daemon context, atomic, no shell parse), specced in `docs/PLAN_v1.3.x_FULL_PROXY.md` §7.b — requires the daemon `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump 2→3 and is scheduled for v1.3.x. **(Touched files.)** `app/build.gradle` (versionCode 199→200, versionName 1.2.8→1.2.9), `MainActivity.java` (`doKillApp` serialize move→kill via LaunchCallback ~50 LoC, `restoreBydDashboard` + `originCluster` pre-remove captured pkg from session set ~14 LoC total), `AdbLocalClient.java` (`verifyForceStop` active kill -9 escalation ~40 LoC), `docs/PLAN_v1.3.x_FULL_PROXY.md` (new §7.b describing the typed-verb robust fix). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings, no protocol bump (still PROTOCOL_VERSION=2). DL3 / DL5 / DL2 behavioural contract identical except for the targeted bug fixes (DL2 has no cluster path so only verifyForceStop escalation can trigger there, but its `Platform.isDiLink2` ROM doesn't ship the typed verbs so the new code path is naturally inert). **(Validation plan.)** (a) DL3 Seal EU — install v1.2.9, start cluster projection, launch Waze on cluster, long-press Waze tile → Kill → confirm process dies (Waze gone from cluster). Open Recents on head unit: tile may still appear (known TaskRecord limitation, roadmap v1.3.x) but tapping it MUST relaunch Waze fresh (not resurrect with prior state on display 1) — proves the move-then-kill race is gone. (b) DL3 Seal EU — start cluster projection, launch Waze on cluster, tap Stop Projection → confirm cluster restored to BYD dashboard AND **NO Waze flash on head unit** (was: Waze popped fullscreen ~1 s after restore) → Fix (b) validated. Logcat tag `MainActivity` should show `restoreBydDashboard ... mSessionClusterPackages remove com.waze` BEFORE any `moveSessionAppsToMainDisplay` line. (c) DL5 testeur — same two scenarios, same expected results (shared MainActivity code path). (d) DL2 — install, navigate UI, no cluster path triggered, zero behaviour change. (e) Logcat tag `AdbLocalClient` — search for `verifyForceStop ... escalating kill -9`: should ONLY appear when force-stop typed verb didn't fully kill a process (rare on healthy device), confirming the escalation is dormant in nominal operation. |
| **1.2.8-build199** | 199 | **DL5 — keyboard bridge (manual + auto), accessibility on-boarding banner, resize sliders dynamic cluster routing, and head-unit display-0 hard guards (defense in depth).** Four coordinated DL5 payloads built on top of the v1.2.7 tactile fix, all gated behind `Platform.isDiLink5(Context)` so the DL3 production hot path is bit-for-bit identical. **(A) Keyboard bridge — manual ⌨ button + `KeyboardBridgeActivity`.** Field report (22/05/2026, post-v1.2.7): tactile injection on the DL5 cluster mirror now works (Yandex maps reacts to taps), BUT tapping an EditText inside Yandex does NOT pop up the system IME on the head unit. Root cause: WindowManager per-display IME isolation routes the focused window's IME request to display 3 (the 1×1 `shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection` shadow framebuffer) where it has nowhere to render — no clean override exists on API 32 without `MANAGE_DEVICE_POLICY_LOCAL_OVERRIDE` (System API unavailable on BYD-AUTO ROM). **Workaround design**: a translucent landscape `KeyboardBridgeActivity` is launched on display 0 (head unit) with a hidden `EditText` that triggers the native IME on tap; a `TextWatcher` computes the per-character diff and forwards each typed character to the cluster via `ClusterInputForwarder.injectKeyEvent(KeyEvent)` which already routes through the existing `TRANSACT_INJECT_KEY` daemon transaction (uid 2000, holds `INJECT_EVENTS`). `KeyCharacterMap.load(VIRTUAL_KEYBOARD).getEvents(char[])` is used to convert each typed character into a properly meta-state-aware `KeyEvent[]` sequence (handles SHIFT for capital letters and ALT for symbols correctly); a fallback `mapAsciiToKeyCode` covers degenerate ASCII when the KCM lookup fails. Backspace is translated to N×`KEYCODE_DEL` based on the `before` count from `TextWatcher`. The EditText is reset to empty after 80 characters to prevent unbounded growth. A new ⌨ button (`btn_keyboard_bridge`, 36 dp, tertiary-container colours) is inserted in the toolbar right after the existing Split-screen button, visibility-gated to DL5 only (`Platform.isDiLink5 → VISIBLE`, else `GONE`). **(B) Auto-popup via `ClusterImeWatcherService` (AccessibilityService).** Manual ⌨ taps work but the user-experience target is automatic pop-up the moment an EditText gains focus on the cluster. Android does not expose an in-process focus listener for foreign apps' windows, so the only platform-supported path is an AccessibilityService. New `com.byd.dashcast.ime.ClusterImeWatcherService` listens for `TYPE_VIEW_FOCUSED \| TYPE_WINDOW_STATE_CHANGED` events (`feedbackGeneric`, `notificationTimeout=100`, `canRetrieveWindowContent=true`, declared in new `res/xml/accessibility_ime_watcher.xml`, registered in the manifest with `BIND_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICE` and the `AccessibilityService` intent-filter). On every event the service runs a **five-guard filter chain** before doing anything: (1) `!Platform.isDiLink5(this) → return` (DL3 strictly never triggers — refreshed on every event in case the platform flag flips at runtime), (2) `eventType != TYPE_VIEW_FOCUSED → return`, (3) `!ClusterService.sIsRunning → return` (hard gate: only react while the DashCast cluster projection is actually live — if DashCast is closed the service is registered with the system but does NOTHING), (4) `event.getDisplayId() <= 0 → return` (strictly secondary displays only, blocks display 0 and the unknown-id (-1) case on pre-API-30 fallbacks), (5) `node not editable \|\| pkg == getPackageName() → return` (no self-trigger on the bridge's own EditText). Additional safety: 600 ms debounce via `mLastLaunchAt`, and `KeyboardBridgeActivity.isShowing()` static check (set in `onStart`/`onStop`/`onDestroy`) to skip launch when the bridge is already visible. Every callback path is wrapped in `try { … } catch (Throwable t) { AppLogger.e(…); }` — the service never crashes the host app. The launch sends an intent with `EXTRA_AUTO_OPENED=true` so the bridge can distinguish manual vs. auto invocations if needed in future builds. **(C) On-boarding banner for the AccessibilityService.** Android requires the user to enable an AccessibilityService manually in Settings (no programmatic enable possible by design). A new `MaterialCardView card_ime_a11y_banner` (tertiary-container, corner 12 dp, elevation 0, hidden by default) is inserted in `MainActivity` right after the top bar; it shows on first launch when (a) `Platform.isDiLink5(this)`, (b) the service is not yet in `Settings.Secure.ENABLED_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICES` (parsed colon-separated, comparing both `flattenToString` and `flattenToShortString`), AND (c) the user has not dismissed it (`PREF_IME_BANNER_DISMISSED`). Three actions: **Enable** (deep-links to `Settings.ACTION_ACCESSIBILITY_SETTINGS` for the user to flip the toggle, then Toast `Clavier automatique actif ✓` is shown on return to `MainActivity` if enabled), **Later** (session-hide only, banner reappears next launch), **Dismiss** (persists `PREF_IME_BANNER_DISMISSED=true`, banner never shown again unless prefs cleared). The banner is fully no-op on DL3 (`Platform.isDiLink5=false` → never shown). All MainActivity wiring is wrapped in `try { … } catch (Throwable t) { AppLogger.e(…); }`. **(D) DL5 resize sliders — dynamic cluster routing.** Field finding: the in-app per-app resize panel (`btn_toggle_resize` → SeekBars W/H → Apply) was a no-op on DL5. Two hard-coded bugs: (i) `MainActivity.btnResizeApply` and `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` both issued `wm overscan W,H,W,H -d 1` (cluster id on DL3 only — on DL5 the cluster lives on display 3 or 4, so the command silently failed), (ii) `ClusterService.resizeActiveTask` hard-coded the freeform `Rect` to `1920 - insetH, 720 - insetV` (DL3 cluster size — on DL5 the composed cluster face may differ). Fix: both MainActivity call sites now resolve the cluster display id dynamically via `ClusterService.getDisplayId()` (which delegates to `DashboardDisplayHelper.getKnownClusterDisplayId()`, the same source already used by every other cluster-routed code path in the app), and skip the `wm overscan` call entirely when `clusterId <= 0` (no cluster connected — never silently retarget to display 0). `ClusterService.resizeActiveTask` and the inline post-`moveTaskToDisplay` resize block now both compute bounds from `mInputForwarder.getClusterWidth()/getClusterHeight()` (set live from `Display.getSize()` in `setClusterDisplay`), with explicit fallback to `1920×720` if either getter returns 0 — DL3 behaviour stays bit-for-bit identical (DL3 cluster IS 1920×720, fallback path even matches the old hard-coded values). On DL5 the bounds now reflect the actual cluster dimensions reported by the system. **(E) Head-unit display-0 hard guards (defense in depth).** Concern raised: even with the per-call-site `clusterId > 0` checks in (D), a single regressed caller (future refactor, off-path code, hand-typed shell command via the dev console) could still ship a `wm overscan\|size\|density … -d 0` to the head-unit display 0 and shrink the main UI (the v1.2.5 DL2 incident playbook). Three new layers, all independent: **(E1)** New `ShellGateway.WM_DISPLAY_ZERO` regex (`^\s*wm\s+(?:overscan\|size\|density)\b.*\s-d\s+0\b.*$`) blocks any `wm` verb explicitly targeting display 0, checked at the top of `execShellWithResult` BEFORE both the proxy-ON typed-verb path AND the legacy `AdbLocalClient` fallback — neither can reach the system. Blocked commands emit `AppLogger.e("BLOCKED wm verb on display 0 (head unit): …")` and the caller's `Callback.onError("blocked: wm command targets display 0 (head unit)")`. **(E2)** New abort in `ClusterService.resizeActiveTask`: if `mDisplayHelper.getKnownClusterDisplayId() <= 0` at call time, log `resizeActiveTask aborted: no cluster display connected (taskId=… pkg=…)` and return without invoking `IActivityTaskManager.resizeTask`. Without this, a stale `taskId` that happens to live on display 0 (because a prior cluster-move failed silently) would have applied DL3 freeform bounds to the head-unit task. **(E3)** The two existing `MainActivity` `clusterId > 0` guards from (D) form the outermost layer at the UI entry points. Coverage matrix: to reach display 0 a caller would have to simultaneously bypass the UI-level guard, the cluster-connected guard in `resizeActiveTask`, AND the regex at the shell gateway — three independent layers, no single point of failure. DL3 zero behaviour change: `clusterId` is always `1`, the regex never matches `-d 1`, the `getKnownClusterDisplayId` abort never fires in nominal operation (cluster is connected before any resize is invoked). **(F) Behavioural contract.** DL3 production (BYD Seal EU, daily-driver reference): (a) ⌨ button is `GONE`, AccessibilityService is registered with the system but on-event-check `!isDiLink5 → return` short-circuits before any work — no behavioural change of the IME. (b) Banner is hidden (`isDiLink5=false`). (c) Resize sliders issue `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 1` (resolved from `getDisplayId()`, identical to the old hard-coded value), `resizeTask` bounds computed as `1920 - insetH, 720 - insetV` (identical to old hard-coded values via the `getClusterWidth/Height` fallback). (d) Display-0 guards are no-ops (never triggered, cluster id is 1 on DL3). DL5 testeur: (a) ⌨ button visible, manual tap → bridge appears on head unit, typing forwards to Yandex/Maps/anything on cluster. (b) Once the user enables the AccessibilityService in Settings, focusing any EditText on the cluster auto-launches the bridge within ~100 ms. (c) Banner walks the user through enabling the service on first launch. (d) Sliders now actually resize the cluster app (was a silent no-op before). (e) Display-0 protections are a strict superset of the v1.2.5 DL2 hard-ban — same defense-in-depth design. DL2: completely unaffected — DashCast cluster surface never runs on DL2, the AccessibilityService still checks `isDiLink5` first so it stays inert if accidentally enabled. **(G) Touched files.** `MyBYDApp/app/build.gradle` (versionCode 196→199, versionName 1.2.7→1.2.8). NEW: `KeyboardBridgeActivity.java`, `ime/ClusterImeWatcherService.java`, `res/xml/accessibility_ime_watcher.xml`. MODIFIED: `AndroidManifest.xml` (+2 component registrations), `MainActivity.java` (+keyboard button + banner + dynamic cluster id in 2 resize sites), `ClusterService.java` (+`getInstance()` + `clusterId <= 0` abort in `resizeActiveTask` + dynamic `cw/ch` bounds), `dashboard/ClusterInputForwarder.java` (+public `injectKeyEvent(KeyEvent)`), `beta/ShellGateway.java` (+`WM_DISPLAY_ZERO` regex + guard), `res/layout/activity_main.xml` (+keyboard button + banner card), `res/values/strings.xml` (+13 string keys, FR fallback for other locales pending). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no protocol bump (PROTOCOL_VERSION=2 unchanged). **(H) Validation plan.** (a) DL5 testeur — install, start cluster projection, open Yandex on cluster: (a1) tap ⌨ → bridge appears on head unit, typing forwards each character to Yandex search bar. (a2) Enable AccessibilityService in Settings, tap Yandex search bar → bridge auto-launches within ~100 ms. (a3) Adjust → slide W/H → Apply: Yandex window on cluster recomputes bounds (head unit unaffected — visual proof display 0 is untouched).
