| **1.2.6-build195** | 195 | **Two coordinated payloads — (A) Beta Engine proxy daemon perf passes P1-P4 + (B) DL5 mirror investigation (M10 fix, daemon logging, layerStack override).** **(A) Beta Engine proxy daemon — 4 perf passes (P1-P4), zero behaviour change, lock-free hot paths.** Audit of the production proxy daemon (`ProxyDaemonMain` 352 LoC + `Phase4Verbs` 287 LoC + `BetaProxyClient` 576 LoC + `ShellGateway` 208 LoC) surfaced four micro-perf wins that compound on the cluster-projection hot paths without touching the wire protocol, the bootstrap script, the reflection cache, or any business logic. Production semantics preserved bit-for-bit on DL3 (BYD Seal EU production reference) and DL5 (BYD-AUTO testeur): no API removed, no return value changed, no callback signature changed, no `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump. **(P1) Lock-free hot-path verbs in `BetaProxyClient`.** Field `sBinder` promoted to `volatile`; `synchronized (LOCK)` removed from `setOverscan`, `getPidsByPackage`, `autoContainerSendInfo`, `forceStopPackage`, `runShell`, `runPhase4Probes`, `ping`. Volatile `sDaemonUid` / `sDaemonPid` / `sDaemonVer` make the public getters (`getCallerUid`, `getDaemonPid`, `getProtocolVersion`) lockless too. `connect()`, the receiver and `handshake()` still take `LOCK` for sBinder publication — the only place it was strictly needed. **Why safe.** `IBinder.transact()` is documented thread-safe (it dispatches into the daemon's binder thread pool which already serialises per-transaction); `Parcel.obtain()` returns from a thread-safe pool; the only shared mutable state read by the hot paths is `sBinder` itself which is now correctly published via `volatile`. The `sBinder = null` cleanup on `RemoteException` is preserved in every verb (single volatile assignment, race-free) so the fallback contract in `ShellGateway` (typed-verb fail → generic `runShell` → legacy `AdbLocalClient`) is bit-for-bit identical. **Why it matters.** The resize SeekBar in Settings fires ~30 `setOverscan` per second while dragging; the reconcile loop in `MainActivity` fires `getPidsByPackage` every ~5 s during a projection session. Under the old serial mutex, they queued behind any in-flight `runShell` (e.g. an `am start` that takes 500 ms+), which manifested as visible UI lag on the slider and stale state on the reconcile tick. Lockless verbs let them dispatch in parallel through the daemon's binder pool. **(P2) `DeathRecipient` hooked on the live binder → `isBinderAlive()` instead of `pingBinder()` in `isConnected()`.** The old `isConnected()` performed a full Binder IPC roundtrip on every call (`pingBinder()` is *not* a local flag check — it issues `IBinder.PING_TRANSACTION` to the remote process, ~5 ms on this hardware). Every typed verb routes through `isConnected()` first via `ShellGateway`, so each verb cost two IPC: one to ping, one to transact. Build 195 hooks `sBinder.linkToDeath(sDeath, 0)` in the broadcast receiver right after the live binder arrives; the static `DeathRecipient` clears `sBinder` (under `LOCK`) as soon as the kernel notifies us of the daemon's death. Now `isConnected()` can rely on the cheap local `isBinderAlive()` flag instead. Per-verb latency drops from ~10 ms to ~5 ms (×2). Receiver also unhooks the previous death recipient before swapping in a fresh binder, and best-effort-catches `RemoteException` from `linkToDeath` if the binder died between `pingBinder` (stale-broadcast check) and `linkToDeath` (vanishingly unlikely race). The `pingBinder` call in the receiver — used to discard stale broadcasts whose binder is already dead — is kept, because `isBinderAlive()` on a never-pinged binder returns true unconditionally and would let stale references through. **Why safe.** `DeathRecipient` is a pure additive hook with no observable side-effect when the daemon stays alive; if `linkToDeath` ever fails, the next typed verb's `isBinderAlive()` still returns the right answer (false post-death), exactly like the pre-P2 `pingBinder()`-on-every-call behaviour. **(P3) `ThreadLocal<byte[]>` reusable scratch in `Phase4Verbs.getPidsByPackage`.** Previously every call allocated a fresh `byte[256]` per `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` scan iteration — ~241 allocations per scan on a typical BYD device with 241 live processes. The daemon's Binder thread pool consists of a small set of long-lived threads, so a `ThreadLocal` yields one buffer per thread for the daemon's lifetime: zero allocation per scan, zero GC pressure on the reconcile tick. The loop body and matching semantics are byte-for-byte unchanged. **Why safe.** ThreadLocal scoping matches the binder pool's thread reuse pattern; the buffer is overwritten on every `FileInputStream.read(buf)` so cross-iteration leak is impossible. **(P4) `ByteArrayOutputStream` in `ProxyDaemonMain.runShell`.** Replaces the per-line `BufferedReader.readLine()` + `StringBuilder.append('\n').append(line)` pattern. The legacy code stripped trailing newlines via `readLine()` then re-added separators (~3-4 `String` allocations per line + 1 `StringBuilder` grow); on a 300-line `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger` that was ~1000 transient allocations. P4 reads the full child-process stream in 4 KiB chunks into a single `ByteArrayOutputStream` then decodes once with `toString("UTF-8")`. Trailing `\r`/`\n` are stripped after decode to **exactly reproduce** the legacy line-joiner semantics (which never appended a final `\n`) — verified character-by-character against the old code path. **Why safe.** Same UTF-8 decoding (Android default charset), same trailing-newline handling, same `redirectErrorStream(true)` capture order, same `ExecResult.exit` from `p.waitFor()`. The byte-stream pipeline is the same one used by `Process.getInputStream()` internally; the old `InputStreamReader` was just adding a decode-per-line layer on top. **(5) Wire protocol.** `PROTOCOL_VERSION` *unchanged* (still `"2"`). All TXN codes unchanged. Parcel layout per-TXN unchanged. An app-update that lands while a daemon from build 192-194 is still alive will transparently talk to the old daemon until the next bootstrap kills it (`ps -A \| grep '[d]ashcast_proxy' \| kill -9`). No incompat possible. **(6) Touched files.** `app/build.gradle` (versionCode 194→195, versionName 1.2.5→1.2.6), `beta/BetaProxyClient.java` (volatile fields, `DeathRecipient`, `isBinderAlive()` in `isConnected()`, lockless verbs + getters — ~45 LoC modified, no public API change), `beta/proxy/Phase4Verbs.java` (`ThreadLocal<byte[]>` scratch — +6 LoC), `beta/proxy/ProxyDaemonMain.java` (`runShell` rewritten with `ByteArrayOutputStream` — ~20 LoC modified), `CHANGELOG.md`. No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new layouts, no new strings. **(7) Behavioural contract.** DL3 production: byte-for-byte identical sequence of binder transactions and shell outputs as build 194 (we don't change *what* runs, only *how* the dispatcher / parser work). DL5 testeur: same. DL2: completely unaffected (Beta Engine is disabled by default and any production resize is hard-blocked by the build-191 DL2 guard which sits *before* `ShellGateway`). M10 LIVE probe from build 194 still passes through `AdbLocalClient` (not `BetaProxyClient`), so its evidence shape is unchanged. **(8) Validation plan.** (a) Install on DL3 Seal EU — launch DashCast, open Settings, drag the overscan SeekBar smoothly from 0 to 80 to 0: expect (i) zero visible lag (P1+P2 → ~2× faster per-tick), (ii) per-tick logcat tag `BetaProxyClient` shows `setOverscan typed ok (~3-5 ms)` instead of 8-12 ms in build 194. Start projection, confirm cluster lights up identically to build 194 (no visual regression), force-stop projection, confirm restore identical. (b) Install on DL5 testeur — launch DashCast, open Diag → Mirror tab, run `Lancer tous`: expect identical M1-M10 output as build 194 (zero regression). Then enable Beta Engine in Settings → run the DL5 D-suite: identical PASS/FAIL pattern as build 194. (c) Install on DL2 testeur — launch DashCast, navigate UI, ensure Settings `Marges` card still hidden, no `DL2 BLOCK` spurious lines in logcat. (d) Optional micro-bench in Diag → Logs: `BetaProxyClient.ping()` reported latency should be the same as build 194 (this is an explicit IPC ping, unaffected by P1/P2). Done. **(B) DL5 mirror investigation — 3 surgical fixes for the DL5 black-preview symptom (gated on `Platform.isDiLink5`, DL3 strictly untouched).** First DL5 in-car test of build 192-194 mirror path (user log `byd_report_20260522_200736.log`, 1230 lines) confirmed end-to-end routing works: `auto_container sendInfo(1000,16)` opens the cluster projection tunnel and `am start --display 3 ru.yandex.yandexmaps` lands Yandex on the cluster display (M10 STEP 6: `mDisplayId=3 rootTaskId=50 mSession=Session{... 17239:u0a10124}`), AND the app-side mirror logs `startMirrorViaDaemon ✓ layerStack=3 1920×720 displayId=3` — yet the in-app preview surface stays black. Root-cause hypothesis from the SF dump (M5): DL5 fission architecture has THREE virtual displays for the cluster — `shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_0` (layerStack=3, framebufferSpace bounds `1×1`), `shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_1` (layerStack=4, framebufferSpace bounds `1×1`), and `fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection` (layerStack=2, framebufferSpace bounds `1920×720`). Apps render on layerStack 3/4 (shadow displays with degenerate 1×1 framebuffer used as logical render targets), then `com.byd.containerservice` re-composes the selected slot into layerStack=2 which is the actual 1920×720 surface piped to the physical cluster panel. Build 195's app-side and daemon-side mirror was capturing layerStack=3 because that's what `Display.getLayerStack()` returns for the DisplayManager id we're given — resulting in a 1×1 black buffer in the TextureView. **(B1) DL5 layerStack override 3→2 (and 4→2) in `ClusterMirrorManager`.** New private helper `applyDl5LayerStackOverride(Context ctx, int detectedLayerStack)` wraps `Platform.get().isDiLink5(ctx)` in a try/catch (any reflection failure returns the input unchanged so DL3 path is bit-for-bit identical); on confirmed DL5, layerStack 3 or 4 is rewritten to 2 with an explicit `AppLogger.i DL5 override: layerStack N → 2 (mirror composed fission output)` line, any other value (1, 5+) is passed through untouched. Helper is called inside both `startMirror(Context, Display, Surface, int, int)` (app-uid direct SurfaceControl path) and `startMirrorViaDaemon(Context, IBinder, Display, Surface, int, int)` (daemon-uid path) right after `getLayerStack()` reflection — symmetric coverage so either fallback chain captures the correct compositor output. Both method signatures gained a leading `Context` parameter; sole caller is `MainActivity.attemptStartMirror` (2 sites), both updated to pass `this`. **(B2) Daemon-side mirror setup logging (`MirrorDaemon.setupMirror`).** All pre-build-195 mirror traces in the daemon went through `Log.i(TAG, …)` (logcat-only), so when the on-device M7 diagnostic test reads `/data/local/tmp/mirrordaemon_latest.log` (which only captures `out()` lines) the file showed only the daemon's startup banner — no setup chain. Build 195 mirrors the critical-path `Log.i` calls of `setupMirror` to `out()`: `setupMirror BEGIN layerStack=N cluster=WxH view=WxH surface=valid=true/false`, `setupMirror createDisplay OK token=…` (or `setupMirror FAIL createDisplay returned null (DL5 SurfaceControl quirk?)`), `setupMirror SF dump (layerStack=N): …` (or `(empty — token NOT in SurfaceFlinger!)` when the post-setup grep returns nothing), `setupMirror DONE ok=true layerStack=N dst=WxH off=(X,Y)`, and `setupMirror EXCEPTION: ClassName: message` on any reflection failure. Now the next in-car M7 capture will show the full daemon chain in the file — if mirror still fails, we can read exactly which step returned null or threw. **(B3) M10 LIVE probe fixes (`MirrorTestRunner.runM10`).** The build-194 M10 contained two known-bad hard-codings that made the test less useful on DL5: STEP 2 / STEP 7 issued `service call AutoContainer 2 …` (PascalCase, the legacy DL3 binder service name), but the cluster service on DL5 is registered as `auto_container` (snake_case, confirmed in production by `ClusterManager`'s prod path) — STEP 2 reported `Service AutoContainer does not exist` and STEP 7's cleanup `sendInfo(18)+sendInfo(0)` was a no-op. STEP 4/5 launched `com.android.settings/.Settings` (BYD ROM doesn't ship this exact AOSP activity name — `Activity class does not exist`). Build 195 replaces `AutoContainer` with `auto_container` (3 occurrences) and `com.android.settings/.Settings` with `com.android.launcher3/.Launcher` (2 occurrences; launcher3 is universally installed on AOSP and visible in M3 dumps). M10 doc comment updated. The test is still ALWAYS PASS (manual analysis of STEP 3 framebufferSpace diff and STEP 6 routing), but next run will produce real sendInfo Parcel hex and real `am start` Status/TotalTime/WaitTime + matching rootTaskId in STEP 6 — no more two-bugs-cancelling-out. **(B4) Behavioural contract for DL3 (production reference).** Zero risk by construction: `applyDl5LayerStackOverride` returns input unchanged when `Platform.isDiLink5` returns false, daemon `out()` additions are pure log writes (no logic), `Context` param addition is a pure signature widening with all callers updated. The only DL3-observable change is in M10 (Diag-only test, never on the runtime hot path): on DL3 `auto_container` may or may not exist as a binder service name and `launcher3` is the universal launcher → the diag result strings will differ from build 194 but the test still PASSes and no production code path is affected. **(B5) Touched files for (B).** `dashboard/ClusterMirrorManager.java` (+`Platform` import, +`Context ctx` param on `startMirror` and `startMirrorViaDaemon`, +`applyDl5LayerStackOverride` helper, 2 call-sites of the helper — ~30 LoC), `daemon/MirrorDaemon.java` (+6 `out()` calls in `setupMirror` — ~12 LoC), `mirror/MirrorTestRunner.java` (3× `AutoContainer`→`auto_container` + 2× `com.android.settings/.Settings`→`com.android.launcher3/.Launcher` + doc comment refresh — ~10 LoC), `MainActivity.java` (+2 `this,` args in `attemptStartMirror` mirror startup — 2 LoC). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings/layouts. **(B6) Validation plan for (B).** (a) DL5 testeur — install, activate cluster, launch any app on display 3 (Yandex, Maps, etc.), **observe in-app preview surface DURING active mirror (don't open Diag first)** — expected: preview now shows the actual cluster content (layerStack=2 composed fission output) instead of black. If still black: open Diag → Mirror → `Lancer tous`, M7 will now contain the full daemon setupMirror chain (createDisplay token, SF dump result, projection params) — paste in next issue. (b) DL5 testeur — re-run M10: STEP 2 should now show a successful `Parcel(00000000 00000001)` from `auto_container`, STEP 4/5 should show `Starting: Intent { ... cmp=com.android.launcher3/.Launcher }` with a real Status/TotalTime, STEP 6 should still show the `rootTaskId=N mSession=...` evidence as in build 194 but now correlated with launcher3 instead of "does not exist". (c) DL3 Seal EU — install, activate cluster, launch Yandex/Waze on cluster: expected zero behaviour change vs build 194 (mirror works exactly as before, `applyDl5LayerStackOverride` short-circuits via `Platform.isDiLink5=false`). Logcat tag `ClusterMirrorManager` should NOT contain any `DL5 override` line on DL3. (d) DL2 testeur — install, navigate UI: zero impact (DL2 has no cluster display so mirror code path never runs). Done. |
| **1.2.5-build194** | 194 | **Diag → Mirror — new M10 LIVE cluster-routing probe (sendInfo 16 + am start --display 3/4).** Builds 192-193 gave us read-only SF / WindowManager snapshots of the DL5 cluster topology and confirmed visually that `sendInfo(1000, 16)` opens a black projection window on the cluster, but no single run captured the BEFORE/AFTER framebufferSpace transition AND the activity-routing outcome in the same evidence file. M10 closes that gap end-to-end in one shot. **(1) Test logic.** STEP 1 snapshots `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger` filtered on `DisplayDevice{\|framebufferSpace=ProjectionSpace\|layerStack=` (head -120) — gives the BEFORE state of every fission/XDJA display, expected mostly `1×1` slots for displays 3/4. STEP 2 fires `service call AutoContainer 2 i32 1000 i32 16 s16 ""` via ADB-local shell (uid 2000, the only Binder path that works on DL5 since the app-uid `getService("autocontainer")` returns SIGNATURE_NO_MATCH). STEP 3 waits 2 s then re-snapshots the same grep — the display whose `framebufferSpace` flips from `1×1` to `1920×720` is the one the cluster compositor latches onto. STEPS 4 & 5 fire `am start-activity -W --display 3` then `--display 4` with `com.android.settings/.Settings` (neutral, multi-window-friendly system app, always present) and capture the `-W` synchronous result (Status / TotalTime / WaitTime). STEP 6 dumps `dumpsys window` filtered on `Display: mDisplayId=\|mCurrentFocus=\|mFocusedApp=\|rootTaskId=` to confirm which display now actually hosts the Settings task. STEP 7 cleans up: `am force-stop com.android.settings` + `sendInfo 18` (close projection) + `sendInfo 0` (restore Qt native video stream). All seven step outputs are concatenated into the single test detail blob shipped via `Envoyer (.log)` → Telegram. **(2) Status semantics.** M10 always PASS — analysis is manual (compare STEP 1 vs STEP 3 framebufferSpace per display, then STEP 6 routing); a hard PASS/FAIL would have required hard-coding an expected display id, but the whole point of this test is to *discover* that id. The result message reads `live routing run complete — compare STEP 1 vs STEP 3, then STEP 6`. **(3) Side-effect contract.** Two non-trivial side-effects, both pre-validated as user-safe: (a) `sendInfo 16` opens a black projection window on the cluster for ~6 s (the user has visually confirmed this in build 192 D28 and it is harmless), (b) the Settings activity briefly launches and is force-stopped. STEP 7 always runs even if STEPS 4/5 fail, so the cluster is always returned to its baseline. No production code path is invoked (the suite stays read-only at the architecture level). **(4) Touched files.** `mirror/MirrorTestRunner.java` (+1 catalog entry, +1 dispatcher case, +1 `runM10` method ~85 LoC, +1 reused grep constant), `build.gradle` (versionCode 193→194, versionName 1.2.4→1.2.5). No new permissions, dependencies, layouts, strings or drawables — M10 reuses the existing `runShellSync` helper, `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` infrastructure, and the existing `include_diag_mirror.xml` row layout. **(5) Validation plan.** (a) Install on DL5 device. Open DashCast → Diag → swipe to Mirror tab → tap `Lancer tous`. Wait ~25 s for M10 (longest test in the suite due to the 2 s + 1 s + 1 s waits). (b) Tap `Envoyer (.log)` → Telegram. (c) In the report look for `[PASS] M10` then under `--- detail ---` read STEP 1 vs STEP 3: ONE of `fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_0/1/2` should have flipped from `framebufferSpace=ProjectionSpace(start=Rect(0, 0, 1, 1)` to `=Rect(0, 0, 1920, 720)`. That display's `layerStack=` value is the cluster compositor's target layerStack. Then STEP 6 should show `rootTaskId=...` for a `mDisplayId=3` or `mDisplayId=4` entry hosting `com.android.settings/.Settings` — confirming the activity-routing side of the pipeline. (d) On DL3 / DL2 devices the test will run too but STEP 2 will likely return `Service AutoContainer does not exist` (DL3) or hit the DL2 single-display ROM (no cluster compositor) — the test still PASSES with the empty-output evidence, which is itself useful (proves AutoContainer is DL5-only). |

Suite writes all heavy artifacts to `/storage/emulated/0/Download/dl5_mirror_diag/` (BYD file-manager visible). **M1 — Cluster display fingerprint (no shell).** `DisplayManager.getDisplays()` + reflective `Display.getLayerStack/getOwnerPackageName/getOwnerUid` for every display: id, name, real size, density, flag breakdown, owner pkg/uid, layerStack. WindowManager-side identity of the cluster surface, decoupled from SF. **M2 — `SurfaceControl.getPhysicalDisplayIds()` (reflection).** Reflective call to the hidden API plus `getPhysicalDisplayToken(id)` for each returned id. SecurityException expected from app uid; the failure mode itself is useful evidence. **M3 — `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger --list`** (top 100 lines). Compositor's view of every display/layer name. **M4 — full `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger \| head -300`** saved verbatim to `dumpsys_sf_full.txt`. **M5 — filtered SF topology**: `grep -E 'Display \|layerStack\|format\|orientation\|XDJA\|fission\|cluster\|byd\|projection\|mirror'`. **M6 — `dumpsys window` cluster state**: `grep -E 'Display #\|mDisplayId\|mCurrentFocus\|mFocusedApp\|cluster\|fission\|XDJA\|imeLayerStack'`. **M7 — MirrorDaemon log tail (200 lines)**: reads `/data/local/tmp/mirrordaemon_latest.log` if present (the daemon writes its own post-setup `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger \| grep byd_myapp_mirror` here, the gold evidence for whether SF accepted the mirror token), WARN if file missing (daemon never launched in this session). **M8 — Copies the daemon log** to `Download/dl5_mirror_diag/mirrordaemon_latest.log` so it ships alongside the textual report. **M9 — Mirror token presence post-setup**: `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger \| grep -E 'byd_myapp_mirror\|mybyd_preview_mirror'`. If the token is absent the daemon's `createDisplay` silently failed; if present but cluster still black, the layerStack mapping is wrong (cluster pixels live in another compositor). **(3) Behavioural contract.** Mirror tab is read-only diagnostic only. Zero changes to `MirrorDaemon`, `ClusterMirrorManager`, `ClusterService` or any cluster touch/launch path. DL3 mirror unchanged. DL5 mirror unchanged. Existing 9 Diag tabs unchanged (DL5 D30 keeps its broader SF dump, M-suite is a more curated mirror-focused subset). **(4) Build.** `versionCode` 192 → 193, `versionName` 1.2.3 → 1.2.4. Touched files outside CHANGELOG/gradle: `DiagActivity.java` (+`TAB_MIRROR`, +`panelMirror`, +`bindMirrorPanel`/`prepareMirrorTestRows`/`bindMirrorRow`/`runMirrorAllTests`/`updateMirrorCounters`/`sendMirrorLog`, +`showPanelForTab` Mirror branch — ~150 LoC), new `mirror/MirrorTestRunner.java` (~330 LoC, 9 tests + `buildReport` + `runShellSync` helper), new `include_diag_mirror.xml` (~140 LoC, clone of DL5 panel minus Spinner), `activity_diag.xml` (+1 TabItem, +1 panel `<include>`), `values/strings.xml` (+8 `diag_mirror_*` strings). (c) Tap **Envoyer (.log)** → Android share chooser opens → pick Telegram → message goes through with the full report attached as text. (e) On DL3 — install, open Diag, confirm default tab is unchanged (Beta Engine), Mirror tab visible at position 8, tapping it just runs the same suite (M1 yields the DL3 cluster display fingerprint, the rest succeeds on shell). | **1.2.6-build192** | 192 | **DL2 diag UX — default tab on DL2 + 10 new RE-oriented tests including APK extraction (cluster prioritised) to Download/.** Two scope items driven by DL2 testeur workflow (22/05/2026, post-build-191). **(1) Default Diag tab.** `DiagActivity.onCreate` previously hard-coded `TAB_DILINK5` when DL5 was auto-detected, `TAB_BETA_ENGINE` otherwise — DL2 users always landed on Beta Engine (which on DL2 is mostly SKIPPED rows since the proxy daemon isn't tested on this platform yet) and had to manually navigate to the rightmost tab. Build 192 adds a priority branch: `Platform.get().isAutoDetectedDiLink2()` → `TAB_DILINK2`, else DL5 → `TAB_DILINK5`, else `TAB_BETA_ENGINE`. DL2 wins over DL5 because the two detectors are mutually exclusive (different brand+API gates) but the priority makes the intent unambiguous if either signature ever overlaps. **(2) `DiLink2TestRunner` — 10 new tests (S16–S25), catalog grows 30 → 40.** **S16 — BYD packages discovery (shell).** `pm list packages -f \| grep -iE 'com\.byd\|com\.alps\|com\.xdja\|com\.dilink\|cluster\|automotive' \| sort -u` parsed into a typed `DiscoveredPkg` list (`pkg`, `apkPath`, `versionName`, `versionCode` via `PackageManager.getPackageInfo`) stored in `private static List<DiscoveredPkg> sLastDiscovery`. Same pattern as DL5 D9. Cleared at the start of each run so re-running the suite gives a fresh capture. PASS when ≥1 match. **S17 — APK extraction → `/storage/emulated/0/Download/dl2_apks/`.** THE headline test of this build. Mirrors DL5 D10's shell-based extraction (avoids app-uid sandbox issues on /sdcard since A10) but with two DL2-specific tweaks: (a) output dir is `dl2_apks/` (visually distinct from DL5's `dashcast_apks/` in the same Download folder), (b) **`com.byd.cluster` is sorted FIRST in the extraction order** via a custom `Collections.sort` so even if the test runs out of time the most-RE-relevant APK is captured first. Whitelist (`S17_INTERESTING`): `com.byd.cluster` (priority #1), `com.byd.appstartmanagement`, `com.byd.providers.appstartup`, `com.byd.containerservice`, `com.xdja.containerservice`, `com.byd.clusterdebug`, `com.byd.car.server`, `com.byd.crosscontrol`, `com.byd.smarttravel`, `com.byd.commander`, `com.byd.freedom`, `com.byd.overdrive`, `com.byd.windowmanagement` + substring tokens `fission`, `projection`, `cluster`, `dilink`, `magicwindow`, `mirror`. Extraction via `cat '<src>' > '<dst>'` shell redirect (uid 2000 has write to `/sdcard/Download/` on A9+), cache via `stat -c %s` to skip already-extracted APKs (filename embeds `versionCode`). On second run with no new packages, S17 produces zero `cat` calls. Status: PASS if ok>0 && fail==0, WARN if mixed, FAIL if no APK extracted. Message reads `N APK(s) in Download/dl2_apks/ (X cached), Y skipped`. SKIPPED if S16 was never run. The target path matches the user's screenshot showing the device file manager browsing `/storage/emulated/0/Download` — direct access from the BYD file picker, no `adb pull` needed. **S18 — `dumpsys window` focused/displays/overscan.** `dumpsys window \| grep -E 'mDisplayId\|mCurrentFocus\|mFocusedApp\|mDisplayContent\|imeLayerStack\|mStackId\|mOverscan' \| head -60`. Snapshot of WindowManager's view of the focused activity, per-display content roots, IME layer stack, and overscan rects — useful baseline before reproducing any display bug. **S19 — `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger` topology.** `--list` (top 30 layers) + filtered topology (`Display \|layerStack\|Output\|orientation\|HWComposer\|hwc composition`). Ground-truth physical/virtual display map from the compositor side, bypasses WindowManager filtering. **S20 — Recent BYD logcat.** `logcat -d -v threadtime \| grep -iE '<BYD/cluster/projection keywords>' \| tail -200`. Captures the persistent buffer's last ~200 BYD/cluster log lines, surfaces silent errors (SecurityException stacks, binder denials) that don't reach UI. **S21 — `com.byd.cluster` full dumpsys package.** Shell-level manifest dump (250 lines): activities/services/receivers/providers with `exported` flag, declared + requested permissions with `protectionLevel`, signature, install location. Complements L11's PackageManager-only view (PM filters some fields for non-system app uids on API 28). Status WARN with `not installed on this DL2 device` when the package is absent. **S22 — `com.byd.appstartmanagement` full dumpsys package.** Same depth as S21 for the second BYD package of interest. On DL5 this package gates app launch routing; if it has the same role on DL2 it's the second mandatory RE target. **S23 — BYD framework JARs + permission XMLs probe.** `ls -la /system/framework/`, `/vendor/framework/`, `/system/etc/permissions/`, `/vendor/etc/permissions/` all filtered on `byd\|alps\|cluster\|automotive`. Enumerates ActivityManager stacks and tasks with their `displayId` — first concrete evidence of whether DL2 actually has tasks living on any display other than 0. Status PASS if any `Stack id=` or `taskId=` line is present. **S25 — Crash logs.** `ls -lt /data/tombstones/`, `/data/anr/`, `/data/system/dropbox/` (last filtered on `byd\|cluster\|crash\|anr`). Existing tests L1–L15 and S1–S15 untouched. The new S16–S25 tests run only when the user opens Diag → DiLink 2 → Run All (or taps a specific row). Every S test self-marks SKIPPED if ADB shell is unreachable (existing `skipIfNoShell` helper). DL3/DL5 are not affected — the new tests live in `DiLink2TestRunner` which is not invoked outside the DL2 panel. **(4) Build.** `versionCode` 191 → 192, `versionName` 1.2.5 → 1.2.6. Touched files outside CHANGELOG/gradle: `DiagActivity.java` (defaultTab branch), `dilink2/DiLink2TestRunner.java` (catalog +10 entries, dispatcher +10 cases, +`DiscoveredPkg` holder, +`sLastDiscovery` list, +`S17_INTERESTING` whitelist, +`s17IsInteresting` helper, +10 `runS16..runS25` test bodies — ~260 LoC). (c) S16 PASS with N pkg discovered. (d) S17 PASS with `N APK(s) in Download/dl2_apks/`. Open BYD file manager → `Download` → `dl2_apks` → confirm `com.byd.cluster_v<X>.apk` is present (first in alphabetical order anyway, but extracted first by S17's sort). (e) `Envoyer (.log)` → ship via Telegram. (f) On DL3/DL5 — install, open Diag, confirm default tab is still DiLink 5 (DL5 device) or Beta Engine (DL3 device with neither auto-detection triggered). | **1.2.5-build191** | 191 | **DL2 hard-ban on every display-resize shell command (defense in depth).** Field log of DL2 testeur (22/05/2026, post-build-190) confirmed (a) DL2 has a single physical display 0 (1280×660, no PRESENTATION display, no second framebuffer — L3 / L5 / S4 / S5 / S15 all PASS/WARN as expected), (b) the BYD MTK MT6765 ROM silently re-routes `wm overscan\|size\|density -d N` to display 0 when display N does not exist (root cause of the screen-shrink incident the user self-recovered by resetting margins). Even though build 190 already hides the Settings → Marges card on DL2 via `applyDiLink2OverscanGuard`, ANY current or future caller that issues a resize shell command could still reach the ROM and shrink the main UI. Build 191 closes that hole at the lowest possible layer. **(1) `AdbLocalClient.isDiLink2Safe(Context)`** — public mirror of `isDiLink5Safe`, exception-safe wrapper around `Platform.get().isDiLink2(ctx)` that falls back to `false` on any reflection error so the DL3/DL5 hot path is bit-for-bit identical. **(2) `AdbLocalClient.isDisplayResizeCmd(String)`** — regex `(?s).*\bwm\s+(overscan\|size\|density)\b.*` matches any command line that contains `wm overscan\|size\|density` (with any arguments, anywhere in the line, including pipelines and `&&` chains). **(3) `AdbLocalClient.blockDiLink2Resize(Context, String)`** — centralised guard returning `true` (with an `AppLogger.w` line `"DL2 BLOCK: refused resize cmd \"…\" — single display 0, would shrink main screen"`) when both predicates fire, `false` otherwise. **(4) Guard wired in `AdbLocalClient.executeShell`** (fire-and-forget) — early-return before the executor dispatch, no callback to honour. **(5) Guard wired in `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult`** — early-return with `callback.onError("blocked on DiLink 2: no cluster display (would shrink main screen)")` so any UI surfacing the error sees a clear message instead of a silent no-op. **(6) Guard wired at the TOP of `ShellGateway.execShellWithResult`** — must be checked BEFORE the proxy-ON path because Beta Engine bypasses `AdbLocalClient.executeShell*` entirely (goes straight to `BetaProxyClient.runShell` / typed verbs); without this layer the typed `setOverscan` path or the proxy `runShell` would let a DL2 resize through. Same error callback contract. **(7) `SettingsActivity.applyOverscan()` — DL2 hard guard at entry.** Defense in depth on top of the build-190 `applyDiLink2OverscanGuard` UI hide: even if the card somehow gets shown (deep-link, future refactor, runtime UI inflation bug), `applyOverscan()` now early-returns when `Platform.get().isDiLink2(this)` with the user-visible message `"⚠️ Marges désactivées sur DiLink 2 (un seul écran)."` in `tv_overscan_result` and an `AppLogger.w` line for forensics. **(8) Coverage matrix.** Every production resize call site is now blocked on DL2 by at least one layer: `SettingsActivity.applyOverscan` (layer 7+2+3+4), `MainActivity.autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` + resize SeekBar (layer 6+2+3 via ShellGateway), `ClusterService.applyClusterFreeformBounds` + `onDashboardDisplayConnected` + `stopProjectionNoAdb` (layer 6+2+3 via ShellGateway). The dead-code `AdbLocalClient.runClusterDisplaySizeTest` (manual diagnostic, no UI caller) bypasses `executeShell*` by going through raw `dadb.shell` — left untouched, would need separate handling if ever wired to a button. **(9) Behavioural contract.** DL3 / DL5 zero behaviour change: `isDiLink2Safe` returns false on both, so `blockDiLink2Resize` is a no-op short-circuit before any work. The `wm overscan -d 1` on DL3 (cluster) and `wm overscan -d 3` on DL5 still run exactly as in build 190. On DL2 every such command is intercepted with a single WARN log line and an `onError` callback when applicable. **(10) Build.** `versionCode` 190 → 191, `versionName` 1.2.4 → 1.2.5. Touched files outside CHANGELOG / gradle: `AdbLocalClient.java` (+`isDiLink2Safe`, +`isDisplayResizeCmd`, +`blockDiLink2Resize`, +2 early-return guards), `beta/ShellGateway.java` (+guard at top of `execShellWithResult`), `SettingsActivity.java` (+DL2 hard guard at entry of `applyOverscan`). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings. **Validation plan.** (a) On DL2 device — install, open Diag → Logs and trigger any code path that would issue `wm overscan/size/density` (impossible from the UI now thanks to build 190 hide, so manual via `am broadcast` or future test): expect log line `DL2 BLOCK: refused resize cmd "<cmd>" — single display 0, would shrink main screen` and no actual command sent. Sanity: install, navigate UI, confirm no spurious `DL2 BLOCK` lines in normal operation (only triggered when something tries a resize). | **1.2.4-build190** | 190 | **Critical DL2 fix — Settings “Marges” card no longer shrinks the main screen on DiLink 2.** Field report (22/05/2026, DL2 testeur): user changed `Settings → Marges` to H=80 / V=50 and the main BYD UI shrank — *“I changed the margins setting to 80/50 and it made the screen smaller”*. Root cause traced in [SettingsActivity.applyOverscan()](MyBYDApp/app/src/main/java/com/byd/dashcast/SettingsActivity.java): the command was hardcoded `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 1` (= cluster display id on BYD Seal EU DL3). On DL2 there is no display 1 (single display 0, confirmed by `DisplayManager.getDisplays().length == 1` in the L3 / S4 reports of build 189), so the MTK MT6765 ROM silently fell back to applying the overscan to display 0 — the main 1280×660 UI screen the user is looking at. User self-fix was to press the Reset button (which sends `wm overscan 0,0,0,0 -d 1` and accidentally undoes the harm by the same MTK fallback path). **Build 190 hard-fixes the root cause** with the three-layer defense (option C in the 22/05/2026 user decision): **(1) `Platform.detectDiLink2(brand, product, api)` + `Platform.isDiLink2(Context)` + `Platform.isAutoDetectedDiLink2()`.** New auto-detector mirroring `detectDiLink5` but scoped to the alps / k65v1 / API 28-29 signature confirmed by two DL2 field reports (21/05/2026): requires `Build.BRAND == "alps"` AND `ro.product.name` contains `k65` AND `Build.VERSION.SDK_INT` is 28 or 29. Conservative gates — returns false on any uncertainty so the DL3 / DL5 hot path is bit-for-bit identical. Constructor now caches both `autoDiLink5` and `autoDiLink2` at process start; the singleton is built in `DashCastApp.onCreate()` so subsequent calls are zero-cost. **(2) `SettingsActivity.applyDiLink2OverscanGuard()`.** Called in `onCreate()` right after `bindViews / loadPreferences / wireListeners / wireSettingsNavRail`. Hides BOTH the `tv_overscan_section_title` TextView and the `card_overscan` MaterialCardView (`View.GONE`) when EITHER (a) `Platform.isDiLink2(this)` is true OR (b) `resolveClusterDisplayId() < 0` (no secondary display present at activity launch, covers a DL3 / DL5 device where the cluster is physically disconnected). The user simply cannot reach the offending button on DL2 anymore. AppLogger.i records the reason (`DL2 platform detected (alps/k65v1) — no cluster display` or `no secondary display present at launch`) for forensic traceability when investigating future reports. **(3) `SettingsActivity.applyOverscan()` — hardcoded `-d 1` removed.** Now calls `resolveClusterDisplayId()` to obtain the actual cluster display id dynamically; aborts cleanly with `⚠️ Aucun cluster détecté — marges ignorées (DL2 ?).` shown in `tv_overscan_result` if `< 0` is returned. Belt-and-suspenders for any code path that could bypass the guard in (2) (e.g. user-fired intent, deep-link, or a future refactor that re-shows the card). The shell command now reads `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d <resolved>` instead of `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 1`. **(4) `SettingsActivity.resolveClusterDisplayId()`.** Pure java helper: tries `DisplayManager.getDisplays(DISPLAY_CATEGORY_PRESENTATION)` first (the strict cluster filter used by `ClusterManager.findClusterDisplay`), falls back to `getDisplays()` and returns the first non-default id. Returns `-1` on (a) no DisplayManager service available (defensive), (b) no display with id != 0 found (DL2 case), (c) any Throwable (defensive). Logic is identical to the existing `ClusterManager.findClusterDisplay()` helper but with `getDisplayId()` returned directly to avoid an unused `Display` object allocation in the Settings hot path. **(5) Layout `activity_settings.xml`.** Two id additions — `android:id="@+id/tv_overscan_section_title"` on the section header TextView and `android:id="@+id/card_overscan"` on the MaterialCardView — needed for the `View.GONE` toggle in (2). No structural change, no style change, no string change. **(6) Behavioural contract.** On DL3 (BYD Seal EU, the user's main vehicle): `Platform.isDiLink2` returns false, `resolveClusterDisplayId()` returns 1 (the cluster), the card is shown, `applyOverscan` issues `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 1` exactly as before — zero behaviour change. On DL5 (BYD-AUTO testeur): `Platform.isDiLink2` returns false, `resolveClusterDisplayId()` returns 3 (the first `XDJAScreenProjection_*` display), the card is shown, `applyOverscan` now issues `wm overscan H,V,H,V -d 3` instead of the previously useless `-d 1` (which on DL5 was rejected by API 30+ anyway with `Unknown command: overscan`, see build 188). DL5 owners can now actually use the margins UI for their cluster — incidental fix. On DL2 (alps / k65v1 / API 28): `Platform.isDiLink2` returns true → the card is `View.GONE` from `onCreate` → the user cannot press Apply → the main screen can never be shrunk by this code path again. **(7) Build.** `versionCode` 189 → 190, `versionName` 1.2.3 → 1.2.4. Touched files: `MyBYDApp/app/build.gradle` (versionCode + versionName), `MyBYDApp/CHANGELOG.md` (this entry), `MyBYDApp/app/src/main/java/com/byd/dashcast/platform/Platform.java` (+`autoDiLink2` field, +`detectDiLink2`, +`isAutoDetectedDiLink2`, +`isDiLink2`), `MyBYDApp/app/src/main/java/com/byd/dashcast/SettingsActivity.java` (+2 view fields, +`applyDiLink2OverscanGuard()`, +`resolveClusterDisplayId()`, `applyOverscan()` rewritten, +2 imports for `DisplayManager`/`Display`/`Platform`), `MyBYDApp/app/src/main/res/layout/activity_settings.xml` (+2 ids). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings (the inline `⚠️ Aucun cluster détecté — marges ignorées (DL2 ?).` is a one-shot diagnostic, the French-only audience for this branch is acceptable). No production hot-path changes outside SettingsActivity. **Validation plan.** (a) On DL2 device — install the OTA, open DashCast → Settings, scroll down, confirm the entire “Marges Overscan” section (title + slider card + Apply/Reset buttons) is no longer visible. Logcat tag `SettingsActivity` should show `Overscan section hidden: DL2 platform detected (alps/k65v1) — no cluster display` at activity start. The previously broken Apply path cannot be triggered. (b) On DL3 Seal EU (production reference device) — install, open Settings, confirm the Marges card IS visible and looks identical to build 189, slide H to 80 / V to 50, tap Apply, confirm the cluster screen shrinks as before, tap Reset, confirm the cluster screen restores. Logcat tag `SettingsActivity` should show `applyOverscan → wm overscan 80,50,80,50 -d 1` (same as build 189) and the result TextView should display `✅ Marges appliquées: H=80px V=50px`. (c) On DL5 testeur — install, open Settings, confirm the Marges card IS visible, slide H to 80 / V to 50, tap Apply, expected log line is `applyOverscan → wm overscan 80,50,80,50 -d 3` (was `-d 1` and silently failing in build 189). On API 32 the `wm overscan` subcommand is removed, so the shell will return `Unknown command: overscan` and the result TextView will display `❌ Unknown command: overscan` — acceptable degradation, no functional regression vs build 189, and a follow-up build can replace the shell call with `BetaProxyClient.setOverscan(displayId, l, t, r, b)` via the typed daemon (already implemented since build 174 for Phase 4a) if needed. (d) On any device where the cluster is physically disconnected at activity launch — the card should be hidden with logcat reason `no secondary display present at launch`. |
| **1.2.3-build189** | 189 | **DiLink 2 diag — 15 new shell-based tests (S1–S15) now that ADB-over-TCP is open on the DL2 testeur device.** Build 185 introduced the DL2 panel with a 15-test reflection-only suite (L1–L15) because ADB-TCP was confirmed CLOSED on the field DL2 device. On 22/05/2026 the user opened ADB on that device, which unlocks the entire shell-driven diagnostic surface that DL3 / DL5 already enjoy. Build 189 adds a second test tier (S1–S15) that complements the existing recon tests with active shell probes via the existing `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` relay (uid 2000, dadb dispatcher). The new tier is additive — every S test self-marks `SKIPPED` if the shell is unreachable (returns `__SHELL_ERR__:…` internally), so legacy DL2 devices with ADB closed still see the panel as fully green on the L tier and cleanly skipped on the S tier. **(1) `dilink2/DiLink2TestRunner.java` — new sync shell helper.** `runShellSync(Context, String, long timeoutMs)` wraps the existing async `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` in a `CountDownLatch` + `AtomicReference<String>` so the dilink2-test-runner thread can call shell synchronously without owning its own background executor (same pattern as `BetaTestRunner.testX1/X2`). Errors and dispatch exceptions are funneled into a sentinel string `__SHELL_ERR__:<reason>`; `isShellErr` / `skipIfNoShell` helpers let every S test add a 1-line gate at the top. `safeTrim` caps detail output at a configurable max with an explicit truncation marker. **(2) Catalog +15 entries S1–S15** appended after L15 in `catalog()`. **(3) Dispatcher** — `runAll()` switch extended with 15 new `case "Sn"` lines mapping to `runSn(ctx, r)`. **(4) Test bodies (15 new private static methods, ~270 LoC).** **S1** — ADB shell smoke: `id -u && id -un && pwd && uname -a` — PASS if first line is a numeric uid (typically 2000=shell). **S2** — `getprop` filtered on `byd\|alps\|adb\|cluster\|display\|sf.lcd` — PASS if any property matched, useful to compare DL2 properties against the dump we already have. **S3** — `wm size && wm density && wm overscan` — CRITICAL test that directly addresses the field-reported issue "applications plus petites que l'écran" after running Diag tests: detects `Override:` lines and non-zero overscan tuples; WARN with an explicit "`wm size reset && wm overscan reset` to restore" message when display state is overridden. **S4** — `dumpsys display` filtered on overscan/Override/Physical/state — captures per-display geometry block count. **S5** — `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger --display-id` + HWC display lines — physical compositor inventory. **S6** — `service list` filtered on the 11 cluster/projection-related keywords — visible binder service inventory from the shell perspective (which can differ from what the app uid sees, useful to spot SELinux-shielded services). **S7** — `service call AutoContainer 1` + `service call auto_container 1` — direct probe of BYD's cluster bridge service on DL2 (expected absent — Phase 4 SDK is DL3+). **S8** — `pm list packages -f` filtered + sorted — full APK path inventory for BYD/Alps/Xdja/cluster packages, foundation for any future D10-style APK extraction on DL2. **S9** — `dumpsys activity activities` filtered — current resumed activity + display routing snapshot. **S10** — `getenforce && id -Z && ls -Zd /system/bin/sh` — SELinux enforcing state + SELinux domain of the shell process. **S11** — `ps -A -o PID,USER,NAME` filtered on cluster/projection/byd daemon names (with `ps`-only fallback for toyboxes that reject `-A`). **S12** — `settings list system\|secure\|global` filtered on byd/cluster/display/overscan keywords — captures any user-settable BYD overrides. **S13** — `am start --display 0` + `am start --display 1` on the DashCast launcher activity itself — probes whether DL2 even exposes display id 1 to `am start`; the response disambiguates "no second display" from "second display present but uid-protected". **S14** — `/proc/cpuinfo` (Hardware/Processor lines) + `/proc/meminfo` head + `df -h /data` — hardware fingerprint cross-check (expected `MT67xx` for the MTK MT6765 SoC). **S15** — Filesystem inventory of `/sys/class/drm/`, `/dev/graphics/`, `/vendor/etc/byd/`, `/system/etc/byd/`, `/data/byd/` — direct DRM + framebuffer + BYD config dir listing, ground-truth where the BYD-specific files actually live (or don't) on this MTK ROM. **(5) Behavioural contract.** Production hot path untouched. The L tier (L1–L15) is bit-for-bit unchanged. DL3 / DL5 code paths untouched. The S tier shares zero state with the L tier and gracefully skips if `AdbLocalClient` rejects the shell connection (legacy DL2 with closed ADB). Each S test has a per-command timeout (4–8 s depending on expected workload) so a hung shell never blocks the suite. **(6) Build.** `versionCode` 188 → 189, `versionName` 1.2.2 → 1.2.3. Sole touched file outside CHANGELOG / gradle: `dilink2/DiLink2TestRunner.java` (doc comment refresh + catalog +15 entries + dispatcher +15 cases + `runShellSync` helper + 15 new `runSn` methods + `isShellErr` / `skipIfNoShell` / `safeTrim` helpers). No new permissions, no new dependencies, no new strings (S tests reuse the existing `item_beta_test.xml` row + DiagActivity DL2 panel which iterates `catalog()` programmatically), no new resources. **Validation plan.** (a) Install on the DL2 device (ADB now open). Open Diag → DiLink 2 tab → tap `Lancer tous`. (b) Expected: 30 rows now appear (L1–L15 + S1–S15) instead of 15. L1–L15 results identical to build 188. S1 should PASS with `uid=2000`; S3 is the focus — if WARN, the user has the exact reset command to type; if PASS, the field-reported "apps smaller than screen" symptom is unrelated to display geometry and we look elsewhere. (c) `Envoyer (.log)` → share the full report via Telegram. The S6 + S7 + S8 outputs combined will tell us if any BYD cluster service exists on DL2 that's invisible to the app uid (= explains why L7 reports nothing despite the cluster screen physically existing). (d) On any DL2 device with ADB still closed (legacy field units), expected: L1–L15 PASS as before, S1–S15 all SKIPPED with message "ADB shell unreachable: …" — non-destructive degradation. (e) On DL3 / DL5, the DL2 tab is still visible (we don't gate it behind Platform.isDiLink2 because Platform doesn't have that detector yet); S1–S15 will all PASS with data that's useful for nothing on those platforms — UI inconvenience only, no production risk. |

Our process uid 10148 is denied cross-display launches by IActivityTaskManager on DL5 even though `INTERNAL_SYSTEM_WINDOW` is granted — this differs from DL3 where the same reflection path works. The D31 diagnostic probe (build 186) had already validated the workaround: route the launch through ADB shell (uid 2000, which has the BYD `auto_container` whitelisting + the unrestricted activity-launch privileges). No behaviour change. **(2) New `ClusterService.startActivityViaShell(packageName, displayId, launchIntent)`.** Resolves the launcher activity from `launchIntent.getComponent()` (which `getPackageManager().getLaunchIntentForPackage(pkg)` already populates), builds `am start --display N -a android.intent.action.MAIN -c android.intent.category.LAUNCHER -n <pkg>/<launcher> --activity-clear-task 2>&1`, and dispatches via `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` so the existing dadb-based shell relay (with cached ADB key pair) runs on the background executor. Errors are logged via `AppLogger.e` but not propagated to the `LaunchCallback` — consistent with the legacy IATM path which also reports `onResult(true)` after best-effort dispatch (RemoteException paths are swallowed there too). The `applyClusterFreeformBounds()` call still runs upstream and still applies `setLaunchWindowingMode(FREEFORM)` + `setLaunchBounds(...)` to the ActivityOptions — these are inert on DL5 (the shell `am start` ignores ActivityOptions entirely) but cost nothing to compute, and they are needed for the DL3 path which falls through to `startActivityViaIAM` unchanged. **(4) `launchOnDashboardWithBounds()` — DL5 branch.** Same pattern as (3): on DL5 the shell path takes over, on DL3 the typed IATM path runs unchanged. Note: the explicit split-mode bounds passed by the caller (Rect left/top/right/bottom) are dropped on DL5 since `am start` does not accept windowing bounds — split mode on DL5 would need a different approach (e.g. two shell launches on display #3 and #4 simultaneously), out of scope for 1.2.2. **(5) `applyClusterFreeformBounds()` — skip `wm overscan` on DL5.** The shell `wm overscan` subcommand was removed in Android 11 (API 30) and replaced by `wm display-add-state` / DisplayInfo overrides. DL5 is API 32, and the field log confirmed: `executeShellWithResult: wm overscan 80,50,80,50 -d 3 -> Unknown command: overscan` (lines 31.016, 38.820, 46.264). On DL5 the method now logs `DL5: skipping app-specific wm overscan (cmd removed in API 30+)` at DEBUG level and skips the fork. On DL3 the existing behaviour is unchanged — `wm overscan` is still applied at launch time. **(6) `onDashboardDisplayConnected()` — skip display-level `wm overscan` on DL5.** Same root cause; same fix; same DL3 fallthrough. **(7) `stopProjectionNoAdb()` — skip `wm overscan reset -d 1` on DL5.** Bonus side-fix unrelated to the main change: the reset command was hardcoding display id 1 (DL3 cluster) even on DL5 (display 3); it was already producing the same `Unknown command: overscan` error. Now skipped entirely on DL5. **(8) Behavioural contract.** DiLink 3 path is **bit-for-bit identical** to build 187: every DL5 branch is gated on `AdbLocalClient.isDiLink5Safe(this)` which returns false on DL3, so the existing IATM-typed launch + `wm overscan` shell calls + legacy `wm overscan reset -d 1` all still run on DL3 exactly as before. **(9) Build.** `versionCode` 187 → 188, `versionName` 1.2.1 → 1.2.2. No new dependencies, no new permissions, no protocol changes, no new resources, no new strings. **Validation plan.** Push to the DL5 testeur device, install, reboot.
