Expected logcat sequence (tag `ClusterService` + `AdbLocalClient`): `DL5 launch via shell: am start --display 3 -a android.intent.action.MAIN -c android.intent.category.LAUNCHER -n <pkg>/<launcher> --activity-clear-task 2>&1` → `executeShellWithResult: am start --display 3 ... -> Starting: Intent { ... }` → activity visible on cluster display id 3. No more `SecurityException: Permission Denial ... with launchDisplayId=3`. Stop projection sequence and ADAS panel from build 187 unchanged. On DL3 reference device, log must still show the typed `Launching via IActivityManager on display=N → <pkg>` and the `Applied app-specific wm overscan during launch on display N` line — any `DL5 launch via shell` log line on DL3 is a regression. \|\n\| **1.2.0-build187** \| 187 \| **Production projection wired for DiLink 5 — DL3 path bit-identical, DL5 path uses `auto_container` + sendInfo(16)-only Start / sendInfo(18)+sendInfo(0) Stop.** First production-side integration of the DL5 projection sequence that D12/D31 PASS confirmed shell-side on the 22/05/2026 field log. **(1) `AdbLocalClient` — DL5-aware service-name dispatch.** New static helper `autoContainerSvcName(Context)` returns `"auto_container"` (snake_case) when `Platform.isDiLink5(ctx)` is true, `"AutoContainer"` (PascalCase, DL3 confirmed by user 22/05/2026) otherwise. Helper is exception-safe — any reflection failure falls back to the DL3 default so the production hot path can never throw because of platform detection. New private helper `isDiLink5Safe(Context)` mirrors the same pattern for boolean gating. Three shell call-sites refactored to call the helper instead of the hardcoded string: `sendInfo()` (line 731), `restoreBydOnCluster()` (lines 540 + 545), `restoreOriginCluster()` (lines 629 + 634 + 639). **(2) Typed daemon path gated on DL3.** `BetaProxyClient.autoContainerSendInfo()` resolves the binder via `ServiceManager.getService("AutoContainer")` (hardcoded in `Phase4Verbs.AUTOCONTAINER_SVC`) — that name does not exist on DL5, so every typed `transact()` would fail and force an extra round-trip before falling back to the shell. Three call-sites in `AdbLocalClient` now skip the typed path when DL5 is detected: `sendInfo()` (`if (BetaConfig.isProxyDaemonEnabled(context) && !isDiLink5Safe(context))`), `restoreBydOnCluster()`, `restoreOriginCluster()`. DL5 goes straight to the shell with the correct service name; DL3 behaviour is unchanged (typed path tried first, shell fallback on failure). `Phase4Verbs` itself is NOT touched — the typed daemon is a DL3-only optimisation. **(3) `ClusterManager.activateClusterDisplay()` — DL5 short-circuit.** New branch at the top of the method: if `isDiLink5Safe()` is true, the full DL3 sequence (`sendInfo(30) → 6s → sendInfo(16) → 6s → sendInfo(35)`) is replaced by a single `AdbLocalClient.sendInfo(CLUSTER_TYPE, CMD_PROJECTION_ON=16, "")` followed by a 500 ms settle delay and `resolveDl5Display()` to notify the callback. Rationale from the clusterdebug `_Di5` enumeration (build 184 doc): slot 30 is empty on DL5 (no screen-size mapping), and slot 35 (Di4.0 mode) is DL3-specific (triggers `AutoDisplayService.createVirtualDisplay` on DL3 only — on DL5 the `XDJAScreenProjection_*` displays already exist as persistent PRESENTATION displays at boot). User explicit constraint: *"câbler Start Projection pour Dilink5 qui fait uniquement la command 16"*. **(4) `ClusterManager.resolveDl5Display()` — display targeting.** New private helper called from the DL5 branch. Reuses the existing `findClusterDisplay(dm)` (already correctly returns the first non-default PRESENTATION display, i.e. id=3 on the DL5 field device which exposes `shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_0` at id 3 and `_1` at id 4). On miss, polls every 250 ms up to a 3 s deadline before calling `onDisplayTimeout()` — this safety net should never trigger on DL5 in practice since the field log confirmed both displays are up at boot. User explicit constraint: *"on vient diffuser les applications lancé sur le display ID3 déjà pour commencer"*. **(5) Stop projection — automatic via existing path.** `restoreBydOnCluster()` already issues exactly the sequence the user asked for (`sendInfo(18)` + `sendInfo(0)`); the only DL5-specific concern was the service name, fixed by (1). Same for `restoreOriginCluster()`, which still sends the optional `sendInfo(<screenSizeCmd>)` step at the end — on DL5 this becomes a no-op (empty slot 30 in the `_Di5` enum) but is harmless since `service call` simply returns an empty Parcel for an unknown info code. **(6) `isClusterDisplay()` left untouched** — already accepts any non-default display, so DL5 displays #3 and #4 qualify without changes. **(7) Behavioural contract — DL3.** Every DL3 code path is bit-for-bit identical to build 186: the new DL5 branch is gated on `Platform.isDiLink5(ctx)`, the typed daemon path still runs first on DL3, the shell fallback still uses `"AutoContainer"` (returned by the helper when DL5 detection is false), the full activation sequence (30 → 6s → 16 → 6s → 35) is preserved on DL3 including all the `onError`-fallback branches. **(8) Build.** `versionCode` 186 → 187, `versionName` pinned `"1.2.0"`. Sole production-source files touched: `AdbLocalClient.java` (+helper +helper +3 call-site refactors +3 typed-path gates), `dashboard/ClusterManager.java` (+DL5 short-circuit at top of `activateClusterDisplay` +`isDiLink5Safe` private helper +`resolveDl5Display` helper). **Validation plan.** Push to the DL5 testeur device, install, reboot (cold start ensures `XDJAScreenProjection_*` is up). Open the dashboard, select a target app (e.g. Waze, com.github.standardadb, or any non-system app declaring a LAUNCHER activity), press the existing Start Projection control. Expected DL5 sequence in logcat (tag `AdbLocalClient` + `ClusterManager`): `DL5 activation path: sendInfo(16) only on auto_container` → `sendInfo ADB: service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 16 s16 "" 2>&1` → `DL5 cluster display ready: id=3 name=...XDJAScreenProjection_0` → the existing app-launch path (which already uses `--display 3` via `ActivityOptions.setLaunchDisplayId`) takes over. Stop Projection: expected `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 18` then `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 0`. On a DL3 device the existing field-tested sequence (30 → 16 → 35) must remain visible in logs unchanged — any deviation is a regression. |
| **1.2.0-build186** | 186 | **DiLink 5 diag — D10 extraction relocated to user-visible Download/ + new D31 end-to-end cluster projection probe.** Two scope items on top of build 185, both rooted in the 22/05/2026 DL5 field log (`byd_report_20260522_115956.log`) and the user's live testing on the DL5 vehicle. **(1) D10 — APK extraction now lands in `/storage/emulated/0/Download/dashcast_apks/` instead of the app-private `getExternalFilesDir(null)/extracted_apks/`.** Field-tester feedback: the previous output dir was effectively invisible — the BYD file manager doesn't surface app-private external dirs without root, so the user could see "PASS, 7 APKs ready" in the report but had no way to actually grab the files without `adb pull` from a PC. Worse, every re-run added duplicate-or-cached copies into a dir the user could never empty by hand, slowly eating internal storage build after build. Fix: all filesystem ops in `runD10` now go through the ADB shell (uid 2000, which on Android 10+ has unrestricted write access to `/sdcard/Download/`) — `mkdir -p` for the new dir, `stat -c %s` for the cache check (avoids relying on the app uid being able to `read()` the destination), `cat <src> > <dst> && stat -c %s <dst>` for the copy. At start-up, `runD10` also `rm -rf`'s the legacy `getExternalFilesDir(null)/extracted_apks/` so the next install no longer accumulates dead bytes. The PASS message now reads `N APK(s) in Download/dashcast_apks/ (X cached), Y skipped`. The destination is browsable by the stock BYD file manager (visible under `Download` → `dashcast_apks`), which removes the `adb pull` requirement entirely for users without a PC. **(2) D31 — Live cluster projection end-to-end probe.** First test that drives the full visual cycle the user has been doing by hand to confirm DL5 projection works: `sendInfo(1000, 16)` (opens the projection tunnel, the `shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_*` layerStack becomes visible — initially black, this is what the user confirmed seeing during D28 of build 185) → sleep 1.5 s → `am start --display N -n <selected pkg>/<launcher activity>` (pushes a real activity onto the projection layerStack so the cluster shows the app, not just a black window) → sleep 3 s (user observes the cluster) → `dumpsys activity activities` snapshot filtered on the package and `displayId=` / `topResumed` to correlate the visual with the stack state → `am force-stop <pkg>` → `sendInfo(1000, 18)` (close projection) → `sendInfo(1000, 0)` (restore native video). Loops once per PRESENTATION display (the DL5 device exposes #3 and #4 as `XDJAScreenProjection_0/1`). **(2a) Target-package selection.** D31 deliberately reuses the package already chosen for D8 via the dropdown at the top of the DL5 panel — no new UI, no new prefs key. The package must be installed: `runD31` calls `PackageManager.getPackageInfo(pkg, 0)` up front and FAILs with `Package '<pkg>' is not installed on this device — install it first` (plus a detail line pointing the user to either install it or pick another package from the D8 dropdown) before touching the cluster. SKIPS with `No target package selected — pick one in the D8 dropdown above` when D8 was never run. This directly answers the question raised by the user about D31 hardcoding `com.github.standardadb`: D31 does not hardcode anything, it reads the user's selection. **(2b) Launcher resolution.** Reuses the existing helper `resolveLauncherComponent(ctx, pkg)` so `am start --display N -n <ComponentName>` always targets the package's declared LAUNCHER activity, whatever its concrete class name is. Returns FAIL with `Could not resolve a launcher activity for '<pkg>'` if the package declares no launcher (rare — typically a service-only package). **(2c) Status semantics.** PASS = every `am start --display` returned without `error` / `SecurityException` / `Permission Denial` — message reads `Cycle run on N display(s) — user to confirm visual rendering` to keep the final go/no-go in the user's hands (this is a visual test; the shell can only say the API accepted the call, not that pixels actually reached the cluster). WARN on partial success, FAIL if every launch was refused. The full per-display sequence (commands + outputs + activity-stack snapshot) is dumped to `r.detail` so the user can read the report to understand which display did or did not light up. **(3) DL5 test catalog grows from 30 to 31 entries.** The new D31 row is appended after D30 in the runner catalog and inflated as a normal row by the existing `prepareDl5TestRows()` / `bindDl5Row()` UI code — no layout, strings, drawables or new dependencies. **(4) Behavioural contract.** Production hot path (cluster projection lifecycle, `ClusterManager`, `AdbLocalClient`, daemon code) is untouched. The new D31 sequence is opt-in (the user has to open Diag → DiLink 5 → tap D31 or `Run all`). D31 leaves the cluster in a clean state regardless of failure (`am force-stop` + `sendInfo(18)` + `sendInfo(0)` are always issued at the end of each per-display iteration, including on partial failure). **(5) Build.** `versionCode` 185 → 186, `versionName` pinned `"1.2.0"`. Sole touched files outside CHANGELOG / gradle: `dilink5/DiLink5TestRunner.java` (D10 body rewritten to shell-based ops + legacy dir cleanup, D31 added at the end of the file as a private static method, catalog +1 entry, dispatcher +1 case). |
| **1.2.0-build185** | 185 | **New DiLink 2 recon diagnostic tab — reflection-only test suite (L1–L15) for the alps / k65v1_64_bsp / MT6765 / Android 9 platform.** Two DL2 field reports (`byd_log_20260521_220933.log` and `byd_report_20260521_222022.log`, 327 lines of SysInfo dump) confirmed the DL2 environment differs from DL3/DL5 on every dimension that matters for projection: **(a)** brand=`alps`, manufacturer=`alps`, product=`full_k65v1_64_bsp`, hardware=`mt6765`, API=28, `ro.byd.*` properties ALL undefined — no BYD identity exposed via system properties; **(b)** ADB-over-TCP `127.0.0.1:5555` is CLOSED (`ECONNREFUSED` confirmed twice in the log, both at boot for `SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW` auto-grant and on the `executeShellWithResult "echo ok"` smoke test) — our entire `AdbLocalClient` shell stack is unreachable on DL2; **(c)** `DisplayManager` exposes a single display (Built-in Screen 1280×660 @160dpi, no PRESENTATION-typed display) even though the vehicle physically has a digital cluster screen — the cluster output is not surfaced to the Android display framework; **(d)** `android.app.ActivityTaskManager` is absent (IATM was introduced in API 29, this device is API 28) — every current reflection path that targets IATM throws ClassNotFoundException; **(e)** no XDJA / Auto_container / containerservice / clusterdebug / crosscontrol / windowmanagement / smarttravel / magicwindow package is installed — the BYD ecosystem we built DL3/DL5 support on top of does not exist on this platform; only `com.byd.cluster v1.0` and `com.byd.appstartmanagement v1.0` are present (vs v1.5.0.0 of appstartmanagement on DL5). **Positive surface confirmed in the log:** `SurfaceControl` accessible after `unlockHiddenApis`, `ActivityOptions.setLaunchDisplayId` available, 3/5 BYDAuto*Device SDK classes loadable via reflection (Speed / Energy / Gearbox). **Design choice for build 185.** Because the shell stack is dead on DL2, the new test runner is built on a completely different foundation than DL5: **zero shell calls**, every probe uses Java APIs callable from the app process — `SystemProperties.get` via reflection, `ServiceManager.listServices/getService` via reflection, `PackageManager` queries, `DisplayManager` + reflective `DisplayManagerGlobal.getDisplayIds`, `IActivityManager` method enumeration via reflection, raw `Socket` TCP probes on localhost ADB ports, and `/proc` + `/sys` scans via `java.io.File`. This means the suite runs and produces useful data even when ADB-TCP is unreachable. **(1) `dilink2/DiLink2TestRunner.java` (new file, 15 tests, ~580 LoC).** Self-contained: own `Status` enum, own `TestDef` / `TestResult` / `Listener` types, single-thread executor named `dilink2-test-runner`, ordered catalog, dispatcher. **L1 — Platform fingerprint.** Dumps the full `Build.*` triplet (BRAND / MANUFACTURER / MODEL / PRODUCT / DEVICE / HARDWARE / BOARD / DISPLAY / FINGERPRINT / TAGS / SDK_INT / RELEASE) plus 26 selected `ro.*`, `persist.sys.*`, `sys.*` and `service.adb.tcp.port` properties read via reflected `SystemProperties.get`. PASS when `Build.BRAND=alps` AND `Build.PRODUCT` contains `k65v1` (DL2 signature), WARN otherwise (so the runner is informative on DL3/DL5 too without breaking them). **L2 — ADB local TCP ports probe.** Pure-Java `Socket.connect(InetSocketAddress, 200ms)` on `127.0.0.1` ports 5037 / 5554 / 5555 / 5556 / 4444, with timeout-bucketing of error classification (OPEN vs CLOSED vs TIMEOUT vs ERR). WARN when 0 ports open (expected on DL2 — confirms the shell-disabled environment), PASS when any port responds. **L3 — Multi-display reflective scan.** `DisplayManager.getDisplays()` + `DisplayManager.getDisplays(DISPLAY_CATEGORY_PRESENTATION)` cross-checked against reflective `DisplayManagerGlobal.getDisplayIds()` — the latter returns the raw list before category filtering and sometimes surfaces ids hidden from the standard enumeration. **L4 — SurfaceFlinger physical displays via IBinder.** `ServiceManager.getService("SurfaceFlinger")` + `IBinder.getInterfaceDescriptor()` — deliberately does NOT call `transact()` with custom codes because the SurfaceFlinger transaction layout has been reshuffled across AOSP forks and a wrong code on a vendor binder can SIGSEGV the calling process. PASS on binder reachability alone. **L5 — DRM / framebuffer inventory.** Lists `/sys/class/drm`, `/dev/graphics`, `/sys/class/graphics` via `java.io.File.listFiles()`; PASS when fb1/fb2 OR multiple `card0-*` connectors are visible (= a second hardware display exists outside Android's view), WARN on single framebuffer. **L6 — ServiceManager full inventory + filter.** Reflective `ServiceManager.listServices()` (returns a `String[]` of every registered binder service) then regex-filtered on `cluster\|display\|secondary\|byd\|alps\|auto\|mirror\|magic\|window\|fission\|xdja\|projection\|cross`. Each match prefixed with `  ` for the report. **L7 — Cluster service candidates brute probe.** `ServiceManager.getService()` on 33 hand-curated candidate names spanning DL3/DL5 conventions (`cluster`, `byd_cluster`, `Auto_container`, `auto_container`), MTK conventions (`mtk_cluster`, `mtk_displayfeature`), alps conventions (`alps_cluster`, `alps_display`) and generic suspects (`secondary_display`, `displayfeature`, `mirror`, `magicwindow`, `crosscontrol`, `xdja_container`, `byd_carapi`, `BYDMgmt`, `byd_datacached`, `IBYDCDRService`, `projection`, `carprojection`, `fission`, `appstartmanagement`, etc.); for each PRESENT binder also dumps `IBinder.getInterfaceDescriptor()` so we know the actual AIDL interface name. **L8 — IActivityManager method enumeration.** Reflection on `android.app.IActivityManager` (NOT `IActivityTaskManager` since DL2 is API 28) filtered on `movetask\|setlaunch\|startactivity\|moveactivitytask\|settaskwindowing\|getfocusedstack\|attachapplication` — each entry shows the full method signature so we can build the right typed invocation path. **L9 — ActivityOptions.setLaunchDisplayId smoke.** Builds `ActivityOptions.makeBasic()`, invokes `setLaunchDisplayId(0)` reflectively, then `toBundle()` to confirm the bundled keys are populated (PASS expected, the SysInfo dump already showed this method is present on DL2). **L10 — BYD packages dynamic scan.** `PackageManager.getInstalledPackages(0)` filtered on `com.byd.*` / `com.alps.*` / `com.xdja.*` / `*cluster*` / `*dilink*` — captures package name + versionName + versionCode + APK path with zero shell. **L11/L12 — Manifest deep dive on `com.byd.cluster` and `com.byd.appstartmanagement`** (the only two BYD packages of interest installed on DL2): `getPackageInfo(GET_ACTIVITIES\|GET_SERVICES\|GET_RECEIVERS\|GET_PROVIDERS\|GET_PERMISSIONS)` produces a structured dump of every declared component with exported flag, all declared permissions with protection level, and all requested permissions — lets us see exactly what these packages can do and what entry points they expose. SKIPPED gracefully when the package is not installed. **L13 — BYD SDK classpath probe.** `Class.forName` + reflective `getInstance()` on 8 `BYDAuto*Device` classes (Speed / Energy / Gearbox / AC / AirConditioner / Door / Light / Wiper). Distinguishes "class loadable" from "instance obtainable" — the SysInfo dump showed 3/5 of the original 5 we probed work on DL2; the new probe widens that to 8 classes for a fuller picture. **L14 — `/proc` cluster process scan.** Pure-Java scan of `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` (`new File("/proc").listFiles()`) filtered on `cluster\|fission\|projection\|secondary\|surface\|display`, capped at 40 matches — reveals which native daemons (e.g. `surfaceflinger`, MTK display daemons, vendor cluster handlers) are visible to the app uid even when the binder facing them isn't. **L15 — Hidden-API reachability sanity.** Reflective `ServiceManager.getService` + `getInterfaceDescriptor()` on 6 core system binders (`window`, `activity`, `package`, `display`, `input`, `power`); PASS when at least 4 are reachable — confirms the app process can still talk to system services despite SELinux on API 28 (relevant because some recon strategies on DL2 will need a typed binder path instead of shell). All exceptions inside the test bodies are caught and reported as `FAIL` with `<ExceptionClass>: <message>`; the dispatcher's `Throwable` catch is a defense-in-depth so a single test crash never breaks the suite. **(2) `res/layout/include_diag_dilink2.xml` (new file).** Mirrors the DL5 panel design but simpler — no D8-style live launch, no app picker, just: a header card with title `Plateforme DiLink 2 — Recon`, dynamic subtitle (`Produit : %1$s · brand : %2$s · API %3$d`), a signature pill (`DL2 détectée` / `non-DL2`), the run/copy counters, two Material buttons `Lancer tous` / `Envoyer (.log)`, and a `LinearLayout ll_dl2_test_list` populated programmatically from `DiLink2TestRunner.catalog()` using the existing `item_beta_test.xml` row layout (full re-use to avoid layout duplication). **(3) `DiagActivity.java` wiring.** New tab constant `TAB_DILINK2 = 7` (Sniffer shifted to 8). New `panelDl2` field bound in `bindTabs()`, `showPanelForTab` now toggles a 5th panel. New `bindDl2Panel()` resolves DL2 signature from `Build.BRAND` + `Build.PRODUCT` at bind time (no Platform changes — we deliberately keep `Platform.java` untouched, the DL2 signature detection is a UI-side hint only). New `prepareDl2TestRows()` / `bindDl2Row()` / `runDl2AllTests()` / `updateDl2Counters()` / `copyDl2Report()` mirror exactly the DL5 lifecycle, with their own `dl2RowViews` / `dl2LastResults` lists so no DL5 / Beta state can collide. The copy-report path goes through `AppLogger.shareWithReport()` to ship as `.log` attachment. **(4) `activity_diag.xml` — new `<TabItem android:text="@string/diag_tab_dilink2"/>` inserted between DiLink 5 and Sniffer + new `<include android:id="@+id/panel_dilink2" layout="@layout/include_diag_dilink2"/>` inside `fl_diag_content`. **(5) `strings.xml` — 9 new keys** (`diag_tab_dilink2`, `diag_dl2_header_title`, `diag_dl2_header_subtitle`, `diag_dl2_header_subtitle_fmt`, `diag_dl2_pill_unknown`, `diag_dl2_pill_detected`, `diag_dl2_pill_other`, `diag_dl2_counters_fmt`). French-only for now (the 12 locale files are intentionally NOT updated yet — will batch with the next translation pass once the DL2 panel labels stabilize after field testing). **No production-path changes.** Zero touches to `ClusterManager`, `AdbLocalClient`, `Platform.java`, `BetaTestRunner`, `DiLink5TestRunner`, no new permissions, no new dependencies, no new protocol verbs. DL3 and DL5 behaviour is bit-for-bit identical to build 184. **Build.** `versionCode` 184 → 185, `versionName` pinned `"1.2.0"` per channel policy. **(6) Horizontal swipe navigation in Diag (Option B, lightweight).** With 9 tabs (Cluster / Display / ADB local / Système / ADAS / Beta Engine / DiLink 5 / DiLink 2 / Sniffer) the tab bar is getting long. Build 185 attaches a `GestureDetector.SimpleOnGestureListener` to the `FrameLayout fl_diag_content` (single touch listener, ~40 LoC, no architecture change): a clear horizontal fling (\|dx\| ≥ 120 px AND \|dx\| ≥ 1.5×\|dy\| AND \|vx\| ≥ 220 px/s) selects the next or previous tab via `tabs.getTabAt(next).select()` which reuses the existing `OnTabSelectedListener` so the same `showPanelForTab` path runs. The listener returns `false` so child scrollers (NestedScrollView in DL5/DL2/Sniffer panels, RecyclerView rows in test lists, vertical scroll inside SysInfo report) keep working untouched — only clear horizontal flings are intercepted. The threshold checks reject diagonal swipes and tap noise. No animation between panels (the bascule is dry, like the current tab click) — a proper `ViewPager2 + FragmentStateAdapter` refactor with animated slide is queued for a later build (see `/memories/repo/byd-project.md` TODO: needs 5 new Fragment classes + refactor of all 5 `bind*Panel` methods + retest on DL3/DL5/DL2). Wiring: new `attachSwipeNavigation(View)` method called once in `bindTabs()`. **Validation plan.** (a) Install on the DL2 testeur device, open Diag, navigate to the new DiLink 2 tab, confirm the signature pill reads `Signature : DL2 détectée`, press `Lancer tous`, wait for the 15-test pass (~3 s total — all tests are sub-second since none of them hit shell). (b) Press `Envoyer (.log)`, share the resulting `byd_report_*.log` via Telegram. (c) Cross-check on a DL3 or DL5 device that the tab is still visible (we did NOT gate it behind Platform.isDiLink2 since we have no DL2 detection in Platform yet) and that pressing `Lancer tous` produces a report where L1 reports WARN ("not a DL2 signature") and the other tests still produce useful data — confirms the suite is non-destructive on non-DL2 platforms. The DL2 report will tell us, for the first time, (i) whether any non-DisplayManager surface exposes a second physical display via SurfaceFlinger or DRM, (ii) which binder services BYD or alps registered for cluster control, (iii) what the `com.byd.cluster` package on DL2 actually contains and exposes, (iv) which `IActivityManager` methods are available for typed display routing on API 28 without IATM, (v) whether the ADB-TCP situation can be unblocked from the device side. Based on these answers, build 186 will design the actual DL2 cluster projection strategy. |
| **1.2.0-build184** | 184 | **Diag UI — new ADAS quick-toggle tab + DiLink 5 test polish based on the build-183 field log.** First DL5 field log (`byd_report_20260522_092905.log`, build 183) gave us actionable signal on three fronts: (a) clusterdebug v1.6.1.4 enumeration confirmed AutoContainer `sendInfo` codes 12 (显示Adas / Show ADAS) and 13 (关闭Adas / Hide ADAS) are universal across all DiLink platforms (DL3 / Di4 / DL5 / DL6) — stripped only on R-series; (b) D28 was running a `sendInfo(30)` screen-size hint that is a no-op on DL5 because the cluster never reads it (the corresponding slot is empty in clusterdebug `_Di5` variant); (c) D10 was re-`cat`ing every whitelisted APK on every run even though the destination filename already embeds `versionCode`. Build 184 turns those three findings into shipped fixes plus a new always-visible quick-toggle for the ADAS overlay. **(1) New `ADAS` diagnostic tab** replaces the previously unused `Stress test` placeholder (tab position #5). Layout `include_diag_adas.xml` (190 lines, 3 M3 cards in a `NestedScrollView`): (a) header card with title, subtitle, a runtime status pill showing the resolved service name (`Auto_container` on DL3/Di4 / `auto_container` on DL5+), and two filled / tonal `MaterialButton`s `Afficher ADAS (12)` and `Masquer ADAS (13)`; (b) result card with a monospace `tv_adas_result` text view showing the last issued command and the raw shell response (Parcel hex or error); (c) info card explaining the underlying `service call <svc> 2 i32 1000 i32 <12\|13> s16 ""` invocation, the service-name dispatch rule, and that R-series is unsupported. **(2) Wiring in `DiagActivity.java`**: `TAB_STRESS` (=4) renamed to `TAB_ADAS`, new `panelAdas` field bound in `bindTabs()`, `showPanelForTab` gated on `isAdas`, `TAB_STRESS` removed from the coming-soon switch. New `bindAdasPanel()` resolves the service name once at bind time (visible in the pill) and wires the two buttons to `sendAdasCmd(12)` / `sendAdasCmd(13)`. `sendAdasCmd(int)` re-resolves the service name on every press (so toggling the DL5 override in Settings is picked up without restarting Diag), builds the exact `service call` command line, displays `$ <cmd>\n…envoi en cours…` on press, and writes back the raw daemon response via `safeRunOnUiThread`. Error path prefixes the response with `ERREUR:`. Service dispatch uses `Platform.get().isDiLink5(this)` (try/catch wraps reflection so a future Platform change cannot crash the panel — falls back to the DL3 default `Auto_container`). **(3) `activity_diag.xml`**: 5th `<TabItem>` now references `@string/diag_tab_adas` (new key, value `"ADAS"`, untranslated acronym, added to `values/strings.xml` next to the legacy `diag_tab_stress` which is left in place to keep the 12 locale files unchanged); new `<include android:id="@+id/panel_adas" layout="@layout/include_diag_adas"/>` inserted into `fl_diag_content`. **(4) D10 — APK extraction cache hit.** Filename already embeds `versionCode` (`<pkg>_v<versionCode>.apk`), so if the destination file exists with `length() > 0` we now skip the `cat` redirect entirely, increment a new `cached` counter, and log `↻ <pkg>  (cached, NN KB)`. Message summary reads `N APK(s) ready (X cached), Y skipped — adb pull the dir` instead of the build-183 `N APK(s) extracted`. On a re-run with no new packages, D10 now produces zero shell calls (was N calls per whitelisted package, each up to 15 s of `cat` budget). **(5) D23 rewritten end-to-end.** Build 183 D23 looped over binder transaction codes 0..8 with a neutral payload to enumerate the auto_container API surface — useful one-off but redundant after the clusterdebug enumeration (codes 0/1/2 are interface-transaction / ping / sendInfo respectively, the rest are not transactions but sendInfo *codes*). D23 now sends `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 2 s16 ""` (= sendInfo code 2 = 所有警告灯点亮 / `All warning lamps ON`), waits 3 s via `Thread.sleep(3000)`, then sends `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 3 s16 ""` (= sendInfo code 3 = 所有警告灯熄灭 / `All warning lamps OFF`). Status `WARN` with message asking the user to visually confirm that the warning lamps lit up for ~3 s and went off again; `FAIL` if `service auto_container does not exist`. This is a much stronger test than scanning the binder API: it cross-validates that (a) the snake-case `auto_container` service name reaches the cluster on DL5, (b) the sendInfo path is end-to-end functional and (c) the cluster Qt side honours the published codes from the clusterdebug enumeration. **(6) D28 — dropped the useless `sendInfo(30)` step.** clusterdebug `_Di5` variant has an empty slot at code 30 (was `设置屏幕尺寸` / `Set screen size` on DL3 / Di4, dropped on DL5). The screen-size hint is a no-op on this platform. New D28 flow: `logcat -c` → `sendInfo(16)` (Qt standby ON, real projection trigger) → sleep 3 s → `dumpsys display` → logcat filtered on `auto_container \| AutoContainer \| cluster \| projection \| fission \| XDJA \| magicwindow \| MagicWindow \| containerservice` (last 80 lines) → cleanup `sendInfo(18)` (close) + `sendInfo(0)` (refresh). Catalog description updated to match. The test now exercises only the real projection trigger, no padding. **(7) Documentation.** Added `doc_api/APK_com.byd.clusterdebug_CODES.md`: full enumeration of clusterdebug v1.6.1.4 (versionCode 10601004) sendInfo codes across all three `infoListInit*` variants (`generic` for DL3/Di4, `_Di5` for DL5, `_R` for R-series), with side-by-side zh / EN translations for every populated slot. Used as the canonical reference for both the ADAS panel codes (12/13) and the D23 warning-lamp cycle (2/3), and as the matrix to audit which AutoContainer codes are platform-stable vs platform-stripped. **(8) Behavioural contract.** Production hot path (`ClusterManager.sendActivationSequence` and the rest of the cluster lifecycle) is unchanged. The ADAS panel reuses the existing `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` background path — no new thread, no new permission, no new system Context. The panel is read-only / write-only with respect to projection state: pressing `Afficher ADAS` while a projection is running just overlays the warning lamps; pressing `Masquer ADAS` does not affect projection. **(9) Build.** `versionCode` 183 → 184, `versionName` pinned `"1.2.0"` per channel policy. (b) Install on a DL5 device, confirm pill reads `auto_container` (snake_case), repeat the cycle, then run Diag → DiLink 5 → D23 → confirm WARN status with the visual-cycle message, run D10 twice in a row → second pass shows all rows as `cached`, run D28 → confirm the report no longer contains a `sendInfo(30)` section. | **1.2.0-build182** | 182 | **DiLink 5 enablement — prerelease branch (`beta/1.2.0-dilink5`).** Major version bump because DL5 is a different platform (Android 12 / API 32, BYD-AUTO brand, new `com.byd.containerservice` rebrand, new `com.byd.appstartmanagement` gatekeeper) and the diagnostic surface required to triage it is non-trivial. **DiLink 5 diag — 11 new tests (D10–D20) + D9 dynamic discovery + APK extraction (whitelisted) for reverse-engineering.** First DL5 field log (`byd_report_20260521_212356.log`, API 32, BYD-AUTO) revealed three actionable insights: (1) `auto_container [android.os.IAutoContainer]` is still present on DL5 — our typed `Phase4Verbs.autoContainerSendInfo` path may work as-is; (2) display naming is the same `shared_fission_bg_XDJAScreenProjection_N` family as DL3; (3) the new gatekeepers `com.byd.appstartmanagement 1.5.0.0` + `com.byd.providers.appstartup 12` + the BYD-rebranded `com.byd.containerservice 12` (replacing `com.xdja.containerservice`) are the suspected behavioural divergence. Build 182 expands the DL5 diagnostic suite to triage all three threads. **(1) D9 reworked — dynamic discovery.** Replaces the 7-package hardcoded whitelist with a shell scan: `pm list packages -f \| grep -iE 'com\.byd\.\|com\.xdja\.\|com\.dilink\.\|cluster\|automotive' \| sort -u`. Parses every line (format `package:/data/app/.../base.apk=<pkg>`), reads `versionName` + `getLongVersionCode()` (API 28+) via PackageManager, and stores the result in a static `sLastDiscovery` list of `DiscoveredPkg` records for downstream tests. Output now shows the full APK path on disk for each match. Status WARN if zero matches, PASS otherwise. **(2) D10 — APK extraction for reverse-engineering.** Iterates `sLastDiscovery`, `cat`s each `apkPath` from `/data/app/...` to `getExternalFilesDir(null)/extracted_apks/<pkg>_v<versionCode>.apk` via the existing `AdbLocalClient` shell relay (uid 2000 has read access to world-readable APK files; `cat` redirect avoids `cp` permission quirks observed on some BYD builds). On success, the per-file `ls -l` is logged plus file size in KB. Final report card shows the target dir path with the exact `adb pull` command to retrieve everything in one shot. SKIPPED if D9 was not run first (empty discovery). **(3) D11 — AutoContainer sendInfo refresh probe.** `service call auto_container 2 i32 1000 i32 0 s16 ""` (the harmless `sendInfo(0)` refresh that's already part of our restoreBydOnCluster sequence). Pattern-matches the output for `SecurityException` / `Permission Denial` / `Result:` / `Parcel`. PASS = service accepted from uid 2000 (confirms our typed Phase 4c path will work on DL5 without modification). FAIL = SecurityException or service-not-found. **(4) D12 — Qt standby cycle probe.** Active 3-step sequence `sendInfo(16)` (Qt standby) → sleep 1.5s → `sendInfo(18)` (close) → sleep 0.5s → `sendInfo(0)` (refresh). Validates the *protocol cycle*, not just the binder reachability. No projection actually started — these three commands together form a complete null cycle. PASS if no SecurityException / no service-not-found anywhere in the 3-step output. **(5) D13 — IATM.setTaskWindowingMode reflection probe.** Resolves `android.app.ActivityTaskManager.getService()` then reflective `setTaskWindowingMode(0, 4, true)` (taskId=0 = bogus on purpose). Three outcomes: `InvocationTargetException(SecurityException)` → WARN (expected on un-signed app, confirms we need signature perms or proxy daemon path); `InvocationTargetException(IllegalArgumentException)` → PASS (method is reachable, only rejected the bogus taskId — best case, means the API is usable once we have a real taskId); `NoSuchMethodException` → FAIL (method removed). Disambiguates the D6 FAIL line. **(6) D14 — Window manager state dump.** `dumpsys window \| grep -E 'Display #\|mDisplayId\|imeLayerStack\|focused\|mStackId\|mCurrentFocus\|mDisplayContent' \| head -80`. Captures the live windowing state without flooding the report. Useful as a baseline snapshot before reproducing a cluster bug. **(7) D15 — `appstartmanagement` package inspection.** `dumpsys package com.byd.appstartmanagement` filtered to permission/receiver/service/activity/authority/provider/signature/version lines, capped at 120. This is the suspected DL5 launch gatekeeper — the inspection will show which intents/categories/actions it claims and which protectionLevel its custom permissions use. **(8) D16 — `containerservice` package inspection.** Same filter applied to `com.byd.containerservice` (the BYD-rebranded XDJA). Will reveal whether the wake/release transaction codes are the same as the old `com.xdja.containerservice` we have in `decompiled/`. **(9) D17 — proxy daemon spawn capability.** Three-step shell probe: `id` (confirm shell uid) → `which app_process64 app_process` (locate the spawn target) → `app_process64 -Xnoimage-dex2oat /system/bin --nice-name=dl5_probe -version` (actually try to spawn it with our daemon's command flags, capturing the first 5 lines of output) → `ls /system/bin/app_process64`. PASS = app_process64 reachable with no permission denial / no `avc:` (SELinux) lines — our Beta proxy daemon strategy is viable on DL5/API 32. FAIL = SELinux denied. WARN = binary not at expected path. **(10) D18 — SELinux context.** `id` + `ps -Z -o LABEL,USER,PID,NAME \| head -10`. Gives the SELinux domain of the shell user and the first 10 processes, needed to diagnose SELinux-related daemon spawn failures. **(11) D19 — Full system services inventory.** Broader than D3: `service list \| grep -iE 'auto\|byd\|cluster\|fission\|display\|window\|projection\|crosscontrol\|xdja\|task' \| head -60`. Captures the full landscape of cluster-relevant binders — useful for spotting BYD-specific services we don't know about yet. **(12) D20 — IActivityTaskManager service presence.** `service list \| grep -iE 'activity_task\|activitytask'`. Confirms the binder exists at the OS level (required precondition for the D13 typed path). **(13) Discovery handoff between tests.** D9 stores its results in `private static final List<DiscoveredPkg> sLastDiscovery` (cleared at the start of each D9 run, so re-running just the suite gives a fresh capture). D10 consumes it and SKIPs if empty. This is the cleanest cross-test data flow without polluting the `TestResult` data class. **(14) Behavioural contract preserved.** No public API changed; existing tests D1–D8 untouched. The `D8Params` holder, the listener contract, and the report format are unchanged. D10's extraction directory is `Context.getExternalFilesDir(null)/extracted_apks/` — falls back to internal `filesDir` if external is unavailable. All new tests use the existing `runShellSync` helper with appropriate timeouts (4–8 s for inspection, 15 s for D10's APK copy loop). **(15) UI / panel.** No change required — the DL5 panel already iterates `DiLink5TestRunner.catalog()` programmatically and inflates one row per `TestDef`. The 11 new entries surface automatically as new rows in the existing scrollable list. **(16) Build.** `versionCode` 181 → 182, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"`. Touched files: `app/build.gradle` (versionCode), `app/src/main/java/com/byd/dashcast/dilink5/DiLink5TestRunner.java` (catalog 9 → 20 entries, switch extended, runD9 rewritten, 11 new run methods + `DiscoveredPkg` holder — +~270 LoC). No new strings, layouts, drawables, dependencies. **(17) Validation plan.** Push to a DL5 testeur, ask them to (a) open Diag → DiLink 5 tab → tap `Run all`, (b) capture the report via the `Copy` button — expected: PASS on D9 with 10–20 packages discovered, PASS on D10 with all APKs in extracted_apks/, PASS or WARN on D11/D12 (the key signal for whether our typed sendInfo path works), useful detail on D13–D20 for triage; (c) `adb pull /sdcard/Android/data/com.byd.dashcast/files/extracted_apks/` from a PC to retrieve all BYD APKs for offline jadx work. |
| **1.1.9-build181** | 181 | **DiagActivity — restore RE Sniffer as a dedicated Material 3 tab.** The Sniffer system (continuous `logcat` + periodic `dumpsys` snapshots into a single `BYD_RE_Sniffer_*.txt` file on the external files dir) was wiped at v0.9.88 (commit `229059c`) when DiagActivity was refactored into the tabbed layout. Only the file-name reference survived in `AppLogger` (storage-cleanup glob). With new Dilink5 testers sending in field logs, we need the Sniffer back to bound user-reported issues — but plugged into the new M3 tab structure rather than the old stacked sections. **(1) New tab `Sniffer` (position 7).** Added `TAB_SNIFFER = 7` constant in `DiagActivity`, new `<TabItem>` in `activity_diag.xml` after `dilink5`, panel toggled in `showPanelForTab` alongside the existing isBeta/isDl5 branches. **(2) New layout `include_diag_sniffer.xml`.** Three stacked M3 cards inside a `NestedScrollView`, identical chrome to `include_diag_beta_engine.xml` (20 dp corner radius, `md_outline_variant` stroke, `md_surface_container_low` background, 20 dp content padding): (a) **Header/status card** — icon + title + subtitle + status pill (`ACTIF` green / `INACTIF` red) + monospace status line + 4 `MaterialButton`s (`Démarrer` filled, `Arrêter` tonal, `Snapshot` outlined, `Exporter` outlined). (b) **"Contenu capturé" info card** — bullet list explaining what lands in the file (header, live logcat threadtime + events, auto snapshots every 10 s of displays/SF/processes, manual snapshot adds `dumpsys window` + broadcasts history, persistence via setsid). (c) **"Procédure type" info card** — 5-step usage flow ending on "Exporter → Telegram". Labels are hardcoded French with `tools:ignore="HardcodedText"` — power-user diagnostic tool, the 12-locale i18n cost was deemed not worth paying for this surface. **(3) Sniffer state machine restored 1:1 from pre-wipe code (`DiagActivity.java` @1160–1500 at commit `229059c^`).** Constants `RE_SNIFFER_TAG = ".re_sniffer_run"`, `RE_SNIFFER_PIDS = ".re_sniffer_pids"`, `RE_SNIFFER_PREFIX = "BYD_RE_Sniffer_"`, prefs key `byd_diag_prefs / re_sniffer_file_path`. Start path: `logcat -c` + create tag file + write header (date, `getprop ro.product.model`, fingerprint, dumpsys display, dumpsys SurfaceFlinger, ps -A), then `setsid sh -c '…'` × 3 (logcat threadtime, snap loop every 10 s, logcat events buffer) with PIDs captured to `.re_sniffer_pids` for clean teardown. The `setsid` is load-bearing: `nohup` alone doesn't survive adbd `SIGHUP` to the process group. Stop path: removes tag file (snap loop exits at next iteration), reads PID file and `kill -9` each PID, `pkill -f BYD_RE_Sniffer_` fallback. Snapshot path: appends `dumpsys display` + `dumpsys window` + `dumpsys SurfaceFlinger` + `ps -A` + `dumpsys activity broadcasts history` with a `=== SNAPSHOT MANUEL HH:MM:SS ===` separator. Export path: `FileProvider.getUriForFile` + `Intent.ACTION_SEND` with `text/plain` + `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION` + `ClipData.newRawUri` for chooser compatibility on API 29. **(4) Persistence across Activity recreation.** `startSniffer` writes the file path to `SharedPreferences(PREF_SNIFFER)` immediately, `stopSniffer` clears it. New `restoreSnifferState()` runs in `onCreate` after `bindSnifferPanel`: reads the saved path, checks the file exists, then runs `[ -f /data/local/tmp/.re_sniffer_run ] && echo ACTIVE \|\| echo STOPPED` via `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` and restores the UI to ACTIF (green pill, Stop/Snapshot/Export enabled) or "Capture précédente arrêtée — exportable" (Export still enabled because the file is on disk). Covers the case where the user rotates / leaves the activity while a capture is running — they come back and find it still going. **(5) Local helper `safeRunOnUiThread`.** The pre-wipe code used a class-wide `safeRunOnUiThread` that checked `mDestroyed` before delivering. The new DiagActivity didn't have it (Beta Engine panel uses runner-side guards). Added a minimal copy at the bottom of `DiagActivity.java`: skips delivery if `mDestroyed`, also rechecks inside the `runOnUiThread` lambda to cover the race window. Used by all four Sniffer callbacks. **(6) Behavioural contract.** No public API change. No new permissions (`adb_local` shell already has everything the Sniffer needs — it was always running uid 2000 since v0.x). The capture is fully opt-in (button-driven) and survives reboot **only as a stale file** on disk: the tag file lives in `/data/local/tmp` and is wiped on reboot, so the `restoreSnifferState` post-reboot check correctly reports "Capture précédente arrêtée" and clears the prefs. **(7) Wire format unchanged.** No daemon work, no new typed verb — Sniffer uses the legacy `AdbLocalClient.executeShell` / `executeShellWithResult` paths exclusively. Could be migrated to `Phase4Verbs.runShell` later for ~50 ms / call saving, but not on a hot path and the shell relay is already plenty fast for a manual user action. **(8) Build.** `versionCode` 180 → 181, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"`. Touched files: `app/build.gradle` (versionCode bump), `app/src/main/res/layout/activity_diag.xml` (1 new TabItem + 1 new `<include>`), `app/src/main/res/layout/include_diag_sniffer.xml` (new, 178 lines), `app/src/main/java/com/byd/dashcast/DiagActivity.java` (TAB_SNIFFER constant + panelSniffer field + 3 surgical edits in bindTabs/showPanelForTab/onCreate + ~270 LoC appended for bindSnifferPanel/setSnifferUiActive/buildSnifferFile/restoreSnifferState/startSniffer/killSnifferProcesses/stopSniffer/snapshotSniffer/exportSniffer/safeRunOnUiThread). No new dependencies, no new drawables (reuses `ic_stethoscope`), no new string resources, no new permissions. **(9) Validation plan.** (a) Open DashCast → Diag → tap `Sniffer` tab → confirm the 3 M3 cards render at 1920×1080 without horizontal scroll and the status pill reads "INACTIF" red. (b) Tap `Démarrer` → toast `Sniffer démarré → BYD_RE_Sniffer_<ts>.txt` → pill flips to "ACTIF" green → status line shows the file name. (c) Reproduce a known bug (ex. project Waze, restore cluster) → tap `Snapshot` → toast `Snapshot ajouté.` → toggle to MainActivity and back to Diag → confirm UI state restored to ACTIF (this validates `restoreSnifferState`). (d) Tap `Arrêter` → pill flips to INACTIF red, status line shows "Arrêté → … Prêt pour export.", Export button stays enabled. (e) Tap `Exporter` → Telegram chooser opens with `BYD_RE_Sniffer_*.txt` attached → forward to dev. (f) Cold-reboot the head unit, reopen DashCast → Diag → Sniffer → status line should read "Capture précédente arrêtée → … exportable." with Export enabled and Start enabled (tag file gone after reboot, file still on disk). |
| **1.1.9-build180** | 180 | **Beta Engine Phase 4d.1 — fix silent `forceStopPackage` no-op + post-kill verification.** Bug surfaced by build-179 field test: when the user stopped a projection (Waze on cluster), the app correctly migrated to display 0 (`moveSessionAppsToMainDisplay` works) but the process was **not** killed despite the device log reporting `beta restoreBydOnCluster typed ok (Xms)`. Root cause: `BetaProxyClient.forceStopPackage(targetPackage, -1)` was passing `userId = -1` (`UserHandle.USER_ALL`), which is silently no-op on the API 29 BYD framework's `IActivityManager.forceStopPackage(String, int)` implementation — the call returned without throwing but did not actually terminate the target process. The shell legacy fallback (`am force-stop pkg` without `--user`) targets the current user (0) implicitly, which is why pre-Phase-4d builds did not exhibit this. **(1) Fix.** Both `AdbLocalClient.restoreBydOnCluster` and `AdbLocalClient.restoreOriginCluster` now pass `userId = 0` (current user) instead of `-1`. The log line now reads `force-stop <pkg> (typed,u=0)` to make the user-id explicit in the report shown by the UI. Two-line change per call site. **(2) Post-kill verification (`verifyForceStop` helper).** After every typed `forceStopPackage` on the teardown hot path, the client now calls `BetaProxyClient.getPidsByPackage(pkg)` (Phase 4b verb, already in production since build 175) and logs `WARN beta force-stop ineffective for <pkg> (pids=<list>)` when surviving PIDs are detected, plus appends `WARN: still alive, pids=…` to the UI report. On success the report shows `verified killed`. Failures inside the verification helper are swallowed (logged at WARN) so the teardown sequence is never broken by a diagnostic. **(3) Why not throw + escalate to shell.** Tempting but counter-productive: the shell fallback uses the same default-user semantics as the new typed userId=0, so if force-stop still doesn't bite the cause is upstream (Android re-launching the app from a sticky service, or BYD framework with a non-standard force-stop hook). Phase 4d.1 prefers visibility over silent retry — the WARN line makes the failure mode obvious in the next device log so we can investigate the real root cause if it recurs. **(4) Out of scope (deferred).** `forceStopApp(Context, String)` (shell-only legacy path, used by the in-list "✕" button) is **unchanged** — that path runs `am force-stop` directly, no userId issue, and includes its own `am task remove` pre-pass for cleaning up Recents. Per-stack `Process.killProcess(pid)` as a third escalation tier was considered and explicitly deferred — depends on whether the new WARN log shows a recurrence at all. **(5) Wire-protocol.** No change. TXN_FORCE_STOP_PACKAGE (=8) and TXN_GET_PIDS (=6) were both shipped in build 178/175 respectively; Phase 4d.1 is a pure client-side correctness fix. | **1.1.9-build175** | 175 | **Beta Engine Phase 4b — typed `pidof` replaces the `sh -c "pidof <pkg>"` fork.** Second production cash-in of the build-173 probe matrix, building directly on the build-174 Phase 4a infrastructure. Validated against the build-174 device log (`byd_log_20260521_231046.log`): Phase 4a measured 8 typed `setOverscan` calls at 11–61 ms (warm) with **zero** shell-fallback log lines, but the same log still showed 7 `beta runShell ok (Xms): pidof com.waze` lines at **48–181 ms** every ~5 s — the next obvious hot path. **(1) `Phase4Verbs.getPidsByPackage(String)`** — new pure-Java verb that opens `/proc`, iterates the numeric PID directories, reads up to 256 bytes of each `/proc/<pid>/cmdline`, splits on the first NUL to extract `argv[0]`, and collects every PID whose argv[0] equals `packageName` or starts with `"packageName:"` (Android sub-processes declared via `android:process=":foo"` in the manifest, exactly what shell `pidof` matches). Returns a space-separated list of PIDs (same wire shape as `pidof` stdout), empty string when nothing matches. Skips non-numeric `/proc` entries cheaply with a manual digit loop instead of `Integer.parseInt`/try-catch, and pre-checks `canRead()` so unreadable kernel threads don't trip `IOException`. Probe P8 (build 173) measured the same scan at **0 ms** on the BYD Seal EU with 241 live processes — replacing a 48–181 ms fork with a ~0–5 ms in-process scan. The choice of `/proc` scan over `IActivityManager.getRunningAppProcesses()` is deliberate: `/proc` is immune to the Android-10 `getRunningAppProcesses` visibility restrictions (which can return an empty list to non-system callers on some OEM builds), it doesn't need any system Context handle, and it precisely reproduces shell `pidof`'s argv[0] semantics. **(2) `ProxyDaemonMain.TXN_GET_PIDS = FIRST_CALL_TRANSACTION + 5` (=6).** Wire format: `String packageName` in → `String spaceSeparatedPids` out (or `writeException` on the unlikely scenario that the scan throws). Defensive try/catch around the scan call: any `Throwable` is unwrapped to a real `Exception` and surfaced via `writeException` so the client falls back to shell with full diagnostic context. Protocol version stays at `"2"` — purely additive transaction. **(3) `BetaProxyClient.getPidsByPackage(String) throws BetaProxyException`** — mirrors the established `runShell` / `setOverscan` Parcel pattern (`writeInterfaceToken` + `writeString` + `transact(TXN_GET_PIDS)` + `readException` + `readString`). Daemon-side exceptions surface as `BetaProxyException` so `ShellGateway` can transparently fall back to the shell path. `RemoteException` nulls the cached binder so the next call triggers a fresh connect. **(4) `ShellGateway` — typed-verb dispatch refactor.** The previous `boolean tryTypedVerb(...)` is now `String tryTypedVerb(...)` returning the stdout payload to hand to the caller's callback on success, or `null` when the command didn't match or the typed call failed. This was needed because `pidof` produces a real payload (the PID list) that the callers consume via `onSuccess(output).trim().isEmpty()` — overscan callers were unaffected because they always received `""` and continue to do so. New pattern `PIDOF = ^\\s*pidof\\s+([A-Za-z0-9._:\\-]+)\\s*$` anchored on both ends and restricted to a single argument so multi-word `pidof a b c` (and any compound invocations) fall through to shell. On match: `BetaProxyClient.getPidsByPackage(pkg)` → log `beta pidof typed ok (Xms): <pkg> → "<pids>"` → callback receives the same string the shell would have produced. **(5) Sites that benefit automatically.** Both `pidof` call sites in `MainActivity` (`reconcileDisplayState` for the cluster app, `reconcileMainDisplayState` for the main-display marker) are unchanged — they already route through `ShellGateway`. They fire every ~5 s while a cluster app or main-display app is alive (driven by `mDisplayPollHandler`). On a build-174 30-minute session, that's ~720 forks/min × 30 min × 2 sites = up to ~720 forks eliminated per session, each ~50–180 ms = **CPU and battery cycles measurable in the dashcam recording timeline**. **(6) Fallback chain still rock-solid.** typed → shell-via-daemon → legacy ADB. If `/proc` scan ever returns an unexpected empty result on a future OEM build that puts apps in cgroups invisible to uid 2000, the typed call succeeds with `""`, callers interpret that as "process dead" (legitimate semantics), and worst case the cluster bookkeeping is cleared one cycle early. If the daemon transaction itself fails (e.g. daemon dead), `BetaProxyException` triggers the existing fall-through to `runShell` → legacy. **(7) Behavioural contract preserved.** No public API removed, no source change in `MainActivity` / `ClusterService` since build 174. With the beta proxy toggle OFF the binary remains byte-for-byte identical to v1.0.1 (gateway short-circuits to `AdbLocalClient` before any pattern matching). **(8) Build.** `versionCode` 174 → 175, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"` per channel policy. No new strings, layouts, drawables, dependencies. **(9) What's left for Phase 4.** Phase 4c (probe P6 confirmed `IAutoContainer` descriptor) — direct typed transact for `AdbLocalClient.sendInfo` to bypass the BYD shell wrapper. Phase 4d (optional, probes P3/P4 to be re-confirmed against shell uid) — typed `forceStopPackage` / `killBackgroundProcesses`. |

Holds a lazy double-checked cache of the `IWindowManager` proxy and the `setOverscan(int,int,int,int,int)` `Method` so subsequent calls skip `Class.forName` / `ServiceManager.getService` / `asInterface` entirely — critical for the resize SeekBar which fires 30+ overscan changes per second while the user drags. `setOverscan(displayId, l, t, r, b) throws Throwable` is the single public verb in this build. **(2) `ProxyDaemonMain` — new `TXN_SET_OVERSCAN` (=5) transaction.** Wire format: `int displayId, int l, int t, int r, int b` in → empty reply (or `writeException` on failure). The onTransact case unwraps `InvocationTargetException` so the client receives the *real* underlying exception (typically a `SecurityException` or `IllegalArgumentException` from the framework) rather than a generic reflection wrapper. Protocol version stays at `"2"` — purely additive. **(3) `BetaProxyClient.setOverscan(int displayId, int l, int t, int r, int b) throws BetaProxyException`** — new public method mirroring the established `runShell(String)` Parcel pattern (`writeInterfaceToken` / 5× `writeInt` / `transact(TXN_SET_OVERSCAN)` / `readException`). Daemon-side exceptions surface as `BetaProxyException` so `ShellGateway` can transparently fall back. **(4) `ShellGateway` — pattern-matching typed-verb interception.** Two pre-compiled regex patterns (`WM_OVERSCAN = ^wm overscan (-?\\d+),(-?\\d+),(-?\\d+),(-?\\d+) -d (\\d+)$` and `WM_OVERSCAN_RESET = ^wm overscan reset -d (\\d+)$`) test the command before any shell call. On match: `BetaProxyClient.setOverscan(...)` is invoked; on success the original callback receives `onSuccess("")` (overscan commands produce no useful stdout) and we log `beta setOverscan typed ok (Xms): d=N L,T,R,B`. On regex miss OR typed-call exception, the code falls through to the existing `runShell` path (which itself falls back to legacy `AdbLocalClient` on its own failure). The fallback chain is now **typed → shell-via-daemon → legacy ADB** instead of the previous **shell-via-daemon → legacy ADB**, so a freshly-installed-but-OEM-modified device that rejects the typed call still ends up doing exactly what build 172 did. Anchored regex (`^…$`) on both ends so any compound or unusual command (e.g. `wm overscan … && wm size …`) cleanly falls through. **(5) Sites that benefit automatically.** All 7 production call sites migrated in build 172 already route through `ShellGateway`, so the speedup is transparent: `MainActivity` resize SeekBar (~30 calls/s while dragging), `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` (500 ms post-launch per-app overscan), `ClusterService.startActivityViaIAM` (launch-time overscan), `ClusterService.stopProjectionNoAdb` (`wm overscan reset -d 1`), `ClusterService` post-projection apply (`INSET_H,INSET_V,INSET_H,INSET_V`). The two `pidof` sites in `reconcileDisplayState` / `reconcileMainDisplayState` are **not** affected by Phase 4a (no `wm overscan` match) — they keep going through `runShell` and will be migrated in Phase 4b via `IActivityManager.getRunningAppProcesses`/`getTasks` (probe P5 PASS in build 173). **(6) Behavioural contract preserved.** No public API removed. `MainActivity` / `ClusterService` source unchanged from build 173. With the beta proxy toggle OFF the binary remains byte-for-byte identical to v1.0.1. Order of overscan calls is still preserved (the single-threaded `shell-gateway` executor stays as the serialisation point). **(7) Expected device behaviour.** The build-172 log showed `ClusterService L595` at 1687 ms (cold), `L525` at 275 ms, `MainActivity autoApplyInsets` at 84 ms, resize SeekBar at 197 ms. With Phase 4a we expect those to drop to single-digit ms after the first call warms `Phase4Verbs`' reflection cache (probe P1 measured 117 ms on the very first call due to the same cache warm-up, then 0 ms on subsequent runs). The SeekBar drag in particular should feel instantaneous. **(8) Build.** `versionCode` 173 → 174, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"` per channel policy. No new strings, layouts, drawables, dependencies. |
| **1.1.9-build173** | 173 | **Beta Engine — Phase 4 feasibility probe suite (P1–P12).** Diagnostic-only build whose sole purpose is to answer one question before we commit to the Phase 4 rewrite: *"which native AOSP APIs can the proxy daemon actually call from uid 2000 / SELinux domain `shell` on the BYD Seal EU?"* No production code path changes — `ShellGateway`, `MainActivity`, `ClusterService` behaviour is identical to build 172. **(1) New class `com.byd.dashcast.beta.proxy.Phase4Probes`** (~330 LoC). Runs **inside the daemon process** and tries every typed Binder verb that Phase 4 would replace a `sh -c "wm …"` / `am …` fork with. Twelve probes total: **P1** `IWindowManager.setOverscan(0,0,0,0,0)` — the headline verb behind every `wm overscan` shell call in the resize SeekBar and `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded`; **P2** `IWindowManager.getInitialDisplaySize(0, Point)` — read-only counterpart, useful to bypass `wm size`/`dumpsys display`; **P3** `IActivityManager.forceStopPackage("com.example.does.not.exist", -1)` — replaces `am force-stop`; **P4** `IActivityManager.killBackgroundProcesses("com.example.does.not.exist", -1)` — replaces `am kill`; **P5** `IActivityManager.getTasks(20)` (with `getRunningTasks(int)` fallback) — replaces the `dumpsys activity recents` + regex pipeline used by `ClusterService.findRunningTaskId`; **P6** raw `INTERFACE_TRANSACTION` against the `AutoContainer` system service — the missing piece to migrate `AdbLocalClient.sendInfo` away from the BYD shell wrapper; **P7** `SystemProperties.get(ro.build.version.release)` + `SystemProperties.set(debug.dashcast.probe, "1")` — read should PASS, write almost certainly fails (sepolicy), detail string carries the exact exception class; **P8** pure-Java scan of `/proc/*/cmdline` for `com.byd.dashcast` — sandbox baseline, should always PASS; if it doesn't, the daemon is sandboxed harder than expected and the entire Phase 4 plan is doomed regardless of any Binder permission; **P9** `ServiceManager.listServices()` enumeration with explicit visibility check for `window` / `activity` / `package` / `input` / `display` / `AutoContainer` / `media_session` / `audio`; **P10** `IPackageManager.getInstallerPackageName("com.byd.dashcast")` — typed PM read; **P11** `IInputManager.Stub.asInterface` + walk of `IInputManager.getDeclaredMethods()` to confirm `injectInputEvent` is reachable (already known to work via the input forwarder — sanity check); **P12** `DisplayManager.getDisplays()` via the daemon's system Context — confirms the framework managers attached to the system Context (already proven by `BetaSystemContext`) are usable from inside the daemon process too. Each probe is wrapped in a single try/catch that **unwraps `InvocationTargetException`** and categorises the failure into one of four buckets: `PASS`, `FAIL_SECURITY` (SecurityException → Phase 4 verb NOT viable from uid 2000), `FAIL_API` (`NoSuchMethodException` / `ClassNotFoundException` → API renamed on this OEM, need device-specific reflection), `FAIL_OTHER` (any other Throwable, detail carries `ClassName message`). Results are concatenated as `P1=PASS:detail\|P2=FAIL_SECURITY:detail\|…` with `\|` and newlines stripped from detail strings to keep the parser trivial. The harness itself never crashes the daemon — any exception escaping `runAll` is caught in the binder layer and returned as `P0=FAIL_OTHER:harness …`. **(2) `ProxyDaemonMain` — new `TXN_PROBE_PHASE4` (=4) transaction.** Wire format: no args in → `String` out. The system Context (already acquired by `acquireSystemContext()` for the connect broadcast) is now also stored in `private static volatile Context sSystemContext` so `ProxyBinder.onTransact` can pass it to `Phase4Probes.runAll(...)` without redoing the reflection. Protocol version stays at `"2"` because the new transaction is purely additive — older clients keep working unchanged. **(3) `BetaProxyClient.runPhase4Probes()` — new public client method**, mirrors the `runShell(String)` pattern (`writeInterfaceToken` / `transact(TXN_PROBE_PHASE4)` / `readException` / `readString`) and rethrows as `BetaProxyException` on transact failure. **(4) `BetaTestRunner` — Family `P` and 12 new tests P1–P12.** `Family` enum extended with `P` (next to `A`, `B`, `X`). Catalog grows from 15 to 27 entries with the 12 probe rows appended after `X3`. `runOne()` switch grew a single shared case-block that dispatches every P-id to a new `testProbe(ctx, r)` helper. To avoid running the probe suite 12 times (once per row in the diag UI), the first P-test populates two static fields: `sProbeCache` (parsed `Map<String,String>` of probe-id → `"STATUS:detail"`) and `sProbeError` (set if the daemon connect or the transact itself failed). Subsequent P-tests hit the cache. The status mapping is strict: only `STATUS == "PASS"` reports green; everything else (including `FAIL_SECURITY`, `FAIL_API`, `FAIL_NULL`, `FAIL_OTHER`) is rendered as a red FAIL with the category prepended to the message so the row title in `DiagActivity` reads e.g. `FAIL_SECURITY: SecurityException Neither user 2000 nor current process has android.permission.WRITE_SECURE_SETTINGS`. The cache is reset at the start of each `runAll()` invocation so re-running the suite re-queries the daemon. **(5) Behavioural impact.** Production users see nothing — neither `ShellGateway` nor the migrated `MainActivity` / `ClusterService` call sites changed at all, and the new transaction is only triggered if the user opens **Diag → Beta Engine** and runs the suite. With the beta toggle OFF the entire P family is `FAIL` with message `daemon probe run failed: daemon not connected`, which is the expected legacy-only behaviour. **(6) What we learn from a single device run.** Each `PASS` row maps 1:1 to a Phase 4 verb that is safe to ship; each `FAIL_SECURITY` row tells us exactly which verb has to stay on the shell path (or be wrapped behind a different mechanism, e.g. a system signature shim); the `FAIL_API` taxonomy isolates OEM divergences from outright denials. The expected steady-state outcome is roughly: P8 always PASS, P2/P5/P9/P10/P11/P12 PASS, P1/P3/P4 PASS (most likely on shell uid for AOSP — to be confirmed), P6 PASS for INTERFACE_TRANSACTION only (the actual sendInfo verb still has to be triaged separately), P7 read=PASS write=FAIL. **(7) Build.** `versionCode` 172 → 173, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"` per channel policy. No new strings, layouts or drawables. No public API removed — `Phase4Probes.runAll(Context)` and `BetaProxyClient.runPhase4Probes()` are purely additive. |
| **1.1.9-build172** | 172 | **Beta Engine Phase 3 — production call-site migration to the proxy daemon.** Build 171 made the proxy daemon production-quality (12 ✓ / 0 ✗ / 3 ⊘ on device, X1 measured at 0 ms beta vs 68 ms legacy — a 60× shell-call latency win). This build cashes that win in: the seven hot-path callers of `AdbLocalClient.executeShell` / `executeShellWithResult` in `MainActivity` and `ClusterService` now route through the proxy when `beta_proxy_enabled` is ON, while staying byte-for-byte identical to v1.0.1 when the toggle is OFF. **(1) New class `com.byd.dashcast.beta.ShellGateway`** (~80 LoC). Drop-in wrapper with the same signatures as the legacy methods (`execShell(ctx, cmd)` and `execShellWithResult(ctx, cmd, AdbLocalClient.Callback)`). Owns its own dedicated single-threaded executor (`shell-gateway`, daemon thread) so call order is preserved within a process. Fast path: if `BetaConfig.isProxyDaemonEnabled(ctx)` returns `false`, delegates synchronously to `AdbLocalClient` with zero overhead. Beta path: lazily connects to the daemon via `BetaProxyClient.connect(ctx)` if needed, then calls `BetaProxyClient.runShell(cmd)` and logs `beta runShell ok (Xms): <cmd>`. On any `Throwable` (RemoteException, daemon dead, timeout, etc.) it logs a warning and transparently falls back to `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult(ctx, cmd, cb)` — the original callback is honoured by the fallback path so consumers never see the swap. **(2) `MainActivity` (4 sites migrated).** `AdbLocalClient.executeShell` → `ShellGateway.execShell` in the resize-SeekBar listener (`wm overscan w,h,w,h -d 1`) and in `autoApplyInsetsIfNeeded` (per-app saved overscan, fires 500 ms after launch). `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` → `ShellGateway.execShellWithResult` in `reconcileDisplayState` (`pidof <clusterPkg>` polling, runs every ~5 s while a cluster app is alive) and `reconcileMainDisplayState` (`pidof <mainPkg>` for the main-display marker). These four are the ones that fire most often in normal usage. **(3) `ClusterService` (3 sites migrated).** All three `wm overscan` system calls — the launch-time per-app overscan in `startActivityViaIAM` setup, the `stopProjectionNoAdb` reset (`wm overscan reset -d 1`), and the post-projection-active overscan apply with `INSET_H,INSET_V,INSET_H,INSET_V` on the cluster display. **(4) Sites intentionally NOT migrated.** `SysInfoActivity`, `SettingsActivity` (`Test ADB` button), `DiLink5TestRunner` and `BetaTestRunner` keep calling `AdbLocalClient.executeShellWithResult` directly because they are diagnostic / self-test surfaces that must exercise the legacy path on purpose (otherwise the test runners would compare beta against beta). `BetaProxyClient` itself keeps using `AdbLocalClient` for its own bootstrap shell (`setsid sh -c …`) and for `readDaemonLogTail` — the proxy obviously cannot route itself. **(5) Build.** `versionCode` 171 → 172, `versionName` pinned at `"1.1.9"` per the channel policy (`1.2.0` reserved for DiLink 5). **Expected device behaviour with the toggle ON**: `wm overscan` and `pidof` calls drop from ~70 ms each to ~0 ms (single-digit ms) as measured by X1 in build 171 — most visible on the resize SeekBar (no more visible lag when dragging), on the per-app overscan auto-apply 500 ms after launch (felt as instantaneous), and on the pidof-based reconciliation timer (lower CPU per poll). **With the toggle OFF** the binary is functionally identical to v1.1.9 build 171 — every migrated site short-circuits to the same `AdbLocalClient` method it was calling before, no executor overhead, no proxy connection attempt. **Regression / fallback safety.** Any failure on the beta path (daemon down, transact failure, exception in `runShell`, etc.) is caught and logged as `ShellGateway: beta runShell failed after Xms, fallback legacy: <msg> [cmd=<cmd>]`, then the same callback is invoked through the legacy path with the same arguments. The user never sees a broken cluster operation because of a proxy hiccup. The dedicated `shell-gateway` executor uses a single thread so beta-then-legacy fallback for the same call cannot interleave with other in-flight commands. **Public API surface preserved.** `BetaProxyClient` and `AdbLocalClient` signatures unchanged. `ShellGateway` is purely additive. No layout / strings / drawable changes in this build. |
| **1.1.0-beta1** | 156 | **Beta Engine scaffolding + Diagnostics rebuild + file-based log sharing (full i18n).** First pre-release on the new `beta/1.1.0` channel. Introduces an opt-in "Beta Engine" pipeline that is wired everywhere needed but **stays inert at runtime by default** so the v1.0.1 stable code path remains the source of truth. **(1) New `com.byd.dashcast.beta` package** (5 classes, ~654 LoC). `BetaConfig` (67 LoC) — SharedPreferences-backed (`byd_app_prefs`, same store as `SettingsActivity`) feature flags: `beta_engine_enabled` and `beta_proxy_enabled` (both default `false`). Granularity = option B (two independent toggles). Activation semantics = next launch (toggles are read once at process start by `BetaSystemContext.refresh()`, and a restart-required dialog is shown when the user flips them). `BetaSystemContext` (119 LoC) — process-wide singleton snapshot of the beta state, exposes `isEngineEnabled()` / `isProxyEnabled()` to the rest of the app without re-reading prefs in hot paths. `BetaProxyClient` (94 LoC) — stub client that will eventually route ADB calls through an alternate transport; currently returns `NOT_IMPLEMENTED` for every probe. `BetaEngineGateway` (65 LoC) — single entry point used by future call-sites, exposes a `safeCall(legacy, beta)` helper that **auto-falls back to the legacy implementation on any exception** so the user can keep using the app even if the beta path fails. `BetaTestRunner` (309 LoC) — 15-test self-check matrix (B1–B6 real beta-engine probes, A1–A6 currently `SKIPPED` placeholders for the ADB-bridge work, X1–X3 mixed cross-checks), each test returns a `Result` with status (`PASS` / `FAIL` / `SKIPPED` / `WARN`), latency in ms and a short message. **(2) `SettingsActivity` (+36 LoC).** New "Beta (expérimental)" card containing two `MaterialSwitch` rows (`sw_beta_engine`, `sw_beta_proxy`), an amber warning banner (`bg_beta_warning` drawable, `bg_beta_pill` for the "BETA" pill) explicitly stating the beta nature, and a restart-required `AlertDialog` triggered whenever either switch is toggled. The card is **always visible** (no hidden gesture, no developer mode required) because the v1.1.0 line is a beta channel and the user opted-in by installing the beta APK. Toggle changes persist through `BetaConfig.set*()` but have **no runtime ADB effect yet** — flipping them off restores the exact v1.0.1 behaviour, flipping them on currently only enables the diagnostics readouts and the auto-fallback path in `BetaEngineGateway`. **(3) `DiagActivity` rebuilt as a 6-tab `TabLayout`** (~257 LoC). Tabs in order: **Beta Engine** (default selected, runs the 15-test `BetaTestRunner` and renders a colour-coded card list via `item_beta_test.xml` + `include_diag_beta_engine.xml`, with a "Lancer tous" / "Run all" button and a "Envoyer (.log)" / "Send (.log)" button), Système, Cluster, ADB, Logs, Apps. The five legacy tabs share a single `include_diag_coming_soon.xml` placeholder that prints "Bientôt disponible" — the existing 1.0.1 diag screens are kept reachable via the overflow menu and will be migrated tab by tab in subsequent beta drops. **(4) `AppLogger` refactor (file-based sharing everywhere, +file API).** Added `writeFile(ctx, prefix, content)` — generic timestamped `.log` writer under `getExternalFilesDir("logs")` with a `files-path` fallback; added `shareFile(ctx, File, subject, chooserTitle)` — generic `Intent.ACTION_SEND` with `EXTRA_STREAM` via the existing FileProvider authority (`${packageName}.fileprovider`, `xml/file_paths.xml` covers both `external-files-path` and `files-path`) and `FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION`. The existing `share(ctx)` is now file-first (attaches a `byd_log_<timestamp>.log`) with a plain-text fallback only when the file write fails. New `shareWithReport(ctx, reportText)` writes a combined `byd_report_<ts>.log` (header line + diagnostic report + full log buffer) and attaches it via `shareFile`. Net effect: **every "Send logs" / "Send report" action in the app now ships a real `.log` file** instead of a raw text body, which fixes the previous 64 KB-ish silent truncation seen on Android share-sheets and lets the user attach the file to GitHub issues / e-mail / WhatsApp without copy-paste loss. **(5) New layouts and drawables.** `layout/include_diag_beta_engine.xml`, `layout/include_diag_coming_soon.xml`, `layout/item_beta_test.xml`, `drawable/bg_beta_pill.xml`, `drawable/bg_beta_warning.xml`, and edits to `layout/activity_diag.xml` (TabLayout root) and `layout/activity_settings.xml` (Beta card insertion). **(6) Strings — full i18n.** Added a block of 28 new keys ending with `diag_beta_run_all` / `diag_beta_copy_report` / `diag_beta_report_copied` to `values/strings.xml` (FR default) and `values-en/strings.xml`. Translated all 28 keys to the 10 remaining locales (`ar`, `be`, `de`, `es`, `it`, `kk`, `ru`, `tr`, `uk`, `uz`) via `scripts/inject_beta_strings.py` (idempotent — re-runs are no-ops thanks to a `diag_beta_run_all` marker check). 12 locales total, parity with the existing UI. **(7) Build.** `versionCode` 155 → 156, `versionName` `"1.0.1"` → `"1.1.0-beta1"`. No business logic from v1.0.1 is modified — cluster projection, overscan, OTA, restore-origin-cluster, app shortcuts, category filters, reconnect popup and visual mode all behave exactly as in v1.0.1 with both beta switches OFF. **Known limitations of this drop**: A1–A6 tests show `SKIPPED`; the proxy ADB transport is a stub; the legacy 5 diag tabs render a placeholder. These are intentional and will be filled in subsequent `1.1.0-betaN` drops on the same channel. |
| **0.9.61-alpha** | 127 | **Settings icons — drop the style attribute entirely.** Even with `android:layout_width="28dp"`/`layout_height="28dp"` inlined on the View tag in 0.9.6, the leading icons still rendered as tiny white dots on the BYD device. Compared to the `+`/`−` slider icons (which render correctly), the only remaining difference was that the leading `<ImageView>` still had `style="@style/Widget.DashCast.ListItem.Leading"` applied. Removed the `style="…"` attribute from the 7 leading `<ImageView>` instances in `activity_settings.xml` and inlined every needed attribute directly on the View tag (`layout_width=28dp`, `layout_height=28dp`, `layout_marginEnd=16dp`, `layout_gravity=center_vertical`, `scaleType=fitCenter`, `tint=@color/md_on_surface_variant`, plus the existing `src`). No other change. |
| **0.9.6-alpha** | 126 | **Settings icons — the actual root cause.** Reported on the car after 0.9.5: the icons in `Comportement` and `À propos` were rendering as tiny white dots, regardless of the `android:tint` value. Root cause finally found: on this BYD Android 10 runtime, `android:layout_width` and `android:layout_height` declared inside a `<style>` (here `Widget.DashCast.ListItem.Leading` with `28dp × 28dp`) are not honored by `LayoutInflater` because layout params are extracted from the View tag itself, not from the applied style. The ImageView therefore fell back to a near-zero size, so the vector was rendered as a 1–2 px white dot. Fix: inlined `android:layout_width="28dp"`, `android:layout_height="28dp"`, `android:layout_marginEnd="16dp"` on every leading `<ImageView>` in `activity_settings.xml` (7 occurrences). The style still applies `layout_gravity`, `scaleType` and other non-layout attributes. No other change. |
| **0.9.5-alpha** | 125 | **Settings icons — third and final fix attempt.** In 0.9.3 the tint was on the vector drawable (didn't work). In 0.9.4 it was inlined as `android:tint="?attr/colorOnSurfaceVariant"` on each `<ImageView>` (still didn't work on the BYD device — the theme attribute reference returned a transparent value at runtime, so every leading icon in Comportement, À propos and the +/- slider icons rendered as empty space). Final fix: replaced every `android:tint="?attr/colorOnSurfaceVariant"` with `android:tint="@color/md_on_surface_variant"` (8 occurrences in `activity_settings.xml`) and `android:tint="?attr/colorOnPrimaryContainer"` with `android:tint="@color/md_on_primary_container"` on the Welcome hero `ic_cast`. The color resource is defined in both `values/colors.xml` (`#43474E`) and `values-night/colors.xml` (`#C3C6CF`), so DayNight is still respected through resource-qualifier resolution instead of theme-attribute indirection — which is what actually works on this Android 10 BYD runtime. No other change. |

The hero `ic_cast` in `activity_welcome.xml` got an inline `android:tint="?attr/colorOnPrimaryContainer"`. **Settings — `Vérifier` button now triggers the real OTA flow**: in 0.9.3 the `btn_check_update` opened the GitHub Releases page in a browser, which did not match the 3-dot-menu OTA. Both existing internal callsites (`MainActivity.onCreate` auto-check at launch and `showOverflowMenu` case 6) were updated to pass the activity. `SettingsActivity.btn_check_update` now calls `UpdateChecker.checkUpdate(this, MainActivity.makeOtaProgressListener(this, true))` — same `AlertDialog` with `ProgressBar` for download, same `startDownload` / `onInstalling` / `onUpToDate` / `onError` handlers as the 3-dot menu. `row_source_code` still opens `https://github.com/Kiroha/byd-dashcast` via `openUrl()`. The supporting text below `Rechercher une mise à jour` was rewritten from `Ouvre la page des releases sur GitHub` to `Vérifie si une nouvelle version est disponible`. **Welcome card — full-width + Continue button**: the card was capped at 800dp in 0.9.3 and still felt narrow; it now uses `match_parent` with 60dp horizontal padding so it spans the full screen, the hero square shrinks from 96dp to 80dp, the title from 44sp to 36sp, the language-select prompt from 16sp to 15sp, vertical paddings/margins are tightened so the four rows of three language buttons fit without scrolling on the 12.3" cluster.
